===== FILE: src/entities/course/service.ts =====
import { getItems, getItem, createRec, updateRec, deleteRec, asId, contentToStr } from '@/shared/api/directus'
import type { Course, CourseAccent, CourseStatus, Lesson, LessonType, Section } from './types'

interface CourseRec { id: unknown; title: string; subtitle?: string; category?: string; icon?: string; instructor?: string; owner?: unknown; institution?: unknown; status?: CourseStatus; total_hours?: number; accent?: CourseAccent }
interface SectionRec { id: unknown; course?: unknown; title: string; order?: number }
interface LessonRec { id: unknown; course?: unknown; section?: unknown; title: string; type?: LessonType; duration_hours?: number; xp?: number; order?: number; content?: unknown; open?: boolean; submission?: boolean }

function toCourse(c: CourseRec, sections: SectionRec[], lessons: LessonRec[]): Course {
  const cid = String(c.id)
  const secs: Section[] = sections
    .filter((s) => String(s.course) === cid)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0))
    .map((s) => ({
      id: String(s.id), title: s.title,
      lessons: lessons.filter((l) => String(l.section) === String(s.id)).sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0))
        .map((l): Lesson => ({ id: String(l.id), title: l.title, type: (l.type ?? 'lecture') as LessonType, durationHours: l.duration_hours ?? 0, xp: l.xp ?? 0, content: contentToStr(l.content), open: !!l.open, submission: !!l.submission })),
    }))
  return { id: cid, title: c.title, subtitle: c.subtitle ?? '', category: c.category ?? '', icon: c.icon || 'code', instructor: c.instructor ?? '', ownerId: String(c.owner ?? ''), institutionShort: '', status: c.status ?? 'draft', totalHours: c.total_hours ?? 0, accent: c.accent || 'aqua', sections: secs }
}

export async function fetchCourses(): Promise<Course[]> {
  const [courses, sections, lessons] = await Promise.all([
    getItems<CourseRec>('courses', { fields: ['*'] }),
    getItems<SectionRec>('sections', { fields: ['*'] }),
    getItems<LessonRec>('lessons', { fields: ['*'] }),
  ])
  return courses.map((c) => toCourse(c, sections, lessons))
}
export async function fetchCourse(id: string): Promise<Course | null> {
  try {
    const c = await getItem<CourseRec>('courses', id)
    const [sections, lessons] = await Promise.all([
      getItems<SectionRec>('sections', { filter: { course: { _eq: asId(id) } }, fields: ['*'] }),
      getItems<LessonRec>('lessons', { filter: { course: { _eq: asId(id) } }, fields: ['*'] }),
    ])
    return toCourse(c, sections, lessons)
  } catch { return null }
}

export async function createCourse(ownerId: string, instructor: string, institutionId: string): Promise<string> {
  const rec = await createRec<CourseRec>('courses', { title: 'Новый курс', subtitle: 'Краткое описание', category: 'Разработка', icon: 'code', accent: 'aqua', instructor, owner: asId(ownerId), institution: asId(institutionId), status: 'draft', total_hours: 0 })
  return String(rec.id)
}
export async function updateCourse(id: string, patch: Partial<Course>): Promise<void> {
  const b: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  if (patch.title !== undefined) b.title = patch.title
  if (patch.subtitle !== undefined) b.subtitle = patch.subtitle
  if (patch.category !== undefined) b.category = patch.category
  if (patch.accent !== undefined) b.accent = patch.accent
  if (patch.instructor !== undefined) b.instructor = patch.instructor
  if (patch.status !== undefined) b.status = patch.status
  if (patch.totalHours !== undefined) b.total_hours = patch.totalHours
  await updateRec('courses', id, b)
}
export const deleteCourse = (id: string) => deleteRec('courses', id)
export const addSection = (courseId: string, order: number) => createRec('sections', { course: asId(courseId), title: 'Новый раздел', order }).then(() => {})
export const updateSection = (id: string, title: string) => updateRec('sections', id, { title }).then(() => {})
export const deleteSection = (id: string) => deleteRec('sections', id)
export const addLesson = (courseId: string, sectionId: string, order: number) =>
  createRec('lessons', { course: asId(courseId), section: asId(sectionId), title: 'Новый урок', type: 'lecture', duration_hours: 2, xp: 10, order, open: false, submission: false }).then(() => {})
export async function updateLesson(id: string, patch: Partial<Lesson>): Promise<void> {
  const b: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  if (patch.title !== undefined) b.title = patch.title
  if (patch.type !== undefined) b.type = patch.type
  if (patch.durationHours !== undefined) b.duration_hours = patch.durationHours
  if (patch.xp !== undefined) b.xp = patch.xp
  if (patch.content !== undefined) b.content = patch.content
  if (patch.open !== undefined) b.open = patch.open
  if (patch.submission !== undefined) b.submission = patch.submission
  await updateRec('lessons', id, b)
}
export const deleteLesson = (id: string) => deleteRec('lessons', id)

===== FILE: src/entities/enrollment/service.ts =====
import { getItems, getFirst, createRec, deleteRec, asId } from '@/shared/api/directus'
export interface Enrollment { id: string; studentId: string; courseId: string; progressPercent: number; studentName: string; studentCallsign: string }
interface Rec { id: unknown; student?: unknown; course?: unknown; progress_percent?: number }
const rid = (v: unknown) => String(typeof v === 'object' && v ? (v as { id?: unknown }).id : v ?? '')
function map(r: Rec): Enrollment {
  const s = typeof r.student === 'object' && r.student ? (r.student as { full_name?: string; callsign?: string; student_id?: string }) : undefined
  return { id: String(r.id), studentId: rid(r.student), courseId: rid(r.course), progressPercent: r.progress_percent ?? 0, studentName: s?.full_name ?? '', studentCallsign: s?.callsign ?? s?.student_id ?? '' }
}
const STUDENT_FIELDS = ['*', 'student.id', 'student.full_name', 'student.callsign', 'student.student_id']
export async function fetchMyEnrollment(courseId: string, studentId: string): Promise<Enrollment | null> {
  const rec = await getFirst<Rec>('enrollments', { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, course: { _eq: asId(courseId) } }, STUDENT_FIELDS)
  return rec ? map(rec) : null
}
export const enroll = (courseId: string, studentId: string) => createRec('enrollments', { student: asId(studentId), course: asId(courseId), enrolled_at: new Date().toISOString(), progress_percent: 0 }).then(() => {})
export async function fetchMyEnrollments(studentId: string): Promise<Enrollment[]> {
  return (await getItems<Rec>('enrollments', { filter: { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) } }, fields: STUDENT_FIELDS })).map(map)
}
export const unenroll = (id: string) => deleteRec('enrollments', id)
export async function fetchCourseEnrollments(courseId: string): Promise<Enrollment[]> {
  return (await getItems<Rec>('enrollments', { filter: { course: { _eq: asId(courseId) } }, fields: STUDENT_FIELDS })).map(map)
}

import { getItems, asId, relStr, relId, relNum } from '@/shared/api/directus'
import { groupCode } from '@/shared/lib/group'
export interface LeaderRow { id: string; userId: string; name: string; xp: number; level: number; rank: string; groupCode: string }
interface Rec { id: unknown; user?: unknown; name?: string; xp?: number; level?: number; rank?: string; institution?: unknown; group?: unknown }
export async function fetchLeaderboard(institutionId?: string, limit = 100): Promise<LeaderRow[]> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('leaderboard', { sort: '-xp', limit, fields: ['*', 'group.id', 'group.specialty', 'group.start_year', 'group.index'], ...(institutionId ? { filter: { institution: { _eq: asId(institutionId) } } } : {}) })
  return list.map((r) => ({ id: String(r.id), userId: relId(r.user), name: r.name ?? 'USER', xp: r.xp ?? 0, level: r.level ?? 1, rank: r.rank ?? 'F', groupCode: relStr(r.group, 'specialty') ? groupCode(relStr(r.group, 'specialty'), relNum(r.group, 'start_year'), relNum(r.group, 'index', 1)) : '' }))
}

===== FILE: src/entities/lesson/content.ts =====
import type { LessonType } from '@/entities/course/types'

export type CalloutTone = 'info' | 'tip' | 'warn'

export type LessonBlock =
  | { id: string; type: 'heading'; text: string }
  | { id: string; type: 'text'; text: string }
  | { id: string; type: 'image'; url: string; caption?: string }
  | { id: string; type: 'callout'; tone: CalloutTone; text: string }
  | { id: string; type: 'divider' }
  | { id: string; type: 'trainer'; exerciseId: string }

export type BlockType = LessonBlock['type']

export function uid(): string {
  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)
}

export function newBlock(type: BlockType): LessonBlock {
  switch (type) {
    case 'heading':
      return { id: uid(), type, text: 'Заголовок' }
    case 'text':
      return { id: uid(), type, text: 'Текст урока…' }
    case 'image':
      return { id: uid(), type, url: '', caption: '' }
    case 'callout':
      return { id: uid(), type, tone: 'info', text: 'Важная заметка' }
    case 'divider':
      return { id: uid(), type }
    case 'trainer':
      return { id: uid(), type, exerciseId: 'free' }
  }
}

export function parseContent(raw: string | undefined): LessonBlock[] {
  if (!raw) return []
  try {
    const v = JSON.parse(raw)
    return Array.isArray(v) ? (v as LessonBlock[]) : []
  } catch {
    return []
  }
}

export function serializeContent(blocks: LessonBlock[]): string {
  return JSON.stringify(blocks)
}

/** Стартовый шаблон блоков по типу урока. */
export function templateFor(type: LessonType): LessonBlock[] {
  switch (type) {
    case 'lecture':
      return [
        { id: uid(), type: 'heading', text: 'Введение' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'text', text: 'Опишите тему лекции и ключевые идеи…' },
      ]
    case 'video':
      return [
        { id: uid(), type: 'heading', text: 'Видео-занятие' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'callout', tone: 'info', text: 'Материалы и конспект к видео' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'text', text: 'Краткое содержание…' },
      ]
    case 'practice':
      return [
        { id: uid(), type: 'heading', text: 'Практика' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'text', text: 'Что нужно сделать в тренажёре…' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'trainer', exerciseId: 'free' },
      ]
    case 'quiz':
      return [
        { id: uid(), type: 'heading', text: 'Проверка знаний' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'callout', tone: 'tip', text: 'Ответьте на вопросы по теме' },
      ]
    case 'assignment':
      return [
        { id: uid(), type: 'heading', text: 'Задание' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'callout', tone: 'tip', text: 'Условие задания: соберите макет по требованиям.' },
        { id: uid(), type: 'trainer', exerciseId: 'landing' },
      ]
  }
}

import { getItems, updateRec, asId } from '@/shared/api/directus'
export type NotifKind = 'grade' | 'board' | 'system'
export interface Notif { id: string; kind: NotifKind; title: string; text: string; link: string; read: boolean; created: string }
interface Rec { id: unknown; kind?: NotifKind; title: string; text?: string; link?: string; read?: boolean; created?: string }
const map = (r: Rec): Notif => ({ id: String(r.id), kind: r.kind ?? 'system', title: r.title, text: r.text ?? '', link: r.link ?? '', read: !!r.read, created: r.created ?? '' })
export async function fetchMyNotifications(userId: string): Promise<Notif[]> {

}

export async function markAllNotifsRead(userId: string): Promise<void> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('notifications', { filter: { user: { _eq: asId(userId) }, read: { _eq: false } }, fields: ['id'] })
  for (const r of list) await updateRec('notifications', String(r.id), { read: true })
}

export type Rank = 'F' | 'E' | 'D' | 'C' | 'B' | 'A' | 'S' | 'SS' | 'SSS' | 'SSS+'

/** Ранги прокачки студента (F→SSS). SSS+ — только у супер-админа. */
export const RANKS: Rank[] = ['F', 'E', 'D', 'C', 'B', 'A', 'S', 'SS', 'SSS']

export type FrameKind = 'none' | 'ticks' | 'hex' | 'double' | 'crown'

export interface RankStyle {
  /** Палитра геометрии аватара. */
  palette: string[]
  /** Кол-во полигонов глифа. */
  shapes: number
  /** Кол-во концентрических колец. */
  rings: number
  /** Сила свечения 0..1. */
  glow: number
  /** Цвет ореола/свечения. */
  halo: string
  frame: FrameKind
  /** Вращение внешнего кольца. */
  spin: boolean
  /** Пульсация ядра. */
  pulse: boolean
  /** Кол-во орбитальных частиц. */
  particles: number
  /** Подпись-девиз ранга. */
  motto: string
}

===== FILE: src/entities/profile/stats.ts =====
import { getItems, asId } from '@/shared/api/directus'
export interface TeacherStats { courses: number; students: number; hours: number }
interface CourseRec { id: unknown; total_hours?: number }
interface EnrollRec { student?: unknown }
const rid = (v: unknown) => String(typeof v === 'object' && v ? (v as { id?: unknown }).id : v ?? '')
export async function fetchTeacherStats(teacherId: string): Promise<TeacherStats> {
  const courses = await getItems<CourseRec>('courses', { filter: { owner: { _eq: asId(teacherId) } }, fields: ['id', 'total_hours'] })
  const ids = courses.map((c) => asId(c.id)).filter((x) => x !== '')
  const hours = courses.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.total_hours ?? 0), 0)
  let students = 0
  if (ids.length) {
    const enrolls = await getItems<EnrollRec>('enrollments', { filter: { course: { _in: ids } }, fields: ['student'] })
    students = new Set(enrolls.map((e) => rid(e.student))).size
  }
  return { courses: courses.length, students, hours }
}

===== FILE: src/entities/progress/service.ts =====
import { getItems, getFirst, createRec, updateRec, asId } from '@/shared/api/directus'
interface Rec { id: unknown; lesson?: unknown; completed?: boolean }
const rid = (v: unknown) => String(typeof v === 'object' && v ? (v as { id?: unknown }).id : v ?? '')
export async function fetchServerProgress(courseId: string, studentId: string): Promise<Record<string, boolean>> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('session_progress', { filter: { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, course: { _eq: asId(courseId) } }, fields: ['*'] })
  const m: Record<string, boolean> = {}
  for (const r of list) if (r.completed) m[rid(r.lesson)] = true
  return m
}
/**
 * Отметка урока на сервере (upsert). NB: XP/level/rank у пользователя и
 * enrollment.progress_percent пересчитает серверный хук ТОЛЬКО после шага 2
 * (Directus Flow). Сейчас пишется только completed/completed_at.
 */
export async function setLessonDone(courseId: string, studentId: string, lessonId: string, done: boolean): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await getFirst<Rec>('session_progress', { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, lesson: { _eq: asId(lessonId) } })
  if (existing) await updateRec('session_progress', String(existing.id), { completed: done, completed_at: done ? new Date().toISOString() : null })
  else await createRec('session_progress', { student: asId(studentId), course: asId(courseId), lesson: asId(lessonId), completed: done, completed_at: done ? new Date().toISOString() : null })
}

export interface QaItemReport { id: string; text: string; status: ItemStatus; add: string; improve: string; problems: string }
export interface QaSectionSummary { role: string; title: string; ok: number; bad: number; na: number; total: number; note: string; items: QaItemReport[] }
export interface QaReport { id: string; reporterId: string; name: string; role: string; score: number; problems: number; sections: QaSectionSummary[]; created: string }
export interface QaShot { item: string; url: string }

  const rec = await createRec<{ id: unknown }>('qa_reports', { reporter: asId(input.reporterId), name: input.name, role: input.role, score: input.score, problems: input.problems, payload: { sections: input.sections }, created: new Date().toISOString() })
  return String(rec.id)
}
/** NB: в PB файл шёл прямо в запись; в Directus сначала грузим в directus_files, потом link. */
export async function uploadQaScreenshot(reportId: string, item: string, file: File): Promise<void> {
  const fileId = await uploadFile(file)
  await createRec('qa_screenshots', { report: asId(reportId), item, image: fileId, created: new Date().toISOString() })
}
interface Rec { id: unknown; reporter?: unknown; name?: string; role?: string; score?: number; problems?: number; payload?: unknown; created?: string }
interface ShotRec { id: unknown; report?: unknown; item?: string; image?: unknown }
export async function fetchQaReports(): Promise<QaReport[]> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('qa_reports', { sort: '-created', fields: ['*'] })
  return list.map((r) => {
    const payload = parseJson<{ sections?: QaSectionSummary[] }>(r.payload, {})
    return { id: String(r.id), reporterId: String(typeof r.reporter === 'object' && r.reporter ? (r.reporter as { id?: unknown }).id : r.reporter ?? ''), name: r.name ?? '', role: r.role ?? '', score: r.score ?? 0, problems: r.problems ?? 0, sections: payload.sections ?? [], created: r.created ?? '' }
  })
}

  const list = await getItems<ShotRec>('qa_screenshots', { filter: { report: { _eq: asId(reportId) } }, fields: ['*'] })
  return list.filter((s) => s.image).map((s) => ({ item: s.item ?? '', url: assetUrl(String(typeof s.image === 'object' ? (s.image as { id?: unknown }).id : s.image)) }))
}

===== FILE: src/entities/quiz/service.ts =====
import { getItems, getItem, createRec, updateRec, deleteRec, asId, parseJson, relStr, relNum } from '@/shared/api/directus'
export type QuestionType = 'single' | 'multi' | 'text'
export interface Quiz { id: string; courseId: string; lessonId: string; authorId: string; title: string; description: string; timeLimit: number; passScore: number; rewardCoins: number; rewardXp: number; published: boolean }
export interface Question { id: string; quizId: string; text: string; type: QuestionType; options: string[]; correct: string[]; points: number; order: number }
export interface Attempt { id: string; quizId: string; quizTitle: string; studentId: string; score: number; passed: boolean; created: string }
interface QuizRec { id: unknown; course?: unknown; lesson?: unknown; author?: unknown; title: string; description?: string; time_limit?: number; pass_score?: number; reward_coins?: number; reward_xp?: number; published?: boolean }
interface QRec { id: unknown; quiz?: unknown; text: string; type?: QuestionType; options?: unknown; correct?: unknown; points?: number; order?: number }
interface ARec { id: unknown; quiz?: unknown; student?: unknown; answers?: unknown; score?: number; passed?: boolean; created?: string }
const rid = (v: unknown) => String(typeof v === 'object' && v ? (v as { id?: unknown }).id : v ?? '')
const mapQuiz = (r: QuizRec): Quiz => ({ id: String(r.id), courseId: rid(r.course), lessonId: rid(r.lesson), authorId: rid(r.author), title: r.title, description: r.description ?? '', timeLimit: r.time_limit ?? 0, passScore: r.pass_score ?? 60, rewardCoins: r.reward_coins ?? 0, rewardXp: r.reward_xp ?? 0, published: !!r.published })
const mapQ = (r: QRec, withCorrect: boolean): Question => ({ id: String(r.id), quizId: rid(r.quiz), text: r.text, type: (r.type ?? 'single') as QuestionType, options: parseJson<string[]>(r.options, []), correct: withCorrect ? parseJson<string[]>(r.correct, []) : [], points: r.points ?? 1, order: r.order ?? 0 })

export async function fetchAuthorQuizzes(authorId: string): Promise<Quiz[]> {
  return (await getItems<QuizRec>('quizzes', { filter: { author: { _eq: asId(authorId) } }, sort: '-created', fields: ['*'] })).map(mapQuiz)
}
export interface NewQuiz { courseId?: string; authorId: string; institutionId?: string; title: string; description?: string; timeLimit?: number; passScore?: number; rewardCoins?: number; rewardXp?: number; published?: boolean }
export async function createQuiz(input: NewQuiz): Promise<string> {
  const rec = await createRec<QuizRec>('quizzes', { ...(input.courseId ? { course: asId(input.courseId) } : {}), author: asId(input.authorId), ...(input.institutionId ? { institution: asId(input.institutionId) } : {}), title: input.title, description: input.description ?? '', time_limit: input.timeLimit ?? 0, pass_score: input.passScore ?? 60, reward_coins: input.rewardCoins ?? 0, reward_xp: input.rewardXp ?? 0, published: !!input.published, created: new Date().toISOString() })
  return String(rec.id)
}
export async function updateQuiz(id: string, patch: Partial<NewQuiz>): Promise<void> {
  const b: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  if (patch.courseId !== undefined) b.course = asId(patch.courseId)
  if (patch.title !== undefined) b.title = patch.title
  if (patch.description !== undefined) b.description = patch.description
  if (patch.timeLimit !== undefined) b.time_limit = patch.timeLimit
  if (patch.passScore !== undefined) b.pass_score = patch.passScore
  if (patch.rewardCoins !== undefined) b.reward_coins = patch.rewardCoins
  if (patch.rewardXp !== undefined) b.reward_xp = patch.rewardXp
  if (patch.published !== undefined) b.published = patch.published
  await updateRec('quizzes', id, b)
}
export const deleteQuiz = (id: string) => deleteRec('quizzes', id)
export async function fetchQuizQuestions(quizId: string): Promise<Question[]> {
  return (await getItems<QRec>('quiz_questions', { filter: { quiz: { _eq: asId(quizId) } }, sort: 'order', fields: ['*'] })).map((r) => mapQ(r, true))
}
export interface NewQuestion { quizId: string; text: string; type: QuestionType; options: string[]; correct: string[]; points: number; order: number }
export const addQuestion = (i: NewQuestion) => createRec('quiz_questions', { quiz: asId(i.quizId), text: i.text, type: i.type, options: i.options, correct: i.correct, points: i.points, order: i.order }).then(() => {})
export async function updateQuestion(id: string, patch: Partial<NewQuestion>): Promise<void> {
  const b: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  if (patch.text !== undefined) b.text = patch.text
  if (patch.type !== undefined) b.type = patch.type
  if (patch.options !== undefined) b.options = patch.options
  if (patch.correct !== undefined) b.correct = patch.correct
  if (patch.points !== undefined) b.points = patch.points
  if (patch.order !== undefined) b.order = patch.order
  await updateRec('quiz_questions', id, b)
}
export const deleteQuestion = (id: string) => deleteRec('quiz_questions', id)
export async function fetchPublishedQuizzes(): Promise<Quiz[]> {
  return (await getItems<QuizRec>('quizzes', { filter: { published: { _eq: true } }, sort: '-created', fields: ['*'] })).map(mapQuiz)
}
export async function fetchQuizForTake(quizId: string): Promise<{ quiz: Quiz; questions: Question[] }> {
  const quiz = mapQuiz(await getItem<QuizRec>('quizzes', quizId))
  const qlist = await getItems<QRec>('quiz_questions', { filter: { quiz: { _eq: asId(quizId) } }, sort: 'order', fields: ['*'] })
  return { quiz, questions: qlist.map((r) => mapQ(r, false)) }
}
/** NB: score/passed считает серверный хук (шаг 2). Сейчас возвращаем 0/false. */
export async function submitAttempt(quizId: string, studentId: string, answers: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<{ score: number; passed: boolean }> {
  const rec = await createRec<ARec>('quiz_attempts', { quiz: asId(quizId), student: asId(studentId), answers, created: new Date().toISOString() })
  return { score: rec.score ?? 0, passed: !!rec.passed }
}
export async function fetchMyAttempts(studentId: string): Promise<Attempt[]> {
  const list = await getItems<ARec>('quiz_attempts', { filter: { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) } }, sort: '-created', fields: ['*', 'quiz.id', 'quiz.title'] })
  return list.map((r) => ({ id: String(r.id), quizId: rid(r.quiz), quizTitle: relStr(r.quiz, 'title'), studentId: rid(r.student), score: r.score ?? 0, passed: !!r.passed, created: r.created ?? '' }))
}
export async function fetchMyQuizXp(studentId: string): Promise<number> {
  const list = await getItems<ARec>('quiz_attempts', { filter: { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, passed: { _eq: true } }, fields: ['*', 'quiz.id', 'quiz.reward_xp'] })
  const seen = new Set<string>(); let xp = 0
  for (const a of list) { const qid = rid(a.quiz); if (seen.has(qid)) continue; seen.add(qid); xp += relNum(a.quiz, 'reward_xp') }
  return xp
}

===== FILE: src/entities/stats/service.ts =====
import { getItems, getCount } from '@/shared/api/directus'

export interface PublicStats { courses: number; students: number; hours: number }
interface ProgRec { lesson?: unknown }
interface LessonRec { id: unknown; duration_hours?: number }
const rid = (v: unknown): string =>
  v == null ? '' : typeof v === 'object' ? String((v as { id?: unknown }).id ?? '') : String(v)

/**
 * Публичные метрики «масштаба платформы» для лендинга/портала — БЕЗ фильтра по
 * колледжу (это не админ-статистика, а витрина). Считаются агрегацией по
 * существующим коллекциям Directus под текущим (в dev — админским) токеном.
 * hours = сумма duration_hours по всем завершённым урокам (session_progress.completed),
 * поэтому НЕ зависит от серверного хука 2.3 — часы выводятся из уже пишемых фронтом записей.
 *
 * NB: getCount/getItems тянут записи целиком (limit:-1) — при маленьких объёмах
 * (сотни уроков/прогрессов) это корректно и просто. На больших данных витрину
 * переведём на серверную агрегацию (Directus aggregate / материализация в 2.x) —
 * заложено, но не блокер сегодня.
 */
export async function fetchPublicStats(): Promise<PublicStats> {
  const [courses, students, progress, lessons] = await Promise.all([
    getCount('courses'),
    getCount('users', { role: { _eq: 'student' } }),
    getItems<ProgRec>('session_progress', { filter: { completed: { _eq: true } }, fields: ['lesson'] }),
    getItems<LessonRec>('lessons', { fields: ['id', 'duration_hours'] }),
  ])
  const hoursById = new Map(lessons.map((l) => [rid(l.id), l.duration_hours ?? 0]))
  const hours = progress.reduce((s, p) => s + (hoursById.get(rid(p.lesson)) ?? 0), 0)
  return { courses, students, hours: Math.round(hours) }
}

===== FILE: src/entities/submission/service.ts =====
import { getItems, getFirst, createRec, updateRec, asId, relStr, uploadFile, assetUrl } from '@/shared/api/directus'
export type SubmissionStatus = 'submitted' | 'graded'
export interface Submission { id: string; studentId: string; courseId: string; lessonId: string; text: string; files: string[]; fileUrls: string[]; status: SubmissionStatus; grade: number; feedback: string; retry: boolean; submittedAt: string; studentName: string; studentCallsign: string; lessonTitle: string }
interface Rec { id: unknown; student?: unknown; course?: unknown; lesson?: unknown; text?: string; files?: unknown; status?: SubmissionStatus; grade?: number; feedback?: string; retry?: boolean; submitted_at?: string }
const rid = (v: unknown) => String(typeof v === 'object' && v ? (v as { id?: unknown }).id : v ?? '')
function map(r: Rec): Submission {
  const files = Array.isArray(r.files) ? r.files.map((f) => rid(f)).filter(Boolean) : (typeof r.files === 'string' && r.files ? [r.files] : [])
  const s = typeof r.student === 'object' && r.student ? (r.student as { full_name?: string; callsign?: string; student_id?: string }) : undefined
  return { id: String(r.id), studentId: rid(r.student), courseId: rid(r.course), lessonId: rid(r.lesson), text: r.text ?? '', files, fileUrls: files.map(assetUrl), status: r.status ?? 'submitted', grade: r.grade ?? 0, feedback: r.feedback ?? '', retry: !!r.retry, submittedAt: r.submitted_at ?? '', studentName: s?.full_name ?? '', studentCallsign: s?.callsign ?? s?.student_id ?? '', lessonTitle: relStr(r.lesson, 'title') }
}
const SUB_FIELDS = ['*', 'student.id', 'student.full_name', 'student.callsign', 'student.student_id', 'lesson.id', 'lesson.title']
export async function fetchMySubmission(lessonId: string, studentId: string): Promise<Submission | null> {
  const rec = await getFirst<Rec>('submissions', { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, lesson: { _eq: asId(lessonId) } }, SUB_FIELDS)
  return rec ? map(rec) : null
}
export interface SubmitInput { id?: string; courseId: string; lessonId: string; studentId: string; text: string; files: File[] }
/**
 * Сдача работы. NB: авто-статус/оценка/`retry`(wasFail)/уведомление преподавателю
 * делает серверный хук — он переносится в шаг 2. Здесь клиент сам ставит
 * стартовые значения, которые в PB проставлял хук onRecordCreateRequest.
 */
export async function submitWork(input: SubmitInput): Promise<void> {
  const fileIds: string[] = []
  for (const f of input.files) fileIds.push(await uploadFile(f))
  const base = { student: asId(input.studentId), course: asId(input.courseId), lesson: asId(input.lessonId), text: input.text, files: fileIds }
  if (input.id) {
    // пересдача студентом: сброс оценки (retry/wasFail — шаг 2)
    await updateRec('submissions', input.id, { ...base, status: 'submitted', grade: 0, feedback: '', graded_at: null, submitted_at: new Date().toISOString() })
  } else {
    await createRec('submissions', { ...base, status: 'submitted', grade: 0, feedback: '', retry: false, submitted_at: new Date().toISOString(), graded_at: null })
  }
}
export async function fetchMyGrades(studentId: string): Promise<number[]> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('submissions', { filter: { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, status: { _eq: 'graded' } }, fields: ['grade'] })
  return list.map((r) => r.grade ?? 0).filter((g) => g > 0)
}
export interface LessonMark { grade: number; retry: boolean }
export type LessonMarks = Record<string, LessonMark>
export async function fetchMyLessonMarks(studentId: string): Promise<LessonMarks> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('submissions', { filter: { student: { _eq: asId(studentId) }, status: { _eq: 'graded' } }, fields: ['*', 'lesson.id'] })
  const marks: LessonMarks = {}
  for (const r of list) marks[rid(r.lesson)] = { grade: r.grade ?? 0, retry: !!r.retry }
  return marks
}
export async function fetchMyCourseSubmissions(courseId: string, studentId: string): Promise<Submission[]> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('submissions', { filter: { course: { _eq: asId(courseId) }, student: { _eq: asId(studentId) } }, fields: SUB_FIELDS })
  return list.map(map)
}
export async function fetchCourseSubmissions(courseId: string): Promise<Submission[]> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('submissions', { filter: { course: { _eq: asId(courseId) } }, fields: SUB_FIELDS })
  return list.map(map).sort((a, b) => (b.submittedAt || '').localeCompare(a.submittedAt || ''))
}
/** Оценка от преподавателя: клиент ставит status/graded_at, которые в PB делал хук. */
export const gradeSubmission = (id: string, grade: number, feedback: string) => updateRec('submissions', id, { grade, feedback, status: 'graded', graded_at: new Date().toISOString() }).then(() => {})
export async function fetchTeacherPassedStudents(teacherId: string): Promise<number> {
  const list = await getItems<Rec>('submissions', { filter: { course: { owner: { _eq: asId(teacherId) } }, status: { _eq: 'graded' }, grade: { _gte: 3 } }, fields: ['student'] })
  return new Set(list.map((r) => rid(r.student))).size
}

===== FILE: src/features/a11y/A11yApply.tsx =====
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useA11y } from './a11yStore'

/** Применяет настройки доступности классами на <html>. Монтируется один раз в корне. */
export function A11yApply() {
  const { contrast, large, reduceMotion, grayscale } = useA11y()
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = document.documentElement
    el.classList.toggle('a11y-contrast', contrast)
    el.classList.toggle('a11y-large', large)
    el.classList.toggle('a11y-reduce-motion', reduceMotion)
    el.classList.toggle('a11y-grayscale', grayscale)
  }, [contrast, large, reduceMotion, grayscale])
  return null
}


===== FILE: src/features/a11y/A11yMenu.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Accessibility, Contrast, Type, Zap, Palette, RotateCcw, Check } from 'lucide-react'
import { useA11y, type A11ySettings } from './a11yStore'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

export function A11yMenu() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const s = useA11y()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    const onDoc = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false)
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDoc)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDoc)
  }, [open])

  const rows: { key: keyof A11ySettings; label: string; desc: string; icon: typeof Contrast }[] = [
    { key: 'contrast', label: t('a11y.contrast'), desc: t('a11y.contrast_desc'), icon: Contrast },
    { key: 'large', label: t('a11y.large'), desc: t('a11y.large_desc'), icon: Type },
    { key: 'reduceMotion', label: t('a11y.reduce_motion'), desc: t('a11y.reduce_motion_desc'), icon: Zap },
    { key: 'grayscale', label: t('a11y.grayscale'), desc: t('a11y.grayscale_desc'), icon: Palette },
  ]
  const active = rows.some((r) => s[r.key])

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative">
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        aria-label={t('a11y.title')}
        title={t('a11y.title')}
        className={cn(
          'flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-[8px] border transition-colors',
          active ? 'border-lime/60 text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:border-aqua/60 hover:text-ink',
        )}
      >
        <Accessibility size={16} />
      </button>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {open && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -6 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -6 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.16 }}
            className="clip-hud absolute right-0 z-50 mt-2 w-64 border border-line-aqua bg-surface p-4 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)]"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-aqua">{t('a11y.title')}</span>
              <button onClick={s.reset} className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
                <RotateCcw size={11} /> {t('a11y.reset')}
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
              {rows.map((r) => {
                const on = s[r.key]
                const Icon = r.icon
                return (
                  <button
                    key={r.key}
                    onClick={() => s.toggle(r.key)}
                    className={cn(
                      'flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors clip-btn',
                      on ? 'border-line-lime bg-lime/[0.06]' : 'border-line-strong hover:border-line-aqua',
                    )}
                  >
                    <span className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2.5">
                      <Icon size={15} className={cn('mt-0.5 shrink-0', on ? 'text-lime' : 'text-ink-dim')} />
                      <span className="min-w-0">
                        <span className={cn('block text-sm', on ? 'text-ink' : 'text-ink-soft')}>{r.label}</span>
                        <span className="mt-0.5 block text-[11px] leading-snug text-ink-muted">{r.desc}</span>
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    <span className={cn('mt-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border', on ? 'border-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-transparent')}>
                      <Check size={11} strokeWidth={3} />
                    </span>
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/a11y/a11yStore.ts =====
import { create } from 'zustand'

const KEY = 'protocol-a11y'

export interface A11ySettings {
  contrast: boolean // высокий контраст
  large: boolean // крупный шрифт
  reduceMotion: boolean // меньше анимаций
  grayscale: boolean // ч/б (для дальтоников)
}

const DEFAULT: A11ySettings = { contrast: false, large: false, reduceMotion: false, grayscale: false }

function read(): A11ySettings {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return DEFAULT
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY)
    return raw ? { ...DEFAULT, ...(JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<A11ySettings>) } : DEFAULT
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT
  }
}

interface A11yState extends A11ySettings {
  toggle: (key: keyof A11ySettings) => void
  reset: () => void
}

export const useA11y = create<A11yState>((set, get) => ({
  ...read(),
  toggle: (key) =>
    set(() => {
      const next = { ...get(), [key]: !get()[key] }
      const settings: A11ySettings = { contrast: next.contrast, large: next.large, reduceMotion: next.reduceMotion, grayscale: next.grayscale }
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(settings))
      return settings
    }),
  reset: () => {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') localStorage.removeItem(KEY)
    set(DEFAULT)
  },
}))


===== FILE: src/features/admin/AdminAudit.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { ScrollText, Download } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useAuditLogs, useAdminUsers } from './useAdmin'
import { fetchAuditForExport } from '@/entities/admin/service'
import { buildStyledXls, downloadXls } from '@/shared/lib/xlsExport'

function fmt(iso: string): string {
  if (!iso) return '—'
  const d = new Date(iso)
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? iso : d.toLocaleString('ru-RU', { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' })
}

const field = 'h-11 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

export function AdminAudit() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: logs = [], isLoading } = useAuditLogs()
  const { data: users = [] } = useAdminUsers()

  const [userId, setUserId] = useState('')
  const [from, setFrom] = useState('')
  const [to, setTo] = useState('')
  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false)

  const nameById = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, string>()
    for (const u of users) m.set(u.id, u.fullName || u.callsign || u.studentId || u.id)
    return m
  }, [users])

  async function exportXls() {
    setExporting(true)
    try {
      const rows = await fetchAuditForExport({ user: userId || undefined, from: from || undefined, to: to || undefined })
      const subtitleParts = [
        userId ? `${t('admin.a_export_user')}: ${nameById.get(userId) || userId}` : t('admin.a_export_all'),
        from ? `${t('admin.a_export_from')}: ${from}` : null,
        to ? `${t('admin.a_export_to')}: ${to}` : null,
      ].filter(Boolean)
      const xls = buildStyledXls(
        rows,
        [
          { header: t('admin.a_when'), value: (r) => fmt(r.created), width: 140 },
          { header: t('admin.a_action'), value: (r) => r.action, width: 180 },
          { header: t('admin.a_user'), value: (r) => nameById.get(r.user) || r.user || '—', width: 200 },
          { header: t('admin.a_target'), value: (r) => r.target || '—', width: 220 },
        ],
        { title: t('admin.a_export_title'), subtitle: subtitleParts.join(' · ') },
      )
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
      downloadXls(xls, `protocol-audit-${userId ? (nameById.get(userId) || userId).replace(/\s+/g, '_') : 'all'}-${stamp}.xls`)
    } finally {
      setExporting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* панель экспорта */}
      <div className="mb-5 border border-line-lime bg-surface p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Download size={15} className="text-lime" />
          <Label>{t('admin.a_export_title')}</Label>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-ink-muted">{t('admin.a_export_hint')}</p>
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 lg:items-end">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('admin.a_export_user')}</Label>
            <select className={field} value={userId} onChange={(e) => setUserId(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">{t('admin.a_export_all')}</option>
              {users.map((u) => (
                <option key={u.id} value={u.id}>{u.fullName || u.callsign || u.studentId}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('admin.a_export_from')}</Label>
            <input type="date" className={field} value={from} onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('admin.a_export_to')}</Label>
            <input type="date" className={field} value={to} onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <Button onClick={exportXls} disabled={exporting}>
            <Download size={15} /> {exporting ? '…' : t('admin.a_export_run')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="p-8 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
      ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-10 text-center text-ink-muted">
          <ScrollText size={22} className="text-ink-dim" />
          {t('admin.a_empty')}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-line bg-surface">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-line font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('admin.a_action')}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('admin.a_user')}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('admin.a_target')}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('admin.a_when')}</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {logs.map((l) => (
                <tr key={l.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-b-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <span className="border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs text-aqua">{l.action}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted">{nameById.get(l.user) || l.user || '—'}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted">{l.target || '—'}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">{fmt(l.created)}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/admin/AdminCourses.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Trash2, Search, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAdminCourses, useCourseModeration } from './useAdmin'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

export function AdminCourses({ scope }: { scope?: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: courses = [], isLoading } = useAdminCourses(scope)
  const mod = useCourseModeration()
  const confirm = useConfirm()
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase()
    return courses.filter((c) => !q || (c.title + ' ' + c.ownerName + ' ' + c.institutionShort).toLowerCase().includes(q))
  }, [courses, query])

  async function remove(id: string, title: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${title} — ${t('admin.c_delete_confirm')}`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true })
    if (ok) mod.remove.mutate(id)
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 clip-btn sm:w-80">
        <Search size={16} className="text-ink-muted" />
        <input value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder={t('admin.c_search')} className="h-11 w-full bg-transparent text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:outline-none" />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-2">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="p-8 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('admin.c_none')}</div>
        ) : (
          filtered.map((c) => (
            <div key={c.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
              <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border border-line-strong text-aqua">
                <BookOpen size={16} />
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-40 flex-1">
                <div className="truncate text-sm text-ink">{c.title}</div>
                <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">
                  {c.institutionShort} · {c.ownerName} · {c.sections} {t('common.sections')} · {c.lessons} {t('common.lessons')}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => mod.setStatus.mutate({ id: c.id, status: c.status === 'published' ? 'draft' : 'published' })}
                className={cn(
                  'border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase transition-colors clip-btn',
                  c.status === 'published' ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-aqua',
                )}
              >
                {c.status === 'published' ? t('admin.c_published') : t('admin.c_draft')}
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => remove(c.id, c.title)} className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger" aria-label={t('common.delete')}>
                <Trash2 size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/admin/AdminGroups.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Plus, Trash2, Users2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useAdminGroups, useGroupMutations, useAdminUsers } from './useAdmin'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'

const field = 'h-11 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'
const CUR_YEAR = new Date().getFullYear()

export function AdminGroups({ scope }: { scope?: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: groups = [] } = useAdminGroups(scope)
  const { data: users = [] } = useAdminUsers(scope)
  const teachers = users.filter((u) => u.role === 'teacher')
  const m = useGroupMutations()
  const confirm = useConfirm()

  const [specialty, setSpecialty] = useState('')
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(1)
  const [startYear, setStartYear] = useState(CUR_YEAR)
  const [curatorId, setCuratorId] = useState('')

  function create() {
    if (!specialty.trim()) return
    m.create.mutate(
      { specialty: specialty.trim(), index, startYear, curatorId: curatorId || undefined, institutionId: scope },
      { onSuccess: () => { setSpecialty(''); setIndex(1); setCuratorId('') } },
    )
  }
  async function remove(id: string, code: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${code} — ${t('admin.g_delete_confirm')}`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true })
    if (ok) m.remove.mutate(id)
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="grid gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 lg:items-end">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('admin.g_specialty')}</Label>
          <input className={field} value={specialty} onChange={(e) => setSpecialty(e.target.value)} placeholder="ИСиП-ИИ" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('admin.g_index')}</Label>
          <input type="number" min={1} className={field} value={index} onChange={(e) => setIndex(Number(e.target.value) || 1)} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('admin.g_start_year')}</Label>
          <input type="number" min={2000} className={field} value={startYear} onChange={(e) => setStartYear(Number(e.target.value) || CUR_YEAR)} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('admin.g_curator')}</Label>
          <select className={field} value={curatorId} onChange={(e) => setCuratorId(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">—</option>
            {teachers.map((tch) => <option key={tch.id} value={tch.id}>{tch.fullName || tch.studentId}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="lg:col-span-5">
          <Button onClick={create} disabled={m.create.isPending || !specialty.trim()}><Plus size={15} /> {t('admin.g_create')}</Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-2">
        {groups.length === 0 && <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('admin.g_none')}</div>}
        {groups.map((g) => (
          <div key={g.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
            <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border border-line-strong text-aqua"><Users2 size={16} /></span>
            <div className="min-w-40 flex-1">
              <div className="text-sm text-ink">{g.code}</div>
              <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">
                {t('admin.g_specialty')}: {g.specialty} · {t('admin.g_start_year')}: {g.startYear}
              </div>
            </div>
            <select
              className="h-9 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-2 text-xs text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
              value={g.curatorId}
              onChange={(e) => m.update.mutate({ id: g.id, patch: { curatorId: e.target.value } })}
            >
              <option value="">{t('admin.g_no_curator')}</option>
              {teachers.map((tch) => <option key={tch.id} value={tch.id}>{tch.fullName || tch.studentId}</option>)}
            </select>
            <button onClick={() => remove(g.id, g.code)} className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger" aria-label={t('common.delete')}>
              <Trash2 size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/admin/AdminInstitutions.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Plus, Trash2, School } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useAdminInstitutions, useInstitutionMutations } from './useAdmin'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'

const field = 'h-11 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

export function AdminInstitutions() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: list = [] } = useAdminInstitutions()
  const m = useInstitutionMutations()
  const confirm = useConfirm()
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [short, setShort] = useState('')

  async function remove(id: string, label: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${label} — ${t('admin.i_delete_confirm')}`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true })
    if (ok) m.remove.mutate(id)
  }

  function create() {
    if (!name.trim() || !short.trim()) return
    m.create.mutate({ name: name.trim(), shortName: short.trim() }, { onSuccess: () => { setName(''); setShort('') } })
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-4">
        <div className="flex min-w-56 flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('admin.i_name')}</Label>
          <input className={field} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Белгородский индустриальный колледж" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex w-32 flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('admin.i_short')}</Label>
          <input className={field} value={short} onChange={(e) => setShort(e.target.value)} placeholder="БИК" />
        </div>
        <Button onClick={create} disabled={m.create.isPending || !name.trim() || !short.trim()}>
          <Plus size={15} /> {t('admin.i_create')}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-2">
        {list.length === 0 && (
          <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('admin.i_none')}</div>
        )}
        {list.map((i) => (
          <div key={i.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
            <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border border-line-strong text-aqua">
              <School size={16} />
            </span>
            <input
              className={`${field} min-w-48 flex-1`}
              defaultValue={i.name}
              onBlur={(e) => e.target.value.trim() && e.target.value !== i.name && m.update.mutate({ id: i.id, patch: { name: e.target.value.trim() } })}
            />
            <input
              className={`${field} w-28`}
              defaultValue={i.shortName}
              onBlur={(e) => e.target.value.trim() && e.target.value !== i.shortName && m.update.mutate({ id: i.id, patch: { shortName: e.target.value.trim() } })}
            />
            <button
              onClick={() => remove(i.id, i.name)}
              className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger"
              aria-label={t('common.delete')}
            >
              <Trash2 size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
