  },
  {
    id: 'profile',
    title: 'Профиль пользователя',
    task: 'Экран профиля: навигация, аватар, заголовок-имя, список данных, кнопка «Редактировать», футер.',
    frame: 'mobile',
    palette: FULL_PALETTE,
    required: { nav: 1, avatar: 1, heading: 1, list: 1, button: 1, footer: 1 },
    rules: { firstShouldBe: 'nav', lastShouldBe: 'footer', singleCTA: true, minBlocks: 5, maxBlocks: 12 },
  },
]

export interface CustomSpec {
  task: string
  required: Partial<Record<LeafKind, number>>
  firstNav?: boolean
  lastFooter?: boolean
  singleCTA?: boolean
  frame?: 'mobile' | 'desktop'
}

/** Собрать своё задание с критериями авто-проверки (режим преподавателя). */
export function makeCustomExercise(spec: CustomSpec | string): Exercise {
  // обратная совместимость: строка = свободный режим с описанием
  if (typeof spec === 'string') {
    return { id: 'custom', title: 'Своё задание', task: spec || 'Соберите макет по заданию преподавателя.', frame: 'desktop', palette: FULL_PALETTE, required: {}, rules: {}, free: true }
  }
  const hasCriteria = Object.values(spec.required).some((n) => (n ?? 0) > 0) || spec.firstNav || spec.lastFooter || spec.singleCTA
  return {
    id: 'custom',
    title: 'Своё задание',
    task: spec.task || 'Соберите макет по заданию преподавателя.',
    frame: spec.frame ?? 'desktop',
    palette: FULL_PALETTE,
    required: spec.required,
    rules: {
      firstShouldBe: spec.firstNav ? 'nav' : undefined,
      lastShouldBe: spec.lastFooter ? 'footer' : undefined,
      singleCTA: spec.singleCTA,
    },
    free: !hasCriteria, // без критериев — свободная эвристика
  }
}


===== FILE: src/features/playground/NodeView.tsx =====
import { TONE_HEX, type Node, type Size } from './blocks'

const H: Record<Size, { pad: string; text: string; bar: number }> = {
  sm: { pad: 'py-2 px-3', text: 'text-sm', bar: 6 },
  md: { pad: 'py-3 px-4', text: 'text-base', bar: 8 },
  lg: { pad: 'py-5 px-6', text: 'text-xl', bar: 12 },
}

export function NodeView({ node }: { node: Node }) {
  const tone = TONE_HEX[node.tone ?? 'default']
  const s = H[node.size ?? 'md']

  switch (node.kind) {
    case 'row':
      return (
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          {(node.children ?? []).length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex-1 border border-dashed border-line-strong/60 py-4 text-center font-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim">
              пустой ряд
            </div>
          ) : (
            (node.children ?? []).map((c) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <NodeView node={c} />
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      )
    case 'nav':
      return (
        <div className="flex h-9 items-center justify-between border border-line bg-surface-2 px-3">
          <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-sm" style={{ background: tone }} />
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <span className="h-1.5 w-8 bg-ink-dim" /><span className="h-1.5 w-8 bg-ink-dim" /><span className="h-1.5 w-8 bg-ink-dim" />
          </div>
        </div>
      )
    case 'hero':
      return (
        <div className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-2 border border-line-aqua bg-surface-2 ${s.pad}`}>
          <span className={`font-display font-bold ${s.text}`} style={{ color: tone }}>{node.text}</span>
          <span className="h-2 w-1/2 bg-ink-muted" />
          <span className="mt-1 h-5 w-24" style={{ background: TONE_HEX.lime }} />
        </div>
      )
    case 'heading':
      return <div className={`font-display font-bold ${s.text}`} style={{ color: tone }}>{node.text}</div>
    case 'text':
      return <p className={`${s.text === 'text-xl' ? 'text-base' : 'text-sm'} leading-relaxed text-ink-muted`}>{node.text}</p>
    case 'button':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-5 font-display font-bold clip-btn" style={{ background: tone, color: '#0b0d12', height: s.bar * 3 }}>
          {node.text}
        </span>
      )
    case 'image':
      return (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center border border-line bg-surface-2" style={{ height: s.bar * 10 }}>
          <span className="rounded-sm border-2 border-ink-dim" style={{ width: s.bar * 3, height: s.bar * 3 }} />
        </div>
      )
    case 'card':
      return (
        <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-3">
          <span className="block h-10 w-full bg-surface-2" />
          <span className="mt-2 block text-xs" style={{ color: tone }}>{node.text}</span>
        </div>
      )
    case 'input':
      return <div className="flex h-9 items-center border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3"><span className="h-1.5 w-24 bg-ink-dim" /></div>
    case 'footer':
      return (
        <div className="flex h-10 items-center justify-between border-t border-line bg-surface-2 px-3">
          <span className="h-1.5 w-16 bg-ink-dim" /><span className="h-1.5 w-10 bg-ink-dim" />
        </div>
      )
    case 'spacer':
      return <div className="h-6 border border-dashed border-line-strong/40" />
    case 'badge':
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider" style={{ border: `1px solid ${tone}`, color: tone }}>
          {node.text}
        </span>
      )
    case 'list':
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5" style={{ background: tone }} />
              <span className="h-1.5 flex-1 bg-ink-dim" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )
    case 'avatar':
      return <span className="block rounded-full" style={{ width: s.bar * 4, height: s.bar * 4, background: tone }} />
    case 'stat':
      return (
        <div className="border border-line bg-surface-2 px-3 py-2 text-center">
          <div className={`font-display font-bold ${s.text}`} style={{ color: tone }}>{node.text}</div>
          <div className="mt-0.5 h-1.5 w-12 bg-ink-dim mx-auto" />
        </div>
      )
  }
}


===== FILE: src/features/playground/PlaygroundRunner.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { Plus, X, RotateCcw, GripVertical, AlignLeft, AlignCenter, AlignRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { ProgressRing } from '@/shared/ui/ProgressRing'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'
import {
  BLOCK_DEFS, TONE_HEX, flattenLeaves, newNode,
  type Align, type NodeKind, type Node, type Size, type Tone,
} from './blocks'
import { NodeView } from './NodeView'
import { scoreDesign } from './score'
import type { Exercise } from './exercises'

const AUTO_WIDTH: NodeKind[] = ['heading', 'button']
const TONES: Tone[] = ['default', 'aqua', 'lime', 'teal', 'muted']
const SIZES: Size[] = ['sm', 'md', 'lg']

function ringColor(p: number): string {
  if (p >= 90) return '#beef8d'
  if (p >= 60) return '#9fe8ff'
  if (p >= 30) return '#e8c14a'
  return '#ff5d6c'
}
function justify(a?: Align): string {
  return a === 'left' ? 'justify-start' : a === 'right' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-center'
}

function mapTree(nodes: Node[], id: string, fn: (n: Node) => Node): Node[] {
  return nodes.map((n) => {
    if (n.id === id) return fn(n)
    if (n.kind === 'row' && n.children) return { ...n, children: mapTree(n.children, id, fn) }
    return n
  })
}
function removeTree(nodes: Node[], id: string): Node[] {
  return nodes
    .filter((n) => n.id !== id)
    .map((n) => (n.kind === 'row' && n.children ? { ...n, children: removeTree(n.children, id) } : n))
}
function findNode(nodes: Node[], id: string): Node | null {
  for (const n of nodes) {
    if (n.id === id) return n
    if (n.kind === 'row' && n.children) {
      const f = findNode(n.children, id)
      if (f) return f
    }
  }
  return null
}

export function PlaygroundRunner({ exercise }: { exercise: Exercise }) {
  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<Node[]>([])
  const [selId, setSelId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const dragIndex = useRef<number | null>(null)

  const result = useMemo(() => scoreDesign(nodes, exercise), [nodes, exercise])
  const selected = selId ? findNode(nodes, selId) : null

  function add(kind: NodeKind): void {
    const node = newNode(kind)
    const sel = selId ? findNode(nodes, selId) : null
    if (sel && sel.kind === 'row' && kind !== 'row') {
      setNodes((ns) => mapTree(ns, sel.id, (r) => ({ ...r, children: [...(r.children ?? []), node] })))
    } else {
      setNodes((ns) => [...ns, node])
    }
    setSelId(node.id)
  }
  function patch(id: string, p: Partial<Node>): void {
    setNodes((ns) => mapTree(ns, id, (n) => ({ ...n, ...p })))
  }
  function remove(id: string): void {
    setNodes((ns) => removeTree(ns, id))
    if (selId === id) setSelId(null)
  }
  function reorderTop(from: number, to: number): void {
    setNodes((ns) => {
      if (from === to || from < 0 || to < 0 || from >= ns.length || to >= ns.length) return ns
      const next = [...ns]
      const [m] = next.splice(from, 1)
      next.splice(to, 0, m)
      return next
    })
  }

  const frameW = exercise.frame === 'mobile' ? 'w-[300px]' : 'w-full'

  function renderNode(n: Node) {
    const full = !AUTO_WIDTH.includes(n.kind)
    return (
      <div className={cn('flex', justify(n.align))}>
        <div
          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setSelId(n.id) }}
          className={cn('relative cursor-pointer', full ? 'w-full' : '', selId === n.id && 'outline outline-2 -outline-offset-2 outline-aqua')}
        >
          <NodeView node={n} />
          {selId === n.id && (
            <button
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); remove(n.id) }}
              aria-label="Удалить блок"
              className="absolute -right-2 -top-2 z-10 flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center border border-line-strong bg-surface text-ink-muted hover:text-danger"
            >
              <X size={12} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[210px_1fr_250px]">
      {/* Палитра */}
      <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-3">
        <Label>// блоки</Label>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          {exercise.palette.map((kind) => (
            <button
              key={kind}
              onClick={() => add(kind)}
              className={cn(
                'flex h-10 items-center justify-between border px-3 text-left text-sm transition-colors',
                kind === 'row' ? 'border-line-lime text-lime hover:bg-lime/10' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-soft hover:border-aqua hover:text-aqua',
              )}
            >
              {BLOCK_DEFS[kind].label}
              <Plus size={14} />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {selected?.kind === 'row' && (
          <p className="mt-3 font-mono text-[10px] text-lime">Ряд выбран — блоки добавятся внутрь</p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Холст */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
        <div
          onClick={() => setSelId(null)}
          className={cn(frameW, 'mx-auto min-h-[280px] border border-line-strong bg-bg p-4')}
        >
          {nodes.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-[240px] max-w-xs items-center justify-center text-center font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-dim">
              Добавляйте блоки слева, перетаскивайте для порядка, выделяйте для настройки
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              {nodes.map((n, i) => (
                <div
                  key={n.id}
                  draggable
                  onDragStart={() => (dragIndex.current = i)}
                  onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                  onDrop={() => { if (dragIndex.current !== null) reorderTop(dragIndex.current, i); dragIndex.current = null }}
                  className="group flex items-start gap-1"
                >
                  <span className="mt-2 cursor-grab text-ink-dim opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"><GripVertical size={14} /></span>
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">{renderNode(n)}</div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => { setNodes([]); setSelId(null) }} className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
          <RotateCcw size={13} /> сбросить
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Панель: свойства ИЛИ оценка */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {selected && selected.kind !== 'row' && (
          <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-4">
            <Label accent>// свойства</Label>
            {BLOCK_DEFS[selected.kind].hasText && (
              <div className="mt-3">
                <span className="label-mono text-[10px]">текст</span>
                <input
                  value={selected.text ?? ''}
                  onChange={(e) => patch(selected.id, { text: e.target.value })}
                  className="mt-1 h-9 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-2 text-sm text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="mt-3">
              <span className="label-mono text-[10px]">выравнивание</span>
              <div className="mt-1 flex gap-1">
                {(['left', 'center', 'right'] as Align[]).map((a) => {
                  const Icon = a === 'left' ? AlignLeft : a === 'center' ? AlignCenter : AlignRight
                  return (
                    <button key={a} onClick={() => patch(selected.id, { align: a })} aria-label={a}
                      className={cn('flex h-8 flex-1 items-center justify-center border', selected.align === a ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted')}>
                      <Icon size={15} />
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3">
              <span className="label-mono text-[10px]">цвет</span>
              <div className="mt-1 flex gap-2">
                {TONES.map((tn) => (
                  <button key={tn} onClick={() => patch(selected.id, { tone: tn })} aria-label={tn}
                    className={cn('h-7 w-7 border', selected.tone === tn ? 'border-aqua' : 'border-line-strong')}
                    style={{ background: TONE_HEX[tn] }} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3">
              <span className="label-mono text-[10px]">размер</span>
              <div className="mt-1 flex gap-1">
                {SIZES.map((sz) => (
                  <button key={sz} onClick={() => patch(selected.id, { size: sz })}
                    className={cn('h-8 flex-1 border font-mono text-xs uppercase', selected.size === sz ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted')}>
                    {sz}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="border border-line-aqua bg-surface p-4">
          <Label accent>// оценка</Label>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col items-center">
            <ProgressRing value={result.percent} size={88} stroke={7} color={ringColor(result.percent)} />
            <motion.div key={result.verdict} initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} className="mt-2 text-center text-sm text-ink">
              {result.verdict}
            </motion.div>
          </div>
          <ul className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2.5">
            {result.checks.map((c, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[14px_minmax(0,1fr)] items-start gap-x-2 text-xs">
                <span className={cn('mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 border', c.ok ? 'border-lime bg-lime/20' : 'border-line-strong')} />
                <span className={c.ok ? 'text-ink-soft' : 'text-ink-muted'}>
                  {c.label}
                  {!c.ok && c.tip && <span className="block text-ink-dim">→ {c.tip}</span>}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <div className="mt-3 font-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim">блоков: {flattenLeaves(nodes).length}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/playground/score.ts =====
import { BLOCK_DEFS, flattenLeaves, type Node } from './blocks'
import type { Exercise } from './exercises'

export interface Check { label: string; ok: boolean; weight: number; tip?: string }
export interface ScoreResult { percent: number; checks: Check[]; verdict: string }

function leafCount(leaves: Node[], kind: string): number {
  return leaves.filter((n) => n.kind === kind).length
}

export function scoreDesign(nodes: Node[], ex: Exercise): ScoreResult {
  const leaves = flattenLeaves(nodes)
  const checks: Check[] = []
  const r = ex.rules

  if (ex.free) {
    // свободный режим — общая эвристика качества
    const ctas = leaves.filter((n) => BLOCK_DEFS[n.kind].cta).length
    const hasStructure = leaves.some((n) => n.kind === 'nav') || leaves.some((n) => BLOCK_DEFS[n.kind].hero)
    const hasHeading = leaves.some((n) => n.kind === 'heading' || BLOCK_DEFS[n.kind].hero)
    checks.push({ label: 'Есть смысловая структура (навигация/герой)', ok: hasStructure, weight: 2, tip: 'Добавьте навигацию или герой' })
    checks.push({ label: 'Есть заголовок', ok: hasHeading, weight: 1, tip: 'Добавьте заголовок' })
    checks.push({ label: 'Один главный CTA', ok: ctas === 1, weight: 2, tip: ctas === 0 ? 'Добавьте кнопку' : 'Оставьте одну главную кнопку' })
    checks.push({ label: 'Достаточно контента', ok: leaves.length >= 3, weight: 1, tip: 'Добавьте блоки' })
    checks.push({ label: 'Не перегружено', ok: leaves.length <= 14, weight: 1, tip: 'Уберите лишнее' })
  } else {
    for (const [kind, need] of Object.entries(ex.required)) {
      const have = leafCount(leaves, kind)
      const def = BLOCK_DEFS[kind as keyof typeof BLOCK_DEFS]
      checks.push({ label: `${def.label}: ${have}/${need}`, ok: have >= (need ?? 0), weight: 3, tip: have < (need ?? 0) ? `Добавьте «${def.label}»` : undefined })
    }
    if (r.firstShouldBe) {
      const ok = nodes[0]?.kind === r.firstShouldBe
      checks.push({ label: `Первым — «${BLOCK_DEFS[r.firstShouldBe].label}»`, ok, weight: 2, tip: ok ? undefined : 'Переместите его наверх' })
    }
    if (r.lastShouldBe) {
      const ok = nodes[nodes.length - 1]?.kind === r.lastShouldBe
      checks.push({ label: `Последним — «${BLOCK_DEFS[r.lastShouldBe].label}»`, ok, weight: 2, tip: ok ? undefined : 'Переместите его вниз' })
    }
    if (r.heroNearTop) {
      const idx = nodes.findIndex((n) => BLOCK_DEFS[n.kind].hero)
      const ok = idx >= 0 && idx <= 1
      checks.push({ label: 'Герой — в начале', ok, weight: 2, tip: ok ? undefined : 'Поднимите герой выше' })
    }
    if (r.singleCTA) {
      const ctas = leaves.filter((n) => BLOCK_DEFS[n.kind].cta).length
      checks.push({ label: 'Один главный CTA', ok: ctas === 1, weight: 2, tip: ctas === 0 ? 'Добавьте кнопку-CTA' : ctas > 1 ? 'Оставьте одну' : undefined })
    }
    if (r.minBlocks || r.maxBlocks) {
      const n = leaves.length
      const ok = (!r.minBlocks || n >= r.minBlocks) && (!r.maxBlocks || n <= r.maxBlocks)
      checks.push({ label: 'Сбалансированный объём', ok, weight: 1, tip: ok ? undefined : r.maxBlocks && n > r.maxBlocks ? 'Слишком много блоков' : 'Добавьте содержания' })
    }
  }

  const totalW = checks.reduce((s, c) => s + c.weight, 0)
  const gotW = checks.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.ok ? c.weight : 0), 0)
  const percent = totalW > 0 ? Math.round((gotW / totalW) * 100) : 0
  const verdict =
    leaves.length === 0 ? 'Начните собирать макет' : percent >= 90 ? 'Отличный дизайн' : percent >= 70 ? 'Хорошо, есть что улучшить' : percent >= 40 ? 'Неплохое начало' : 'Ещё поработать'
  return { percent, checks, verdict }
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/GenerativeAvatar.tsx =====
import { useId, useMemo } from 'react'
import { RANK_STYLES, type Rank } from '@/entities/profile/ranks'

function hash(str: string): number {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i)
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619)
  }
  return h >>> 0
}

function makeRng(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a)
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296
  }
}

/** Полярная точка: угол 0 = вверх, центр (50,50). */
function polar(angle: number, r: number): string {
  return `${(50 + Math.sin(angle) * r).toFixed(1)},${(50 - Math.cos(angle) * r).toFixed(1)}`
}

interface Petal {
  points: string
  fill: string
  opacity: number
}

/**
 * Уникальный голографический аватар: радиально-симметричная эмблема,
 * детерминированная по `seed` (ID). «Крутость» растёт с рангом (F→SSS).
 */
export function GenerativeAvatar({
  seed,
  rank,
  size = 96,
  className,
}: {
  seed: string
  rank: Rank
  size?: number
  className?: string
}) {
  const gid = useId().replace(/:/g, '')
  const st = RANK_STYLES[rank]

  const model = useMemo(() => {
    const rnd = makeRng(hash(seed) ^ (rank.length * 2654435761))
    // Порядок симметрии — чётко геометричный
    const sym = [4, 5, 6, 8][Math.floor(rnd() * 4)]
    const motifCount = Math.max(1, Math.round(st.shapes / 4))
    const petals: Petal[] = []
    for (let m = 0; m < motifCount; m++) {
      const rOuter = 40 - m * 8 - rnd() * 4
      const rInner = 7 + rnd() * 6
      const rMid = rInner + (rOuter - rInner) * (0.5 + rnd() * 0.2)
      const width = (Math.PI / sym) * (0.55 + rnd() * 0.35)
      // лепесток-ромб, зеркально-симметричный относительно своей оси
      const points = [polar(0, rInner), polar(width, rMid), polar(0, rOuter), polar(-width, rMid)].join(' ')
      petals.push({
        points,
        fill: st.palette[(m + 1) % st.palette.length],
        opacity: 0.62 - m * 0.14,
      })
    }
    const orbR = 9 + rnd() * 5
    const orbColor = st.palette[Math.floor(rnd() * Math.min(2, st.palette.length))]
    return { sym, petals, orbR, orbColor }
  }, [seed, rank, st])

  const accent = st.palette[0]
  const glowFilter = st.glow > 0 ? `drop-shadow(0 0 ${Math.round(st.glow * 9)}px ${st.halo})` : undefined

  const hexPts = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => {
    const a = (Math.PI / 3) * i - Math.PI / 2
    return `${(50 + Math.cos(a) * 44).toFixed(1)},${(50 + Math.sin(a) * 44).toFixed(1)}`
  }).join(' ')

  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 100 100"
      width={size}
      height={size}
      className={className}
      role="img"
      aria-label={`Цифровой аватар, ранг ${rank}`}
      style={glowFilter ? { filter: glowFilter } : undefined}
    >
      <rect x="0" y="0" width="100" height="100" fill="#0e1119" />

      {/* концентрические кольца */}
      {Array.from({ length: st.rings }, (_, i) => (
        <circle key={`r${i}`} cx="50" cy="50" r={20 + i * 9} fill="none" stroke={accent} strokeWidth="0.5" opacity={0.25 + i * 0.12} />
      ))}

      {/* симметричный глиф: каждый лепесток размножен по кругу */}
      {model.petals.map((p, mi) =>
        Array.from({ length: model.sym }, (_, k) => (
          <polygon
            key={`${mi}-${k}`}
            points={p.points}
            fill={p.fill}
            opacity={p.opacity}
            transform={`rotate(${((360 / model.sym) * k).toFixed(2)} 50 50)`}
          />
        )),
      )}

      {/* ядро */}
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id={`orb-${gid}`} cx="50%" cy="50%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ffffff" stopOpacity="0.9" />
          <stop offset="35%" stopColor={model.orbColor} />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#0b0d12" />
        </radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <circle
        cx="50"
        cy="50"
        r={model.orbR}
        fill={`url(#orb-${gid})`}
        style={st.pulse ? { animation: 'protocol-pulse 2.4s ease-in-out infinite' } : undefined}
      />

      {/* вращающееся внешнее кольцо (A+) */}
      {st.spin && (
        <g style={{ transformBox: 'fill-box', transformOrigin: 'center', animation: 'protocol-spin 9s linear infinite' }}>
          <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="40" fill="none" stroke={accent} strokeWidth="0.6" strokeDasharray="3 5" opacity="0.7" />
        </g>
      )}

      {/* рамки */}
      {st.frame === 'ticks' && (
        <g stroke={accent} strokeWidth="1.4">
          <path d="M6 6 h10 M6 6 v10" />
          <path d="M94 6 h-10 M94 6 v10" />
          <path d="M6 94 h10 M6 94 v-10" />
          <path d="M94 94 h-10 M94 94 v-10" />
        </g>
      )}
      {(st.frame === 'hex' || st.frame === 'double' || st.frame === 'crown') && (
        <polygon points={hexPts} fill="none" stroke={accent} strokeWidth="0.9" opacity="0.85" />
      )}
      {st.frame === 'double' && (
        <g style={{ transformBox: 'fill-box', transformOrigin: 'center', animation: 'protocol-spin-rev 14s linear infinite' }}>
          <polygon points={hexPts} fill="none" stroke={st.palette[1] ?? accent} strokeWidth="0.5" opacity="0.5" transform="scale(0.82)" style={{ transformBox: 'fill-box', transformOrigin: 'center' }} />
        </g>
      )}
      {st.frame === 'crown' && (
        <g fill={st.halo}>
          {[-1, 0, 1].map((d) => (
            <polygon key={d} points={`${50 + d * 10},4 ${47 + d * 10},11 ${53 + d * 10},11`} />
          ))}
        </g>
      )}

      {/* орбитальные частицы (S+) */}
      {st.particles > 0 && (
        <g style={{ transformBox: 'fill-box', transformOrigin: 'center', animation: 'protocol-spin 7s linear infinite' }}>
          {Array.from({ length: st.particles }, (_, i) => {
            const a = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / st.particles
            return (
              <circle key={i} cx={(50 + Math.cos(a) * 43).toFixed(1)} cy={(50 + Math.sin(a) * 43).toFixed(1)} r="1.6" fill={st.palette[i % st.palette.length]} />
            )
          })}
        </g>
      )}
    </svg>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/ProfileCabinet.tsx =====
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { LogOut } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { ProfileIdCard } from './ProfileIdCard'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { logout } from '@/features/auth/authService'
import { useXp } from '@/features/progress/useXp'
import { useProfileRank } from '@/features/progress/useRank'
import { useMyGrades } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { average } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import type { ProfileCard } from '@/entities/profile/types'

const ROLE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { student: 'Студент', teacher: 'Преподаватель', admin: 'Администратор', superadmin: 'Супер-админ' }
function fmtDate(iso?: string): string {
  if (!iso) return '—'
  const d = new Date(iso)
  return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? '—' : d.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: '2-digit', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })
}

/** Полный личный кабинет для портала: большая карточка + личные данные. */
export function ProfileCabinet() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const xp = useXp()
  const rankInfo = useProfileRank()
  const { data: grades = [] } = useMyGrades(user?.id ?? '', user?.role === 'student')
  if (!user) return null

  const card: ProfileCard = {
    id: user.id,
    callsign: user.callsign,
    fullName: user.fullName,
    institutionShort: user.institutionShort,
    institutionName: user.institutionName,
    role: user.role,
    level: xp.level,
    xp: xp.xp,
    xpForNext: xp.forNext,
    progressPct: xp.forNext > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((xp.inLevel / xp.forNext) * 100))) : 0,
    rank: rankInfo.rank,
    hours: rankInfo.hours,
    teacherStudents: rankInfo.teacherStudents,
    avgGrade: average(grades),
    coins: user.coins,
    groupCode: user.groupCode,
  }

  return (
    <div className="clip-hud mb-10 grid items-start gap-6 border border-line-aqua bg-surface p-5 sm:p-6 lg:grid-cols-[22rem_1fr]">
      <ProfileIdCard card={card} />

      <div className="flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <Label accent>{t('profile.personal')}</Label>
          <button
            onClick={() => logout()}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:border-danger hover:text-danger clip-btn"
          >
            <LogOut size={13} /> {t('nav.logout')}
          </button>
        </div>
        <dl className="mt-4 flex flex-col divide-y divide-line">
          {[
            { k: t('profile.full_name'), v: user.fullName || '—' },
            { k: t('profile.login'), v: user.studentId || '—' },
            { k: 'Email', v: user.email || '—' },
            { k: t('profile.role'), v: ROLE_LABEL[user.role] ?? user.role },
            { k: t('profile.institution'), v: user.role === 'superadmin' ? 'Вся система' : (user.institutionName || user.institutionShort || '—') },
            ...(user.role === 'student' ? [{ k: t('profile.group'), v: user.groupCode || '—' }] : []),
            ...(user.role === 'student' ? [{ k: t('profile.curator'), v: user.curatorName || '—' }] : []),
            { k: t('profile.registered'), v: fmtDate(user.createdAt) },
          ].map((row) => (
            <div key={row.k} className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 py-2.5">
              <dt className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{row.k}</dt>
              <dd className="min-w-0 truncate text-right text-sm text-ink-soft">{row.v}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
        <p className="mt-auto border-t border-line pt-4 text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-muted">
          {t('profile.data_rights')}{' '}
          <Link to="/privacy" className="text-aqua hover:underline">{t('privacy.link')}</Link>.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/ProfileIdCard.tsx =====
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { RoleAvatar } from './RoleAvatar'
import { Coin } from '@/features/currency/Coin'
import { gradeTone } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import type { ProfileCard } from '@/entities/profile/types'

export function ProfileIdCard({ card }: { card: ProfileCard }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const isStudent = card.role === 'student'
  const pct = card.progressPct ?? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((card.xp / card.xpForNext) * 100)))

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20, rotateX: 6 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, rotateX: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
      whileHover={{ y: -4 }}
      className="clip-hud relative w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden border border-line-aqua bg-gradient-to-br from-surface to-bg shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(159,232,255,0.10)]"
    >
      {/* голо-блик */}
      <div
        aria-hidden
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0"
        style={{
          background:
            'linear-gradient(110deg, transparent 20%, rgba(159,232,255,0.10) 40%, rgba(190,239,141,0.10) 55%, transparent 75%)',
          backgroundSize: '200% 100%',
          animation: 'protocol-holo 4.5s linear infinite',
        }}
      />
      {/* скан-линия */}
      <div
        aria-hidden
        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 h-10"
        style={{
          background: 'linear-gradient(rgba(159,232,255,0.14), transparent)',
          animation: 'protocol-scan 3.6s linear infinite',
        }}
      />

      <div className="relative flex gap-4 p-5">
        <div className="shrink-0 self-start border border-line-strong">
          {card.photoUrl ? (
            <img src={card.photoUrl} alt={t('profile.avatar_alt')} className="h-24 w-24 object-cover" />
          ) : (
            <RoleAvatar seed={card.id} rank={card.rank} role={card.role} size={96} />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span className="label-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim">CALLSIGN</span>
          <div className="truncate font-display text-2xl font-bold text-ink">{card.callsign}</div>
          <div className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted">
            {card.institutionShort} · {card.institutionName}
          </div>

          {isStudent ? (
            <>
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-lime">LVL {card.level}</span>
                <div className="h-1.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden bg-surface-2">
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ width: 0 }}
                    animate={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                    transition={{ duration: 1.4, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
                    className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-aqua to-lime"
                  />
                </div>
                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">{card.xp.toLocaleString('ru-RU')} XP</span>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">
                <span>{t('profile.hours', { count: Math.round(card.hours ?? 0) })}</span>
                {card.groupCode && <span className="border border-line-strong px-1.5 py-0.5 text-aqua">{card.groupCode}</span>}
                {card.avgGrade != null && card.avgGrade > 0 && (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    {t('profile.gpa')}
                    <span className={`inline-flex h-5 min-w-[2.5rem] items-center justify-center border px-1 text-xs ${gradeTone(Math.round(card.avgGrade))}`}>
                      {card.avgGrade.toFixed(2)}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Coin size={18} />
                <span className="font-display text-lg text-[#ffd24a]">{card.coins ?? 0}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{t('profile.coins')}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-3 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted">
              <span className="border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 text-aqua">{t('auth.role_' + card.role)}</span>
              {card.role === 'teacher' && (
                <span className="ml-2 text-ink-dim">{t('profile.students_advanced', { count: card.teacherStudents ?? 0 })}</span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative flex items-center justify-between border-t border-line px-5 py-2.5 font-mono">
        <span className="text-[10px] text-ink-dim">ID · {card.id}</span>
        <span className="jp text-xs text-ink-dim">認証済</span>
        <span className="text-[10px] text-aqua">◆ RANK {card.rank}</span>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/ProfileMiniCard.tsx =====
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { RoleAvatar } from './RoleAvatar'
import type { Role } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import type { Rank } from '@/entities/profile/ranks'

interface Props {
  id: string
  callsign: string
  role: Role
  rank: Rank
  institutionShort?: string
  level?: number
  xp?: number
  progressPct?: number
  groupCode?: string
}

/** Компактная карточка профиля для СДО: минимум информации. */
export function ProfileMiniCard({ id, callsign, role, rank, institutionShort, level, xp, progressPct, groupCode }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const isStudent = role === 'student'

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
      className="clip-hud relative w-full max-w-sm overflow-hidden border border-line-aqua bg-gradient-to-br from-surface to-bg"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 p-4">
        <div className="shrink-0 border border-line-strong">
          <RoleAvatar seed={id} rank={rank} role={role} size={60} />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span className="label-mono text-[9px] text-ink-dim">CALLSIGN</span>
          <div className="truncate font-display text-lg font-bold text-ink">{callsign}</div>
          <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 font-mono text-[10px]">
            <span className="border border-line-strong px-1.5 py-0.5 text-aqua">{t('auth.role_' + role)}</span>
            <span className="text-aqua">◆ {rank}</span>
            {isStudent && groupCode && <span className="border border-line-strong px-1.5 py-0.5 text-lime">{groupCode}</span>}
            {institutionShort && <span className="text-ink-dim">{institutionShort}</span>}
          </div>
          {isStudent && (
            <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-lime">LVL {level ?? 1}</span>
              <div className="h-1.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden bg-surface-2">
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ width: 0 }}
                  animate={{ width: `${progressPct ?? 0}%` }}
                  transition={{ duration: 1.2, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
                  className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-aqua to-lime"
                />
              </div>
              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim">{(xp ?? 0).toLocaleString('ru-RU')} XP</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/RankShowcase.tsx =====
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { GenerativeAvatar } from './GenerativeAvatar'
import { RANKS, RANK_STYLES, type Rank } from '@/entities/profile/ranks'
import { staggerContainer, fadeInUp } from '@/shared/lib/motion'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

/** Витрина: аватар на каждый ранг F→SSS. `current` подсвечивается рамкой. */
export function RankShowcase({ seed = 'PROTOCOL', current }: { seed?: string; current?: Rank }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  return (
    <motion.ul
      variants={staggerContainer}
      initial="hidden"
      whileInView="visible"
      viewport={{ once: true, margin: '-60px' }}
      className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-px border border-line bg-line sm:grid-cols-5 lg:grid-cols-9"
    >
      {RANKS.map((rank) => {
        const isCurrent = rank === current
        return (
          <motion.li
            key={rank}
            variants={fadeInUp}
            className={cn(
              'relative flex flex-col items-center gap-2 bg-surface p-4 transition-colors',
              isCurrent && 'z-10 bg-surface-2 ring-2 ring-inset ring-aqua',
            )}
          >
            {isCurrent && (
              <span className="absolute right-2 top-2 font-mono text-[8px] uppercase tracking-widest text-aqua">
                {t('profile.you')}
              </span>
            )}
            <GenerativeAvatar seed={seed} rank={rank} size={72} />
            <span className={cn('font-display text-lg font-bold', isCurrent ? 'text-aqua' : 'text-ink')}>{rank}</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{RANK_STYLES[rank].motto}</span>
          </motion.li>
        )
      })}
    </motion.ul>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/RoleAvatar.tsx =====
import { GraduationCap, Shield, Crown } from 'lucide-react'
import { GenerativeAvatar } from './GenerativeAvatar'
import type { Rank } from '@/entities/profile/ranks'
import type { Role } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

const BADGE = {
  teacher: { Icon: GraduationCap, cls: 'border-line-lime text-lime' },
  admin: { Icon: Shield, cls: 'border-line-aqua text-aqua' },
  superadmin: { Icon: Crown, cls: 'border-danger text-danger shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(255,59,87,0.55)]' },
} as const

/** Аватар с ролевой рамкой и значком: сразу видно преподавателя, админа, супер-админа. */
export function RoleAvatar({
  seed,
  rank,
  role,
  size = 96,
  className,
}: {
  seed: string
  rank: Rank
  role: Role
  size?: number
  className?: string
}) {
  const badge = role !== 'student' ? BADGE[role] : null
  const iconSize = Math.max(9, Math.round(size * 0.2))
  const ring =
    role === 'superadmin'
      ? 'ring-1 ring-danger/70'
      : role === 'admin'
        ? 'ring-1 ring-aqua/50'
        : role === 'teacher'
          ? 'ring-1 ring-lime/50'
          : ''

  return (
    <span className={cn('relative inline-block leading-none', className)} style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
      <GenerativeAvatar seed={seed} rank={rank} size={size} className={ring} />
      {badge && (
        <span
          className={cn('absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 flex items-center justify-center border bg-bg', badge.cls)}
          style={{ width: Math.round(size * 0.4), height: Math.round(size * 0.4) }}
        >
          <badge.Icon size={iconSize} />
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/profile/useProfileStats.ts =====
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { fetchTeacherStats } from '@/entities/profile/stats'
import { fetchStats } from '@/entities/admin/service'

export function useTeacherStats(teacherId?: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['profile', 'teacher-stats', teacherId ?? ''],
    queryFn: () => fetchTeacherStats(teacherId as string),
    enabled: enabled && !!teacherId,
  })
}

export function useAdminScopeStats(institution?: string, enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['profile', 'admin-stats', institution ?? 'all'],
    queryFn: () => fetchStats(institution),
    enabled,
  })
}


===== FILE: src/features/progress/achievements.ts =====
import { Sparkles, Zap, Star, GraduationCap, Clock, FileCheck2, Coins, CalendarCheck, Crown, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import type { Rank } from '@/entities/profile/ranks'

export interface AchStats {
  xp: number
  level: number
  hours: number
  coins: number
  avgGrade: number
  quizzesPassed: number
  attendancePct: number // -1 если нет данных
  rank: Rank
}

export interface Achievement {
  id: string
  icon: LucideIcon
  done: (s: AchStats) => boolean
}

const RANK_ORDER: Rank[] = ['F', 'E', 'D', 'C', 'B', 'A', 'S', 'SS', 'SSS', 'SSS+']
function rankAtLeast(r: Rank, min: Rank): boolean {
  return RANK_ORDER.indexOf(r) >= RANK_ORDER.indexOf(min)
}

/** Достижения. Названия/описания — в i18n (ach.<id>_title / ach.<id>_desc). */
export const ACHIEVEMENTS: Achievement[] = [
  { id: 'first_steps', icon: Sparkles, done: (s) => s.xp > 0 },
  { id: 'level5', icon: Zap, done: (s) => s.level >= 5 },
  { id: 'level10', icon: Star, done: (s) => s.level >= 10 },
  { id: 'scholar', icon: GraduationCap, done: (s) => s.avgGrade >= 4.5 },
  { id: 'marathon', icon: Clock, done: (s) => s.hours >= 50 },
  { id: 'quiz_master', icon: FileCheck2, done: (s) => s.quizzesPassed >= 5 },
  { id: 'rich', icon: Coins, done: (s) => s.coins >= 100 },
  { id: 'attendance', icon: CalendarCheck, done: (s) => s.attendancePct === 100 },
  { id: 'rank_a', icon: Crown, done: (s) => rankAtLeast(s.rank, 'A') },
]


===== FILE: src/features/progress/passed.ts =====
import type { Course, Lesson } from '@/entities/course/types'
import type { LessonMarks } from '@/entities/submission/service'
import { courseLessons } from '@/entities/course/lib'
import { earnedXp } from '@/shared/lib/grade'

/**
 * Урок пройден студентом, если:
 *  - это задание/чекпоинт (submission) — оценка ≥ 3;
 *  - это лекция (без сдачи) — преподаватель открыл её (open).
 * Самоотметки у студента нет: прохождение определяет преподаватель оценкой.
 */
export function isPassed(lesson: Lesson, marks: LessonMarks): boolean {
  const m = marks[lesson.id]
  if (m) return m.grade >= 3 // работа оценена преподавателем — прохождение по оценке (3–5)
  if (!lesson.submission) return !!lesson.open // лекция без сдачи засчитывается при открытии
  return false // задание без проверки — ещё не пройдено
}

/** Заработанный за урок опыт (0, если не пройден). */
export function lessonXp(lesson: Lesson, marks: LessonMarks): number {
  if (!isPassed(lesson, marks)) return 0
  const m = marks[lesson.id]
  if (m) return earnedXp(lesson.xp, { submission: true, grade: m.grade, retry: m.retry })
  return lesson.xp // лекция, засчитанная по открытию
}

export function coursePassedCount(course: Course, marks: LessonMarks): number {
  return courseLessons(course).filter((l) => isPassed(l, marks)).length
}

export function courseProgress(course: Course, marks: LessonMarks): number {
  const ls = courseLessons(course)
  return ls.length ? Math.round((coursePassedCount(course, marks) / ls.length) * 100) : 0
}

/** Первый открытый непройденный урок — точка, с которой студенту продолжать. */
export function resumeLessonId(course: Course, marks: LessonMarks): string | null {
  for (const l of courseLessons(course)) {
    if (l.open && !isPassed(l, marks)) return l.id
  }
  return null
}


===== FILE: src/features/progress/progressStore.ts =====
import { create } from 'zustand'

const KEY = 'protocol-progress'

function readInitial(): Record<string, boolean> {
  if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return {}
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY)
    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, boolean>) : {}
  } catch {
    return {}
  }
}

interface ProgressState {
  completed: Record<string, boolean>
  toggle: (lessonId: string) => void
  isDone: (lessonId: string) => boolean
}

export const useProgressStore = create<ProgressState>((set, get) => ({
  completed: readInitial(),
  toggle: (lessonId) =>
    set((s) => {
      const completed = { ...s.completed, [lessonId]: !s.completed[lessonId] }
      if (!completed[lessonId]) delete completed[lessonId]
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(completed))
      return { completed }
    }),
  isDone: (lessonId) => !!get().completed[lessonId],
}))


===== FILE: src/features/progress/useRank.ts =====
import { useAuthStore, type Role } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useXp } from './useXp'
import { useTeacherPassedStudents } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { rankFromHours, rankFromTeacherStudents } from '@/shared/lib/levels'
import type { Rank } from '@/entities/profile/ranks'

export interface RoleRank {
  rank: Rank
  role: Role
  hours: number // накопленные часы (студент)
  teacherStudents: number // студентов продвинул (преподаватель)
}

/** Ранг с учётом роли: студент — по часам, препод — по числу продвинутых студентов, админ — SSS, супер — SSS+. */
export function useProfileRank(): RoleRank {
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const role: Role = user?.role ?? 'student'
  const xp = useXp()
  const { data: teacherStudents = 0 } = useTeacherPassedStudents(user?.id ?? '', role === 'teacher')

  let rank: Rank
  if (role === 'superadmin') rank = 'SSS+'
  else if (role === 'admin') rank = 'SSS'
  else if (role === 'teacher') rank = rankFromTeacherStudents(teacherStudents)
  else rank = rankFromHours(xp.hours)

  return { rank, role, hours: xp.hours, teacherStudents }
}

  })

export function useSyncLesson() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { courseId: string; studentId: string; lessonId: string; done: boolean }) =>
      api.setLessonDone(v.courseId, v.studentId, v.lessonId, v.done),
    onSuccess: (_d, v) => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['server-progress', v.courseId, v.studentId] })
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['enrollments', v.courseId] })
    },
  })
}


===== FILE: src/features/progress/useXp.ts =====
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { usePublishedCourses } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useMyEnrollments } from '@/features/enrollment/useEnrollment'
import { useMyLessonMarks } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { useMyBoardXp } from '@/features/board/useBoard'
import { useMyQuizXp } from '@/features/quiz/useQuiz'
import { courseLessons } from '@/entities/course/lib'
import { isPassed, lessonXp } from './passed'
import { levelFromXp, rankFromLevel } from '@/shared/lib/levels'

export interface XpState {
  xp: number
  level: number
  inLevel: number
  forNext: number
  rank: ReturnType<typeof rankFromLevel>
  completedCount: number
  hours: number
}

/**
 * Прогресс студента: опыт и часы по пройденным урокам (оценка ≥ 3 для заданий,
 * открытие для лекций) в рамках записанных курсов. Опыт масштабируется от оценки.
 */
export function useXp(): XpState {
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const isStudent = user?.role === 'student'
  const { data: courses = [] } = usePublishedCourses()
  const { data: enrollments = [] } = useMyEnrollments(user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)
  const { data: marks = {} } = useMyLessonMarks(user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)
  const { data: boardXp = 0 } = useMyBoardXp(user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)
  const { data: quizXp = 0 } = useMyQuizXp(user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)

  return useMemo(() => {
    const enrolled = new Set(enrollments.map((e) => e.courseId))
    let xp = 0
    let hours = 0
    let completedCount = 0
    for (const course of courses) {
      if (!enrolled.has(course.id)) continue
      for (const lesson of courseLessons(course)) {
        if (isPassed(lesson, marks)) {
          xp += lessonXp(lesson, marks)
          hours += lesson.durationHours
          completedCount++
        }
      }
    }
    xp += boardXp // опыт за принятые задания с доски
    xp += quizXp // опыт за пройденные тесты
    const { level, inLevel, forNext } = levelFromXp(xp)
    return { xp, level, inLevel, forNext, rank: rankFromLevel(level), completedCount, hours }
  }, [courses, enrollments, marks, boardXp, quizXp])
}

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'

export function useQuizMutations() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const inval = () => void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['quiz'] })
  return {
    create: useMutation({ mutationFn: (v: api.NewQuiz) => api.createQuiz(v), onSuccess: inval }),
    update: useMutation({ mutationFn: (v: { id: string; patch: Partial<api.NewQuiz> }) => api.updateQuiz(v.id, v.patch), onSuccess: inval }),
    remove: useMutation({ mutationFn: (id: string) => api.deleteQuiz(id), onSuccess: inval }),
    addQuestion: useMutation({ mutationFn: (v: api.NewQuestion) => api.addQuestion(v), onSuccess: inval }),
    updateQuestion: useMutation({ mutationFn: (v: { id: string; patch: Partial<api.NewQuestion> }) => api.updateQuestion(v.id, v.patch), onSuccess: inval }),
    removeQuestion: useMutation({ mutationFn: (id: string) => api.deleteQuestion(id), onSuccess: inval }),
    submit: useMutation({ mutationFn: (v: { quizId: string; studentId: string; answers: Record<string, unknown> }) => api.submitAttempt(v.quizId, v.studentId, v.answers), onSuccess: inval }),
  }
}

===== FILE: src/features/submission/FilePreview.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { FileText, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Download } from 'lucide-react'

const IMG = ['png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'gif', 'webp', 'svg', 'bmp', 'avif']
const CODE = [
  'txt', 'md', 'markdown', 'js', 'jsx', 'ts', 'tsx', 'py', 'html', 'htm', 'css', 'scss',
  'json', 'csv', 'tsv', 'java', 'c', 'cpp', 'h', 'cs', 'go', 'rs', 'php', 'sql', 'xml',
  'yml', 'yaml', 'sh', 'rb', 'kt', 'swift', 'vue', 'log',
]

function ext(name: string): string {
  const m = name.split('.').pop()
  return (m || '').toLowerCase()
}

/** Инлайн-просмотр сданного файла: изображение, PDF или код/текст — без скачивания. */
export function FilePreview({ url, name }: { url: string; name: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [text, setText] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [err, setErr] = useState(false)
  const e = ext(name)
  const kind = IMG.includes(e) ? 'image' : e === 'pdf' ? 'pdf' : CODE.includes(e) ? 'code' : 'other'

  async function toggle() {
    const next = !open
    setOpen(next)
    if (next && kind === 'code' && text === null) {
      try {
        const r = await fetch(url)
        setText(await r.text())
      } catch {
        setErr(true)
      }
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="border border-line-strong">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-surface-2 px-3 py-2">
        <button onClick={toggle} className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
          {open ? <ChevronDown size={14} className="shrink-0" /> : <ChevronRight size={14} className="shrink-0" />}
          <FileText size={14} className="shrink-0 text-ink-muted" />
          <span className="truncate">{name}</span>
        </button>
        <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="shrink-0 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua" title={t('review.download')}>
          <Download size={14} />
        </a>
      </div>
      {open && (
        <div className="border-t border-line bg-bg p-2">
          {kind === 'image' && <img src={url} alt={name} className="mx-auto max-h-[70vh] w-auto border border-line" />}
          {kind === 'pdf' && <iframe src={url} title={name} className="h-[70vh] w-full border border-line bg-white" />}
          {kind === 'code' &&
            (err ? (
              <p className="p-3 text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('review.cannot_preview')}</p>
            ) : text === null ? (
              <p className="p-3 font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">…</p>
            ) : (
              <pre className="max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto p-3 font-mono text-xs leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">
                <code>{text}</code>
              </pre>
            ))}
          {kind === 'other' && <p className="p-3 text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('review.cannot_preview')}</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/submission/SubmitWork.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Upload, Paperclip, CheckCircle2, Clock, FileText } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { gradeTone } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import { validateFiles, ACCEPT_ATTR, MAX_FILES, MAX_FILE_MB } from '@/shared/lib/upload'
import { useMySubmission, useSubmitWork } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'

interface Props {
  courseId: string
  lessonId: string
  studentId: string
}

export function SubmitWork({ courseId, lessonId, studentId }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: submission } = useMySubmission(lessonId, studentId, true)
  const submit = useSubmitWork()

  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false)
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([])
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  function onPickFiles(picked: File[]) {
    const { ok, error } = validateFiles(picked)
    setFiles(ok)
    setFileError(error)
  }

  const showForm = editing || !submission
  const canSend = text.trim().length > 0 || files.length > 0

  function send() {
    if (!canSend) return
    submit.mutate(
      { id: submission?.id, courseId, lessonId, studentId, text: text.trim(), files },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setEditing(false)
          setText('')
          setFiles([])
          setFileError(null)
        },
      },
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="clip-hud border border-line-lime bg-surface p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>// {t('submit.title')}</Label>
        {submission && (
          <span
            className={
              'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase ' +
              (submission.status === 'graded' ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-aqua text-aqua')
            }
          >
            {submission.status === 'graded' ? <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> : <Clock size={12} />}
            {submission.status === 'graded' ? t('submit.status_graded') : t('submit.status_submitted')}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {submission && !editing && (
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4">
          {submission.text && (
            <div>
              <div className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{t('submit.your_answer')}</div>
              <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm text-ink-soft">{submission.text}</p>
            </div>
          )}
          {submission.fileUrls.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{t('submit.your_files')}</div>
              <ul className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1">
                {submission.fileUrls.map((url, i) => (
                  <li key={url}>
                    <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-aqua hover:underline">
                      <FileText size={14} /> {submission.files[i]}
                    </a>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {submission.status === 'graded' && (
            <div className="border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted">{t('submit.grade')}</span>
                <span className={`flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center border font-display text-xl ${gradeTone(submission.grade)}`}>{submission.grade}</span>
              </div>
              {submission.feedback && (
                <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm text-ink-soft">
                  <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{t('submit.feedback')}: </span>
                  {submission.feedback}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
          <div>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setEditing(true)}>{t('submit.resend')}</Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {showForm && (
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('submit.desc')}</p>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label>{t('submit.answer')}</Label>
            <textarea
              value={text}
              onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t('submit.answer_ph')}
              rows={5}
              className="w-full resize-y border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 p-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Label>{t('submit.files')}</Label>
            <label className={`flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 border border-dashed bg-surface-2 px-3 py-3 text-sm transition-colors hover:border-aqua hover:text-ink ${fileError ? 'border-danger/60 text-danger' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted'}`}>
              <Paperclip size={15} className="shrink-0" />
              <span className="truncate">{files.length > 0 ? files.map((f) => f.name).join(', ') : t('submit.files_hint')}</span>
              <input
                type="file"
                multiple
                accept={ACCEPT_ATTR}
                className="hidden"
                onChange={(e) => onPickFiles(Array.from(e.target.files ?? []))}
              />
            </label>
            {fileError ? (
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-danger">{fileError}</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{t('submit.limits', { count: MAX_FILES, mb: MAX_FILE_MB })}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Button onClick={send} disabled={!canSend || submit.isPending}>
              <Upload size={15} /> {submit.isPending ? t('submit.sending') : t('submit.send')}
            </Button>
            {!canSend && <span className="font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">{t('submit.empty')}</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/submission/useSubmissions.ts =====
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import * as api from '@/entities/submission/service'

export function useMySubmission(lessonId: string, studentId: string, enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['submission', lessonId, studentId],
    queryFn: () => api.fetchMySubmission(lessonId, studentId),
    enabled: enabled && !!lessonId && !!studentId,
  })
}

export function useSubmitWork() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: api.SubmitInput) => api.submitWork(input),
    onSuccess: (_d, v) => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['submission', v.lessonId, v.studentId] })
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['submissions', v.courseId] })
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-submissions', v.courseId, v.studentId] })
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-marks', v.studentId] })
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-grades', v.studentId] })
    },
  })
}

export function useGradeSubmission(courseId: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { id: string; grade: number; feedback: string }) => api.gradeSubmission(v.id, v.grade, v.feedback),
    onSuccess: () => {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['submissions', courseId] })
    },
  })
}
