===== FILE: src/features/trainers/CodeTrainer.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Check, X, Play, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { ProgressRing } from '@/shared/ui/ProgressRing'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

interface CodeTest { args: unknown[]; expected: unknown }
interface CodeTask {
  id: string
  title: string
  prompt: string
  fn: string
  starter: string
  tests: CodeTest[]
}

const TASKS: CodeTask[] = [
  {
    id: 'sum',
    title: 'Сумма массива',
    fn: 'sumArray',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте функцию sumArray(nums), возвращающую сумму чисел массива. Для пустого массива — 0.',
    starter: 'function sumArray(nums) {\n  // ваш код\n  return 0\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: [[1, 2, 3]], expected: 6 },
      { args: [[]], expected: 0 },
      { args: [[-5, 5, 10]], expected: 10 },
      { args: [[100]], expected: 100 },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'reverse',
    title: 'Переворот строки',
    fn: 'reverseStr',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте функцию reverseStr(s), возвращающую строку, записанную в обратном порядке.',
    starter: 'function reverseStr(s) {\n  // ваш код\n  return s\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: ['protocol'], expected: 'locotorp' },
      { args: [''], expected: '' },
      { args: ['a'], expected: 'a' },
      { args: ['UX/UI'], expected: 'IU/XU' },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'palindrome',
    title: 'Палиндром',
    fn: 'isPalindrome',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте функцию isPalindrome(s): true, если строка читается одинаково в обе стороны, иначе false.',
    starter: 'function isPalindrome(s) {\n  // ваш код\n  return false\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: ['level'], expected: true },
      { args: ['radar'], expected: true },
      { args: ['protocol'], expected: false },
      { args: [''], expected: true },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'fizzbuzz',
    title: 'FizzBuzz',
    fn: 'fizzbuzz',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте fizzbuzz(n): массив 1..n, где кратные 3 → "Fizz", кратные 5 → "Buzz", кратные 15 → "FizzBuzz", остальные — само число.',
    starter: 'function fizzbuzz(n) {\n  // ваш код\n  return []\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: [5], expected: [1, 2, 'Fizz', 4, 'Buzz'] },
      { args: [3], expected: [1, 2, 'Fizz'] },
      { args: [15], expected: [1, 2, 'Fizz', 4, 'Buzz', 'Fizz', 7, 8, 'Fizz', 'Buzz', 11, 'Fizz', 13, 14, 'FizzBuzz'] },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'max',
    title: 'Максимум массива',
    fn: 'maxOf',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте maxOf(nums) — наибольшее число массива. Для пустого массива верните null.',
    starter: 'function maxOf(nums) {\n  // ваш код\n  return null\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: [[3, 7, 2, 9, 4]], expected: 9 },
      { args: [[-5, -1, -8]], expected: -1 },
      { args: [[42]], expected: 42 },
      { args: [[]], expected: null },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'vowels',
    title: 'Счётчик гласных',
    fn: 'countVowels',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте countVowels(s) — количество гласных латиницы (a, e, i, o, u) в строке, без учёта регистра.',
    starter: 'function countVowels(s) {\n  // ваш код\n  return 0\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: ['protocol'], expected: 3 },
      { args: ['SKY'], expected: 0 },
      { args: ['Education'], expected: 5 },
      { args: [''], expected: 0 },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'factorial',
    title: 'Факториал',
    fn: 'factorial',
    prompt: 'Реализуйте factorial(n) — произведение чисел от 1 до n. factorial(0) = 1.',
    starter: 'function factorial(n) {\n  // ваш код\n  return 1\n}',
    tests: [
      { args: [0], expected: 1 },
      { args: [1], expected: 1 },
      { args: [5], expected: 120 },
      { args: [7], expected: 5040 },
    ],
  },
]

interface Result { ok: boolean; got: string; error?: string }
const show = (v: unknown) => {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(v)
  } catch {
    return String(v)
  }
}
const eq = (a: unknown, b: unknown) => show(a) === show(b)

export function CodeTrainer() {
  const [taskId, setTaskId] = useState(TASKS[0].id)
  const task = useMemo(() => TASKS.find((t) => t.id === taskId) ?? TASKS[0], [taskId])
  const [code, setCode] = useState(task.starter)
  const [results, setResults] = useState<Result[] | null>(null)
  const [compileError, setCompileError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  function selectTask(id: string) {
    const next = TASKS.find((t) => t.id === id) ?? TASKS[0]
    setTaskId(id)
    setCode(next.starter)
    setResults(null)
    setCompileError(null)
  }

  function runTests() {
    setCompileError(null)
    let fn: ((...a: unknown[]) => unknown) | null = null
    try {
      // песочница: код выполняется в изолированной функции в браузере студента
      const factory = new Function(`${code}\n;return typeof ${task.fn} === 'function' ? ${task.fn} : null;`)
      fn = factory() as ((...a: unknown[]) => unknown) | null
    } catch (e) {
      setResults(null)
      setCompileError((e as Error).message)
      return
    }
    if (!fn) {
      setResults(null)
      setCompileError(`Функция ${task.fn}(...) не найдена — проверьте название.`)
      return
    }
    const res: Result[] = task.tests.map((t) => {
      try {
        const got = fn!(...t.args)
        return { ok: eq(got, t.expected), got: show(got) }
      } catch (e) {
        return { ok: false, got: '—', error: (e as Error).message }
      }
    })
    setResults(res)
  }

  const passedCount = results ? results.filter((r) => r.ok).length : 0
  const score = results ? Math.round((passedCount / task.tests.length) * 100) : 0

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {TASKS.map((tk) => (
          <button
            key={tk.id}
            onClick={() => selectTask(tk.id)}
            className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn', tk.id === taskId ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
          >
            {tk.title}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="clip-hud mt-5 border border-line bg-surface p-5">
        <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-lime">задание</span>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-soft">{task.prompt}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_280px]">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col border border-line-strong bg-[#05070a]">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line px-3 py-2">
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-muted">editor.js</span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => { setCode(task.starter); setResults(null); setCompileError(null) }} className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
                <RotateCcw size={12} /> сброс
              </button>
              <button onClick={runTests} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-lime px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-lime transition-colors hover:bg-lime/10 clip-btn">
                <Play size={12} /> запустить
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <textarea
            value={code}
            onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
            spellCheck={false}
            rows={14}
            className="w-full resize-y bg-transparent p-3 font-mono text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft focus:outline-none"
          />
          {compileError && <div className="border-t border-line px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-danger">Ошибка: {compileError}</div>}
        </div>

        <div className="clip-hud flex flex-col items-center border border-line bg-surface p-5">
          <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">тесты</span>
          <div className="mt-3"><ProgressRing value={score} size={92} /></div>
          <ul className="mt-4 w-full space-y-2">
            {task.tests.map((t, i) => {
              const r = results?.[i]
              return (
                <li key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[16px_minmax(0,1fr)] items-start gap-2 text-xs">
                  <span className={cn('mt-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center border', !r ? 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim' : r.ok ? 'border-lime text-lime' : 'border-danger text-danger')}>
                    {r ? r.ok ? <Check size={11} /> : <X size={11} /> : ''}
                  </span>
                  <span className="min-w-0 break-words font-mono text-ink-muted">
                    {task.fn}({t.args.map(show).join(', ').replace(/^\[|\]$/g, '')}) → {show(t.expected)}
                    {r && !r.ok && <span className="text-danger"> · получено {r.error ? '⚠ ' + r.error : r.got}</span>}
                  </span>
                </li>
              )
            })}
          </ul>
          {score === 100 && <div className="mt-4 w-full border border-line-lime bg-surface-2 py-2 text-center font-mono text-xs text-lime">все тесты пройдены</div>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/trainers/ConsoleTrainer.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { Check, X, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { ProgressRing } from '@/shared/ui/ProgressRing'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

// ---- виртуальная файловая система -----------------------------------------
interface FSNode {
  type: 'dir' | 'file'
  mode: string
  content?: string
  children?: Record<string, FSNode>
}
const mkdir = (children: Record<string, FSNode> = {}): FSNode => ({ type: 'dir', mode: 'rwxr-xr-x', children })
const mkfile = (content = '', mode = 'rw-r--r--'): FSNode => ({ type: 'file', mode, content })

/** Узел по абсолютному пути ('/a/b'); null если нет. */
function getNode(root: FSNode, path: string): FSNode | null {
  const parts = path.split('/').filter(Boolean)
  let cur: FSNode = root
  for (const p of parts) {
    if (cur.type !== 'dir' || !cur.children || !cur.children[p]) return null
    cur = cur.children[p]
  }
  return cur
}

function normalize(cwd: string[], arg: string): string[] {
  const base = arg.startsWith('/') ? [] : [...cwd]
  for (const seg of arg.split('/')) {
    if (!seg || seg === '.') continue
    if (seg === '..') base.pop()
    else base.push(seg)
  }
  return base
}

// ---- задания ---------------------------------------------------------------
interface TaskCheck {
  label: string
  test: (root: FSNode) => boolean
}
interface ConsoleTask {
  id: string
  title: string
  prompt: string
  init: () => FSNode
  checks: TaskCheck[]
}

const TASKS: ConsoleTask[] = [
  {
    id: 'files',
    title: 'Файлы и каталоги',
    prompt: 'Создайте каталог project, внутри — файл index.html и файл README.md с текстом Protocol. Затем перейдите в project.',
    init: () => mkdir({ home: mkdir() }),
    checks: [
      { label: 'Каталог project создан', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/project')?.type === 'dir' },
      { label: 'Есть project/index.html', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/project/index.html')?.type === 'file' },
      { label: 'README.md содержит «Protocol»', test: (r) => !!getNode(r, '/project/README.md')?.content?.includes('Protocol') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'perms',
    title: 'Права доступа',
    prompt: 'Создайте файл deploy.sh и сделайте его исполняемым командой chmod +x.',
    init: () => mkdir({ home: mkdir() }),
    checks: [
      { label: 'Файл deploy.sh создан', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/deploy.sh')?.type === 'file' },
      { label: 'deploy.sh исполняемый (+x)', test: (r) => !!getNode(r, '/deploy.sh')?.mode.includes('x') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'cleanup',
    title: 'Навигация и очистка',
    prompt: 'В каталоге logs уже есть файл old.log — удалите его командой rm.',
    init: () => mkdir({ logs: mkdir({ 'old.log': mkfile('...'), 'app.log': mkfile('ok') }) }),
    checks: [
      { label: 'logs/old.log удалён', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/logs/old.log') === null },
      { label: 'logs/app.log не тронут', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/logs/app.log')?.type === 'file' },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'nested',
    title: 'Вложенная структура',
    prompt: 'Создайте каталог src, внутри него — каталог app, а в нём файл main.ts с текстом start. Подсказка: сначала mkdir src, затем mkdir src/app.',
    init: () => mkdir({ home: mkdir() }),
    checks: [
      { label: 'Каталог src создан', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/src')?.type === 'dir' },
      { label: 'Каталог src/app создан', test: (r) => getNode(r, '/src/app')?.type === 'dir' },
      { label: 'src/app/main.ts содержит start', test: (r) => !!getNode(r, '/src/app/main.ts')?.content?.includes('start') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'append',
    title: 'Дозапись в файл',
    prompt: 'В файле notes.txt уже есть строка «one». Допишите к нему строку «two» через echo two >> notes.txt (двойная стрелка — дозапись).',
    init: () => mkdir({ 'notes.txt': mkfile('one') }),
    checks: [
      { label: 'notes.txt содержит one', test: (r) => !!getNode(r, '/notes.txt')?.content?.includes('one') },
      { label: 'notes.txt содержит two', test: (r) => !!getNode(r, '/notes.txt')?.content?.includes('two') },
    ],
  },
]

interface Line { text: string; kind: 'in' | 'out' | 'err' }

export function ConsoleTrainer() {
  const [taskId, setTaskId] = useState(TASKS[0].id)
  const task = useMemo(() => TASKS.find((t) => t.id === taskId) ?? TASKS[0], [taskId])

  const rootRef = useRef<FSNode>(task.init())
  const [cwd, setCwd] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<Line[]>([])
  const [input, setInput] = useState('')
  const [tick, setTick] = useState(0)
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  function reset(next = task) {
    rootRef.current = next.init()
    setCwd([])
    setLines([{ text: next.prompt, kind: 'out' }])
    setInput('')
    setTick((t) => t + 1)
  }
  // сброс при смене задания
  useEffect(() => {
    reset(task)

  }, [taskId])
  useEffect(() => {
    scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ top: scrollRef.current.scrollHeight })
  }, [lines])

  const prompt = '/' + cwd.join('/')

  function print(out: string[], kind: Line['kind'] = 'out') {
    if (out.length) setLines((l) => [...l, ...out.map((text) => ({ text, kind }))])
  }

  function run(raw: string) {
    const root = rootRef.current
    const cmd = raw.trim()
    setLines((l) => [...l, { text: `${prompt} $ ${cmd}`, kind: 'in' }])
    if (!cmd) return

    // разбор редиректа echo ... > file
    const redir = cmd.match(/^echo\s+(.*?)\s*(>>?)\s*(\S+)$/)
    if (redir) {
      const text = redir[1].replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
      const append = redir[2] === '>>'
      const path = normalize(cwd, redir[3])
      const name = path.pop()
      if (!name) return print(['echo: неверный путь'], 'err')
      const parent = getNode(root, '/' + path.join('/'))
      if (!parent || parent.type !== 'dir') return print(['echo: каталог не найден'], 'err')
      const ex = parent.children![name]
      parent.children![name] = mkfile(append && ex?.content ? ex.content + '\n' + text : text)
      return setTick((t) => t + 1)
    }

    const [name, ...args] = cmd.split(/\s+/)
    switch (name) {
      case 'help':
        return print(['команды: pwd, ls, cd, mkdir, touch, cat, echo > файл, rm [-r], chmod +x, whoami, clear'])
      case 'clear':
        return setLines([])
      case 'whoami':
        return print(['student'])
      case 'pwd':
        return print(['/' + cwd.join('/')])
      case 'ls': {
        const target = args[0] ? getNode(root, '/' + normalize(cwd, args[0]).join('/')) : getNode(root, '/' + cwd.join('/'))
        if (!target) return print([`ls: нет такого пути`], 'err')
        if (target.type === 'file') return print([args[0]])
        return print([Object.entries(target.children ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => (v.type === 'dir' ? k + '/' : k)).join('  ') || '(пусто)'])
      }
      case 'cd': {
        const path = normalize(cwd, args[0] ?? '')
        const node = getNode(root, '/' + path.join('/'))
        if (!node || node.type !== 'dir') return print([`cd: нет каталога: ${args[0] ?? ''}`], 'err')
        return setCwd(path)
      }
      case 'mkdir':
      case 'touch': {
        if (!args[0]) return print([`${name}: не указано имя`], 'err')
        const path = normalize(cwd, args[0])
        const leaf = path.pop()!
        const parent = getNode(root, '/' + path.join('/'))
        if (!parent || parent.type !== 'dir') return print([`${name}: путь не найден`], 'err')
        parent.children![leaf] = name === 'mkdir' ? mkdir() : mkfile('')
        return setTick((t) => t + 1)
      }
      case 'cat': {
        const node = args[0] ? getNode(root, '/' + normalize(cwd, args[0]).join('/')) : null
        if (!node || node.type !== 'file') return print([`cat: файл не найден`], 'err')
        return print([node.content ?? ''])
      }
      case 'rm': {
        const recursive = args.includes('-r') || args.includes('-rf')
        const target = args.find((a) => !a.startsWith('-'))
        if (!target) return print(['rm: не указан файл'], 'err')
        const path = normalize(cwd, target)
        const leaf = path.pop()!
        const parent = getNode(root, '/' + path.join('/'))
        const node = parent?.children?.[leaf]
        if (!parent || !node) return print([`rm: нет файла: ${target}`], 'err')
        if (node.type === 'dir' && !recursive) return print([`rm: ${target} — каталог, нужен флаг -r`], 'err')
        delete parent.children![leaf]
        return setTick((t) => t + 1)
      }
      case 'chmod': {
        const flag = args[0]
        const target = args[1]
        const node = target ? getNode(root, '/' + normalize(cwd, target).join('/')) : null
        if (!node) return print(['chmod: файл не найден'], 'err')
        if (flag === '+x' || flag === '755' || flag === '+rwx') node.mode = 'rwxr-xr-x'
        else if (flag === '-x' || flag === '644') node.mode = 'rw-r--r--'
        else return print(['chmod: поддерживаются +x / -x / 755 / 644'], 'err')
        return setTick((t) => t + 1)
      }
      default:
        return print([`${name}: команда не найдена (введите help)`], 'err')
    }
  }

  const passed = task.checks.map((c) => c.test(rootRef.current))
  void tick // пересчёт проверок при каждом изменении FS
  const score = Math.round((passed.filter(Boolean).length / task.checks.length) * 100)

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {TASKS.map((tk) => (
          <button
            key={tk.id}
            onClick={() => setTaskId(tk.id)}
            className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn', tk.id === taskId ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
          >
            {tk.title}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="clip-hud mt-5 border border-line bg-surface p-5">
        <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-lime">задание</span>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-soft">{task.prompt}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_260px]">
        {/* терминал */}
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col border border-line-strong bg-[#05070a]">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line px-3 py-2">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-muted">
              <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-danger/70" />
              <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-warning/70" />
              <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-lime/70" />
              <span className="ml-2">student@protocol</span>
            </span>
            <button onClick={() => reset()} className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
              <RotateCcw size={12} /> сброс
            </button>
          </div>
          <div ref={scrollRef} className="h-72 overflow-auto p-3 font-mono text-[13px] leading-relaxed">
            {lines.map((l, i) => (
              <div key={i} className={cn('whitespace-pre-wrap break-words', l.kind === 'in' ? 'text-aqua' : l.kind === 'err' ? 'text-danger' : 'text-ink-soft')}>
                {l.text}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <form
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault()
              run(input)
              setInput('')
            }}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-line px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[13px]"
          >
            <span className="shrink-0 text-lime">{prompt} $</span>
            <input
              value={input}
              onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
              autoFocus
              spellCheck={false}
              className="w-full bg-transparent text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:outline-none"
              placeholder="введите команду…"
            />
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* авто-оценка */}
        <div className="clip-hud flex flex-col items-center border border-line bg-surface p-5">
          <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">авто-оценка</span>
          <div className="mt-3"><ProgressRing value={score} size={92} /></div>
          <ul className="mt-4 w-full space-y-2">
            {task.checks.map((c, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[16px_minmax(0,1fr)] items-start gap-2 text-xs">
                <span className={cn('mt-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center border', passed[i] ? 'border-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim')}>
                  {passed[i] ? <Check size={11} /> : <X size={11} />}
                </span>
                <span className={passed[i] ? 'text-ink-soft' : 'text-ink-muted'}>{c.label}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          {score === 100 && <div className="mt-4 w-full border border-line-lime bg-surface-2 py-2 text-center font-mono text-xs text-lime">задание выполнено</div>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/features/trainers/NetworkTrainer.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Check, X, RotateCcw, Laptop, Server, ShieldCheck, Router } from 'lucide-react'
import { ProgressRing } from '@/shared/ui/ProgressRing'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

type NodeType = 'client' | 'server' | 'coordinator' | 'gateway'
interface NetNode { id: string; label: string; type: NodeType; x: number; y: number }
interface Link { a: string; b: string; enc: boolean }

const ICONS: Record<NodeType, typeof Laptop> = {
  client: Laptop,
  server: Server,
  coordinator: ShieldCheck,
  gateway: Router,
}

interface NetCheck {
  label: string
  test: (links: Link[]) => boolean
}
interface NetTask {
  id: string
  title: string
  prompt: string
  nodes: NetNode[]
  checks: NetCheck[]
}

function has(links: Link[], a: string, b: string): Link | undefined {
  return links.find((l) => (l.a === a && l.b === b) || (l.a === b && l.b === a))
}
const linked = (links: Link[], a: string, b: string) => !!has(links, a, b)
const secured = (links: Link[], a: string, b: string) => !!has(links, a, b)?.enc

const TASKS: NetTask[] = [
  {
    id: 'basic',
    title: 'Базовая топология',
    prompt: 'Подключите оба узла к координатору ViPNet: клиент → координатор и сервер → координатор. Соединение: кликните один узел, затем другой.',
    nodes: [
      { id: 'client', label: 'Клиент', type: 'client', x: 90, y: 60 },
      { id: 'coord', label: 'Координатор', type: 'coordinator', x: 300, y: 170 },
      { id: 'server', label: 'Сервер', type: 'server', x: 510, y: 60 },
    ],
    checks: [
      { label: 'Клиент подключён к координатору', test: (l) => linked(l, 'client', 'coord') },
      { label: 'Сервер подключён к координатору', test: (l) => linked(l, 'server', 'coord') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'secure',
    title: 'Защищённый канал',
    prompt: 'Постройте сеть ViPNet: клиент и сервер подключены к координатору, а между клиентом и сервером — защищённый канал. Кликните по линии, чтобы включить шифрование (канал станет зелёным).',
    nodes: [
      { id: 'client', label: 'Клиент', type: 'client', x: 90, y: 60 },
      { id: 'coord', label: 'Координатор', type: 'coordinator', x: 300, y: 175 },
      { id: 'server', label: 'Сервер', type: 'server', x: 510, y: 60 },
    ],
    checks: [
      { label: 'Клиент ↔ координатор', test: (l) => linked(l, 'client', 'coord') },
      { label: 'Сервер ↔ координатор', test: (l) => linked(l, 'server', 'coord') },
      { label: 'Защищённый канал клиент ↔ сервер', test: (l) => secured(l, 'client', 'server') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'segment',
    title: 'Сегмент со шлюзом',
    prompt: 'Локальный клиент выходит наружу через шлюз. Соедините: клиент → шлюз, шлюз → координатор, координатор → удалённый сервер. Канал шлюз ↔ удалённый сервер сделайте защищённым.',
    nodes: [
      { id: 'lan', label: 'ЛВС-клиент', type: 'client', x: 80, y: 60 },
      { id: 'gw', label: 'Шлюз', type: 'gateway', x: 230, y: 180 },
      { id: 'coord', label: 'Координатор', type: 'coordinator', x: 400, y: 60 },
      { id: 'remote', label: 'Удал. сервер', type: 'server', x: 540, y: 185 },
    ],
    checks: [
      { label: 'Клиент ↔ шлюз', test: (l) => linked(l, 'lan', 'gw') },
      { label: 'Шлюз ↔ координатор', test: (l) => linked(l, 'gw', 'coord') },
      { label: 'Координатор ↔ удал. сервер', test: (l) => linked(l, 'coord', 'remote') },
      { label: 'Защищённый канал шлюз ↔ удал. сервер', test: (l) => secured(l, 'gw', 'remote') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'branch',
    title: 'Два филиала',
    prompt: 'Объедините два филиала через координатор ViPNet защищёнными каналами: офис-1 ↔ координатор и офис-2 ↔ координатор, оба канала зашифрованы.',
    nodes: [
      { id: 'o1', label: 'Офис-1', type: 'gateway', x: 90, y: 60 },
      { id: 'coord', label: 'Координатор', type: 'coordinator', x: 300, y: 175 },
      { id: 'o2', label: 'Офис-2', type: 'gateway', x: 510, y: 60 },
    ],
    checks: [
      { label: 'Защищённый канал офис-1 ↔ координатор', test: (l) => secured(l, 'o1', 'coord') },
      { label: 'Защищённый канал офис-2 ↔ координатор', test: (l) => secured(l, 'o2', 'coord') },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'redundant',
    title: 'Резервный координатор',
    prompt: 'Отказоустойчивость: подключите клиента к двум координаторам сразу (основной и резервный), оба канала — защищённые.',
    nodes: [
      { id: 'client', label: 'Клиент', type: 'client', x: 90, y: 120 },
      { id: 'main', label: 'Осн. координатор', type: 'coordinator', x: 420, y: 55 },
      { id: 'backup', label: 'Рез. координатор', type: 'coordinator', x: 420, y: 190 },
    ],
    checks: [
      { label: 'Защищённый канал к основному', test: (l) => secured(l, 'client', 'main') },
      { label: 'Защищённый канал к резервному', test: (l) => secured(l, 'client', 'backup') },
    ],
  },
]

export function NetworkTrainer() {
  const [taskId, setTaskId] = useState(TASKS[0].id)
  const task = useMemo(() => TASKS.find((t) => t.id === taskId) ?? TASKS[0], [taskId])
  const [links, setLinks] = useState<Link[]>([])
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<string | null>(null)

  function reset() {
    setLinks([])
    setSel(null)
  }
  function pickNode(id: string) {
    if (sel === null) return setSel(id)
    if (sel === id) return setSel(null)
    setLinks((cur) => {
      const ex = has(cur, sel, id)
      if (ex) return cur.filter((l) => l !== ex) // повторный выбор пары — убрать связь
      return [...cur, { a: sel, b: id, enc: false }]
    })
    setSel(null)
  }
  function toggleEnc(a: string, b: string) {
    setLinks((cur) => cur.map((l) => (l.a === a && l.b === b ? { ...l, enc: !l.enc } : l)))
  }

  const nodeById = (id: string) => task.nodes.find((n) => n.id === id)!
  const passed = task.checks.map((c) => c.test(links))
  const score = Math.round((passed.filter(Boolean).length / task.checks.length) * 100)

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {TASKS.map((tk) => (
          <button
            key={tk.id}
            onClick={() => { setTaskId(tk.id); reset() }}
            className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn', tk.id === taskId ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
          >
            {tk.title}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="clip-hud mt-5 border border-line bg-surface p-5">
        <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-lime">задание</span>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-soft">{task.prompt}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_260px]">
        <div className="border border-line-strong bg-[#05070a]">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line px-3 py-2">
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-muted">схема сети · кликните узлы, затем линию для шифрования</span>
            <button onClick={reset} className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
              <RotateCcw size={12} /> сброс
            </button>
          </div>
          <svg viewBox="0 0 600 240" className="w-full">
            {/* связи */}
            {links.map((l, i) => {
              const na = nodeById(l.a)
              const nb = nodeById(l.b)
              return (
                <g key={i} className="cursor-pointer" onClick={() => toggleEnc(l.a, l.b)}>
                  <line x1={na.x} y1={na.y} x2={nb.x} y2={nb.y} stroke="transparent" strokeWidth={16} />
                  <line
                    x1={na.x} y1={na.y} x2={nb.x} y2={nb.y}
                    stroke={l.enc ? 'var(--color-lime)' : 'var(--color-line-strong)'}
                    strokeWidth={l.enc ? 2.5 : 1.5}
                    strokeDasharray={l.enc ? '0' : '5 4'}
                  />
                  {l.enc && (
                    <text x={(na.x + nb.x) / 2} y={(na.y + nb.y) / 2 - 6} textAnchor="middle" className="fill-lime font-mono" style={{ fontSize: 9 }}>
                      🔒 VPN
                    </text>
                  )}
                </g>
              )
            })}
            {/* узлы */}
            {task.nodes.map((n) => {
              const active = sel === n.id
              return (
                <g key={n.id} className="cursor-pointer" onClick={() => pickNode(n.id)}>
                  <rect
                    x={n.x - 30} y={n.y - 22} width={60} height={44} rx={4}
                    fill="var(--color-surface)"
                    stroke={active ? 'var(--color-aqua)' : 'var(--color-line-strong)'}
                    strokeWidth={active ? 2 : 1}
                  />
                  <text x={n.x} y={n.y + 30} textAnchor="middle" className="fill-current font-mono" style={{ fontSize: 9, fill: 'var(--color-ink-muted)' }}>
                    {n.label}
                  </text>
                </g>
              )
            })}
          </svg>
          {/* иконки узлов поверх SVG невозможны как lucide — рисуем легенду */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 border-t border-line px-3 py-2">
            {task.nodes.map((n) => {
              const Icon = ICONS[n.type]
              return (
                <span key={n.id} className={cn('flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px]', sel === n.id ? 'text-aqua' : 'text-ink-muted')}>
                  <Icon size={13} /> {n.label}
                </span>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="clip-hud flex flex-col items-center border border-line bg-surface p-5">
          <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">авто-оценка</span>
          <div className="mt-3"><ProgressRing value={score} size={92} /></div>
          <ul className="mt-4 w-full space-y-2">
            {task.checks.map((c, i) => (
              <li key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[16px_minmax(0,1fr)] items-start gap-2 text-xs">
                <span className={cn('mt-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center border', passed[i] ? 'border-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim')}>
                  {passed[i] ? <Check size={11} /> : <X size={11} />}
                </span>
                <span className={passed[i] ? 'text-ink-soft' : 'text-ink-muted'}>{c.label}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          {score === 100 && <div className="mt-4 w-full border border-line-lime bg-surface-2 py-2 text-center font-mono text-xs text-lime">сеть настроена верно</div>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

  icon: LucideIcon

===== FILE: src/features/trainers/UxUiTrainer.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react'
import { PlaygroundRunner } from '@/features/playground/PlaygroundRunner'
import { EXERCISES, makeCustomExercise, type CustomSpec } from '@/features/playground/exercises'
import { BLOCK_DEFS, type LeafKind } from '@/features/playground/blocks'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

// блоки, доступные как критерии в конструкторе задания
const CRITERIA_BLOCKS: LeafKind[] = ['nav', 'hero', 'heading', 'text', 'button', 'image', 'card', 'input', 'footer', 'badge', 'list', 'avatar', 'stat']

export function UxUiTrainer() {
  const [exId, setExId] = useState(EXERCISES[0].id)
  const isCustom = exId === 'custom'

  // конструктор своего задания
  const [task, setTask] = useState('')
  const [required, setRequired] = useState<Partial<Record<LeafKind, number>>>({})
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState<'desktop' | 'mobile'>('desktop')
  const [firstNav, setFirstNav] = useState(false)
  const [lastFooter, setLastFooter] = useState(false)
  const [singleCTA, setSingleCTA] = useState(false)

  const bump = (k: LeafKind, d: number) =>
    setRequired((r) => {
      const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(6, (r[k] ?? 0) + d))
      const next = { ...r }
      if (v === 0) delete next[k]
      else next[k] = v
      return next
    })

  const exercise = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isCustom) return EXERCISES.find((e) => e.id === exId) ?? EXERCISES[0]
    const spec: CustomSpec = { task, required, frame, firstNav, lastFooter, singleCTA }
    return makeCustomExercise(spec)
  }, [exId, isCustom, task, required, frame, firstNav, lastFooter, singleCTA])

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {EXERCISES.map((e) => (
          <button
            key={e.id}
            onClick={() => setExId(e.id)}
            className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn', e.id === exId ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
          >
            {e.title}
          </button>
        ))}
        <button
          onClick={() => setExId('custom')}
          className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn', isCustom ? 'border-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-lime text-lime/70 hover:text-lime')}
        >
          + Своё задание
        </button>
      </div>

      {isCustom ? (
        <div className="clip-hud mt-5 border border-line-lime bg-surface p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-lime">конструктор задания</span>
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {(['desktop', 'mobile'] as const).map((f) => (
                <button key={f} onClick={() => setFrame(f)} className={cn('border px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase clip-btn', frame === f ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim')}>
                  {f === 'desktop' ? 'десктоп' : 'мобайл'}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <textarea
            value={task}
            onChange={(e) => setTask(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Опишите задание для студентов: что собрать, на что обратить внимание…"
            rows={2}
            className="mt-3 w-full resize-y border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 p-2 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
          />

          <div className="mt-4 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">Обязательные блоки (авто-проверка)</div>
          <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
            {CRITERIA_BLOCKS.map((k) => {
              const n = required[k] ?? 0
              return (
                <div key={k} className={cn('flex items-center justify-between border px-2 py-1.5', n > 0 ? 'border-line-lime' : 'border-line-strong')}>
                  <span className="truncate text-xs text-ink-soft">{BLOCK_DEFS[k].label}</span>
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <button onClick={() => bump(k, -1)} className="text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-aqua" aria-label="меньше"><Minus size={12} /></button>
                    <span className={cn('w-4 text-center font-mono text-xs', n > 0 ? 'text-lime' : 'text-ink-dim')}>{n}</span>
                    <button onClick={() => bump(k, 1)} className="text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-aqua" aria-label="больше"><Plus size={12} /></button>
                  </span>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {[
              { on: firstNav, set: setFirstNav, label: 'Навигация первой' },
              { on: lastFooter, set: setLastFooter, label: 'Футер последним' },
              { on: singleCTA, set: setSingleCTA, label: 'Один CTA' },
            ].map((o) => (
              <button
                key={o.label}
                onClick={() => o.set(!o.on)}
                className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn', o.on ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
              >
                {o.on ? '✓ ' : ''}{o.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="clip-hud mt-5 border border-line bg-surface p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-lime">задание</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase text-ink-dim">{exercise.frame}</span>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-soft">{exercise.task}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-6">
        <PlaygroundRunner key={exId + (isCustom ? frame : '')} exercise={exercise} />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/main.tsx =====
import '@/shared/lib/noApiCache'
import { StrictMode } from 'react'
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
// Шрифты (самохостинг через @fontsource, без сторонних CDN)
import '@fontsource-variable/inter'
import '@fontsource-variable/unbounded'
import '@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/400.css'
import '@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/500.css'
import '@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/700.css'
import { App } from './app/App'
import './styles/index.css'

// Автовосстановление при устаревшем кэше: если после деплоя старый index.html
// ссылается на удалённый JS-чанк — вместо ошибки один раз перезагружаем страницу
// (получаем свежий index и актуальные файлы).
window.addEventListener('vite:preloadError', () => {
  if (!sessionStorage.getItem('protocol-reloaded')) {
    sessionStorage.setItem('protocol-reloaded', '1')
    window.location.reload()
  }
})
// после успешной загрузки снимаем флаг, чтобы восстановление работало и в будущем
window.addEventListener('load', () => {
  setTimeout(() => sessionStorage.removeItem('protocol-reloaded'), 4000)
})

const rootEl = document.getElementById('root')
if (!rootEl) throw new Error('Root element #root not found')

createRoot(rootEl).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
)


===== FILE: src/pages/app/AdminPage.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { LayoutGrid, Users, School, ScrollText, BookOpen, Users2, FlaskConical } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { AdminOverview } from '@/features/admin/AdminOverview'
import { AdminUsers } from '@/features/admin/AdminUsers'
import { AdminGroups } from '@/features/admin/AdminGroups'
import { AdminCourses } from '@/features/admin/AdminCourses'
import { AdminInstitutions } from '@/features/admin/AdminInstitutions'
import { AdminAudit } from '@/features/admin/AdminAudit'
import { AdminQA } from '@/features/admin/AdminQA'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

type Tab = 'overview' | 'users' | 'groups' | 'courses' | 'institutions' | 'audit' | 'qa'

export function AdminPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('overview')
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const isSuper = user?.role === 'superadmin'
  // админ колледжа видит только свой колледж; супер-админ — всё
  const scope = isSuper ? undefined : user?.institutionId

  const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string; icon: typeof Users }[] = [
    { id: 'overview', label: t('admin.tab_overview'), icon: LayoutGrid },
    { id: 'users', label: t('admin.tab_users'), icon: Users },
    { id: 'groups', label: t('admin.tab_groups'), icon: Users2 },
    { id: 'courses', label: t('admin.tab_courses'), icon: BookOpen },
    ...(isSuper ? [{ id: 'institutions' as Tab, label: t('admin.tab_institutions'), icon: School }] : []),
    ...(isSuper ? [{ id: 'audit' as Tab, label: t('admin.tab_audit'), icon: ScrollText }] : []),
    ...(isSuper ? [{ id: 'qa' as Tab, label: t('admin.tab_qa'), icon: FlaskConical }] : []),
  ]

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('admin.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{isSuper ? t('admin.scope_all') : user?.institutionShort}</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('admin.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>

      <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-line pb-3">
        {tabs.map((tb) => {
          const Icon = tb.icon
          const active = tab === tb.id
          return (
            <button
              key={tb.id}
              onClick={() => setTab(tb.id)}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-2 border px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors clip-btn',
                active ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink',
              )}
            >
              <Icon size={14} /> {tb.label}
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8">
        {tab === 'overview' && <AdminOverview scope={scope} />}
        {tab === 'users' && <AdminUsers scope={scope} isSuper={isSuper} />}
        {tab === 'groups' && <AdminGroups scope={scope} />}
        {tab === 'courses' && <AdminCourses scope={scope} />}
        {tab === 'institutions' && isSuper && <AdminInstitutions />}
        {tab === 'audit' && isSuper && <AdminAudit />}
        {tab === 'qa' && isSuper && <AdminQA />}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/AppProfilePage.tsx =====
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Clock, Star, Users, BookOpen, Layers, Award, TrendingUp, CalendarCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { restoreSession } from '@/features/auth/authService'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Coin } from '@/features/currency/Coin'
import { ProfileMiniCard } from '@/features/profile/ProfileMiniCard'
import { RankShowcase } from '@/features/profile/RankShowcase'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useXp } from '@/features/progress/useXp'
import { useProfileRank } from '@/features/progress/useRank'
import { useMyGrades } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { useTeacherStats, useAdminScopeStats } from '@/features/profile/useProfileStats'
import { useMyAttendance } from '@/features/attendance/useAttendance'
import { average } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

function StatBlock({ icon, label, value, accent }: { icon: ReactNode; label: string; value: ReactNode; accent?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className={`clip-hud flex items-center gap-3 border bg-surface p-4 ${accent ? 'border-line-lime' : 'border-line'}`}>
      <span className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border ${accent ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-aqua'}`}>
        {icon}
      </span>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <div className="truncate font-display text-2xl text-ink">{value}</div>
        <div className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{label}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function AppProfilePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const xp = useXp()
  const rankInfo = useProfileRank()
  const { data: grades = [] } = useMyGrades(user?.id ?? '', user?.role === 'student')
  const isTeacher = user?.role === 'teacher'
  const isAdmin = user?.role === 'admin' || user?.role === 'superadmin'
  const scope = user?.role === 'superadmin' ? undefined : user?.institutionId
  const { data: teacherStats } = useTeacherStats(user?.id, isTeacher)
  const { data: adminStats } = useAdminScopeStats(scope, isAdmin)
  const { data: myAttendance = [] } = useMyAttendance(user?.id, user?.role === 'student')

  // обновляем запись пользователя (монеты/группа/опыт меняются на сервере)
  useEffect(() => { void restoreSession() }, [])

  if (!user) return null

  const avg = average(grades)
  const attPct = myAttendance.length
    ? Math.round((myAttendance.filter((a) => a.status !== 'absent').length / myAttendance.length) * 100)
    : -1
  const progressPct = xp.forNext > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((xp.inLevel / xp.forNext) * 100))) : 0
  const coins = <span className="text-[#ffd24a]">{user.coins ?? 0}</span>

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('profile.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{t('profile.idcard')}</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('profile.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>

      <div className="mt-8 grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,24rem)_1fr]">
        <ProfileMiniCard
          id={user.id}
          callsign={user.callsign}
          role={user.role}
          rank={rankInfo.rank}
          institutionShort={user.role === 'superadmin' ? 'Вся система' : user.institutionShort}
          level={xp.level}
          xp={xp.xp}
          progressPct={progressPct}
          groupCode={user.groupCode}
        />

        {/* инфо-блоки по роли */}
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {user.role === 'student' && (
            <>
              <StatBlock icon={<Clock size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_hours')} value={Math.round(rankInfo.hours ?? 0)} />
              <StatBlock icon={<Star size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_gpa')} value={avg > 0 ? avg.toFixed(2) : '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<CalendarCheck size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_attendance')} value={attPct < 0 ? '—' : attPct + '%'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<TrendingUp size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_xp')} value={xp.xp.toLocaleString('ru-RU')} />
              <StatBlock icon={<Coin size={18} />} label={t('profile.coins')} value={coins} accent />
            </>
          )}
          {isTeacher && (
            <>
              <StatBlock icon={<Users size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_students')} value={teacherStats?.students ?? '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<BookOpen size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_courses')} value={teacherStats?.courses ?? '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<Clock size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_course_hours')} value={teacherStats?.hours ?? '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<Coin size={18} />} label={t('profile.coins')} value={coins} accent />
            </>
          )}
          {isAdmin && (
            <>
              <StatBlock icon={<Users size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_users')} value={adminStats?.users ?? '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<BookOpen size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_courses')} value={adminStats?.courses ?? '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<Layers size={18} />} label={t('profile.stat_groups_scope')} value={adminStats?.students ?? '—'} />
              <StatBlock icon={<Coin size={18} />} label={t('profile.coins')} value={coins} accent />
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* полный кабинет — на портале */}
      <div className="clip-hud mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3 text-sm text-ink-muted">
        <Award size={15} className="text-aqua" />
        {t('profile.cabinet_hint')}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-14">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <Label accent>{t('profile.ranks_kicker')}</Label>
          <Label>F → SSS</Label>
        </div>
        <h2 className="mt-3 text-2xl sm:text-3xl">{t('profile.ranks_title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h2>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-lg text-ink-soft">{t('profile.ranks_subtitle')}</p>
        <div className="mt-8">
          <RankShowcase seed={user.id} current={rankInfo.rank} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/BoardPage.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { ClipboardList, Plus, Trash2, Check, X, Send, Clock, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Ban, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { Coin } from '@/features/currency/Coin'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useAdminGroups } from '@/features/admin/useAdmin'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'
import {
  useStudentTasks,
  useMyCompletions,
  useAuthorTasks,
  useAuthorCompletions,
  useBoardMutations,
} from '@/features/board/useBoard'
import type { CompletionStatus } from '@/entities/board/service'
import type { BoardTask } from '@/entities/board/service'

const field = 'h-11 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

function RewardBadges({ coins, xp }: { coins: number; xp: number }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 font-mono text-xs">
      {coins > 0 && (
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-300"><Coin size={16} /> +{coins}</span>
      )}
      {xp > 0 && <span className="text-lime">+{xp} {t('board.xp')}</span>}
    </div>
  )
}

// ---- студент ----------------------------------------------------------------
type StudentTab = 'all' | 'active' | 'done' | 'declined'

function TaskCard({ task, actions, badge }: { task: BoardTask; actions?: ReactNode; badge?: ReactNode }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  return (
    <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-aqua">
              {task.target === 'all' ? t('board.for_all') : task.groupCode || t('board.for_group')}
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{task.authorName}</span>
          </div>
          <h3 className="mt-2 text-lg text-ink">{task.title}</h3>
          {task.description && <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-ink-soft">{task.description}</p>}
          <div className="mt-3"><RewardBadges coins={task.rewardCoins} xp={task.rewardXp} /></div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col items-end gap-2">
          {badge}
          {actions}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function StudentBoard() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const { data: tasks = [], isLoading } = useStudentTasks(user?.groupId)
  const { data: mine = [] } = useMyCompletions(user?.id)
  const m = useBoardMutations()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<StudentTab>('all')

  const statusByTask = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, CompletionStatus>()
    for (const c of mine) map.set(c.taskId, c.status)
    return map
  }, [mine])

  const buckets = useMemo(() => {
    const all: BoardTask[] = []
    const active: BoardTask[] = []
    const done: BoardTask[] = []
    const declined: BoardTask[] = []
    for (const task of tasks) {
      const st = statusByTask.get(task.id)
      if (st === 'submitted') active.push(task)
      else if (st === 'approved') done.push(task)
      else if (st === 'declined') declined.push(task)
      else all.push(task) // не тронуто или отклонено преподавателем (можно переделать)
    }
    return { all, active, done, declined }
  }, [tasks, statusByTask])

  const sid = user?.id
  const submit = (id: string) => sid && m.submit.mutate({ taskId: id, studentId: sid })
  const decline = (id: string) => sid && m.decline.mutate({ taskId: id, studentId: sid })
  const reset = (id: string) => sid && m.reset.mutate({ taskId: id, studentId: sid })

  const TABS: { id: StudentTab; label: string; count: number }[] = [
    { id: 'all', label: t('board.tab_all'), count: buckets.all.length },
    { id: 'active', label: t('board.tab_active'), count: buckets.active.length },
    { id: 'done', label: t('board.tab_done'), count: buckets.done.length },
    { id: 'declined', label: t('board.tab_declined'), count: buckets.declined.length },
  ]

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>

  const list = buckets[tab]

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {TABS.map((tb) => (
          <button
            key={tb.id}
            onClick={() => setTab(tb.id)}
            className={cn('flex items-center gap-2 border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors clip-btn', tab === tb.id ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
          >
            {tb.label} <span className="text-ink-dim">{tb.count}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {list.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-10 text-center text-ink-muted">
          <ClipboardList size={22} className="text-ink-dim" /> {t('board.empty_' + tab)}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {list.map((task) => {
            const st = statusByTask.get(task.id)
            if (tab === 'all') {
              return (
                <TaskCard
                  key={task.id}
                  task={task}
                  badge={st === 'rejected' ? <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-danger"><XCircle size={15} /> {t('board.st_rejected')}</span> : undefined}
                  actions={
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
                      <Button size="sm" onClick={() => submit(task.id)} disabled={m.submit.isPending}>
                        <Send size={14} /> {st === 'rejected' ? t('board.retry') : t('board.submit')}
                      </Button>
                      <button onClick={() => decline(task.id)} disabled={m.decline.isPending} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger">
                        <Ban size={13} /> {t('board.decline')}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  }
                />
              )
            }
            if (tab === 'active') {
              return <TaskCard key={task.id} task={task} badge={<span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted"><Clock size={15} /> {t('board.st_submitted')}</span>} />
            }
            if (tab === 'done') {
              return <TaskCard key={task.id} task={task} badge={<span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-lime"><CheckCircle2 size={15} /> {t('board.st_approved')}</span>} />
            }
            return (
              <TaskCard
                key={task.id}
                task={task}
                badge={<span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted"><Ban size={15} /> {t('board.st_declined')}</span>}
                actions={
                  <button onClick={() => reset(task.id)} disabled={m.reset.isPending} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-aqua transition-colors hover:underline">
                    <RotateCcw size={13} /> {t('board.return')}
                  </button>
                }
              />
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ---- преподаватель / админ --------------------------------------------------
function TeacherBoard() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const scope = user?.role === 'superadmin' ? undefined : user?.institutionId
  const { data: groups = [] } = useAdminGroups(scope)
  const { data: tasks = [] } = useAuthorTasks(user?.id)
  const { data: completions = [] } = useAuthorCompletions(user?.id)
  const m = useBoardMutations()
  const confirm = useConfirm()

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('')
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('')
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<'all' | 'group'>('all')
  const [groupId, setGroupId] = useState('')
  const [coins, setCoins] = useState(10)
  const [xp, setXp] = useState(20)

  const pending = completions.filter((c) => c.status === 'submitted')

  function publish() {
    if (!title.trim() || !user) return
    if (target === 'group' && !groupId) return
    m.publish.mutate(
      {
        authorId: user.id,
        title: title.trim(),
        description: description.trim(),
        target,
        groupId: target === 'group' ? groupId : undefined,
        rewardCoins: coins,
        rewardXp: xp,
        institutionId: user.institutionId || undefined,
      },
      { onSuccess: () => { setTitle(''); setDescription(''); setCoins(10); setXp(20) } },
    )
  }
  async function remove(id: string, name: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${name} — ${t('board.delete_confirm')}`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true })
    if (ok) m.remove.mutate(id)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      {/* публикация */}
      <div className="border border-line-lime bg-surface p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Plus size={15} className="text-lime" /><Label>{t('board.publish_title')}</Label></div>
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 sm:col-span-2">
            <Label>{t('board.f_title')}</Label>
            <input className={field} value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder={t('board.f_title_ph')} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 sm:col-span-2">
            <Label>{t('board.f_desc')}</Label>
            <textarea className={field + ' h-auto py-2'} rows={3} value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('board.f_target')}</Label>
            <select className={field} value={target} onChange={(e) => setTarget(e.target.value as 'all' | 'group')}>
              <option value="all">{t('board.for_all')}</option>
              <option value="group">{t('board.for_group')}</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          {target === 'group' && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Label>{t('board.f_group')}</Label>
              <select className={field} value={groupId} onChange={(e) => setGroupId(e.target.value)}>
                <option value="">—</option>
                {groups.map((g) => <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>{g.code}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('board.f_coins')}</Label>
            <input type="number" min={0} className={field} value={coins} onChange={(e) => setCoins(Number(e.target.value) || 0)} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('board.f_xp')}</Label>
            <input type="number" min={0} className={field} value={xp} onChange={(e) => setXp(Number(e.target.value) || 0)} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4">
          <Button onClick={publish} disabled={m.publish.isPending || !title.trim() || (target === 'group' && !groupId)}>
            <Plus size={15} /> {t('board.publish')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* заявки на проверку */}
      <div>
        <Label accent>{t('board.review_title')}</Label>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
          {pending.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-6 text-center text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('board.review_empty')}</div>
          ) : (
            pending.map((c) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
                <div className="min-w-40 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-sm text-ink">{c.studentName}</div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{c.taskTitle}</div>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => m.review.mutate({ id: c.id, status: 'approved' })} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-lime px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-lime transition-colors hover:bg-lime/10 clip-btn">
                  <Check size={14} /> {t('board.approve')}
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => m.review.mutate({ id: c.id, status: 'rejected' })} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-danger transition-colors hover:bg-danger/10 clip-btn">
                  <X size={14} /> {t('board.reject')}
                </button>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* мои задания */}
      <div>
        <Label accent>{t('board.my_tasks')}</Label>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
          {tasks.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-6 text-center text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('board.empty_teacher')}</div>
          ) : (
            tasks.map((task) => (
              <div key={task.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-3">
                <span className="border border-line-strong px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-aqua">
                  {task.target === 'all' ? t('board.for_all') : task.groupCode || t('board.for_group')}
                </span>
                <div className="min-w-40 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-sm text-ink">{task.title}</div>
                  <div className="mt-1"><RewardBadges coins={task.rewardCoins} xp={task.rewardXp} /></div>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => remove(task.id, task.title)} className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger" aria-label={t('common.delete')}>
                  <Trash2 size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function BoardPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const role = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.role ?? 'student')
  const isTeacher = role === 'teacher' || role === 'admin' || role === 'superadmin'

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <Label accent>{t('board.kicker')}</Label>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('board.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
      <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink-muted">{isTeacher ? t('board.lead_teacher') : t('board.lead_student')}</p>
      <div className="mt-8">{isTeacher ? <TeacherBoard /> : <StudentBoard />}</div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/CourseEditorPage.tsx =====
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { ArrowLeft, Plus, Trash2, FileText, ClipboardCheck, Eye, EyeOff, Paperclip } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { GenerativeAvatar } from '@/features/profile/GenerativeAvatar'
import { useCourse, useCourseMutations } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useCourseEnrollments } from '@/features/enrollment/useEnrollment'
import type { CourseAccent, CourseStatus, LessonType } from '@/entities/course/types'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'

const LESSON_TYPES: LessonType[] = ['lecture', 'practice']
const ACCENTS: CourseAccent[] = ['aqua', 'lime', 'teal']
const CATEGORIES = ['Разработка', 'Программирование', 'Дизайн', 'Аналитика', 'Языки']

const fieldCls =
  'h-11 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

export function CourseEditorPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { id = '' } = useParams()
  const { data: course, isLoading } = useCourse(id)
  const m = useCourseMutations()
  const { data: students = [] } = useCourseEnrollments(id, true)
  const confirm = useConfirm()

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-6 py-20 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
  }
  if (!course) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl">{t('editor.not_found')}</h1>
        <Link to="/app/teach" className="mt-4 inline-block font-mono text-sm text-aqua">{t('editor.to_my')}</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }
  const c = course

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Link to="/app/teach" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
          <ArrowLeft size={14} />{' '}{t('editor.back')}
        </Link>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Link
            to={`/app/teach/${c.id}/submissions`}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:border-aqua hover:text-aqua clip-btn"
          >
            <ClipboardCheck size={14} /> {t('review.open_review')}
          </Link>
          <button
            onClick={() => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { status: (c.status === 'published' ? 'draft' : 'published') as CourseStatus } })}
            className={'border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase transition-colors clip-btn ' + (c.status === 'published' ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-aqua')}
          >
            {c.status === 'published' ? t('editor.status_published') : t('editor.status_draft')}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('editor.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>

      <div className="mt-8 grid items-start gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_320px]">
      <div className="min-w-0">
      <div className="grid gap-5 border border-line bg-surface p-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="sm:col-span-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label>{t('editor.field_title')}</Label>
          <input className={fieldCls} defaultValue={c.title} onBlur={(e) => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { title: e.target.value } })} />
        </div>
        <div className="sm:col-span-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label>{t('editor.field_subtitle')}</Label>
          <input className={fieldCls} defaultValue={c.subtitle} onBlur={(e) => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { subtitle: e.target.value } })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label>{t('editor.field_category')}</Label>
          <select className={fieldCls} defaultValue={c.category} onChange={(e) => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { category: e.target.value } })}>
            {CATEGORIES.map((x) => <option key={x} value={x}>{x}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label>{t('editor.field_accent')}</Label>
          <select className={fieldCls} defaultValue={c.accent} onChange={(e) => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { accent: e.target.value as CourseAccent } })}>
            {ACCENTS.map((a) => <option key={a} value={a}>{a}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label>{t('editor.field_hours')}</Label>
          <input type="number" min={0} className={fieldCls} defaultValue={c.totalHours} onBlur={(e) => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { totalHours: Number(e.target.value) } })} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Label>{t('editor.field_instructor')}</Label>
          <input className={fieldCls} defaultValue={c.instructor} onBlur={(e) => m.updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { instructor: e.target.value } })} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-10 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-2xl">{t('editor.structure')}</h2>
        <button
          onClick={() => m.addSection.mutate({ courseId: c.id, order: c.sections.length })}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-line-strong px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink transition-colors hover:border-aqua clip-btn"
        >
          <Plus size={15} /> {t('editor.section')}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-4">
        {c.sections.length === 0 && (
          <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('editor.no_sections')}</div>
        )}
        {c.sections.map((sec, si) => (
          <div key={sec.id} className="border border-line bg-surface">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-line p-4">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">{String(si + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
              <input
                className="h-9 flex-1 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
                defaultValue={sec.title}
                onBlur={(e) => m.updateSection.mutate({ id: sec.id, title: e.target.value })}
              />
              <button onClick={async () => { const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${sec.title} — удалить раздел? Все уроки внутри будут отвязаны.`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true }); if (ok) m.deleteSection.mutate(sec.id) }} aria-label={t('editor.del_section')} className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger">
                <Trash2 size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-line">
              {sec.lessons.map((l) => (
                <div key={l.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-3">
                  <input
                    className="h-9 min-w-40 flex-1 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
                    defaultValue={l.title}
                    onBlur={(e) => m.updateLesson.mutate({ id: l.id, patch: { title: e.target.value } })}
                  />
                  <select
                    className="h-9 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-2 text-xs text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
                    defaultValue={l.type}
                    onChange={(e) => m.updateLesson.mutate({ id: l.id, patch: { type: e.target.value as LessonType } })}
                  >
                    {LESSON_TYPES.map((lt) => <option key={lt} value={lt}>{t('course.type_' + lt)}</option>)}
                  </select>
                  <input
                    type="number" min={0} step={0.5} aria-label={t('editor.field_hours')}
                    className="h-9 w-16 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-2 text-xs text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
                    defaultValue={l.durationHours}
                    onBlur={(e) => m.updateLesson.mutate({ id: l.id, patch: { durationHours: Number(e.target.value) } })}
                  />
                  <input
                    type="number" min={0} aria-label="XP"
                    className="h-9 w-16 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-2 text-xs text-lime focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
                    defaultValue={l.xp}
                    onBlur={(e) => m.updateLesson.mutate({ id: l.id, patch: { xp: Number(e.target.value) } })}
                  />
                  <button
                    onClick={() => m.updateLesson.mutate({ id: l.id, patch: { submission: !l.submission } })}
                    title={l.submission ? t('editor.submission_on') : t('editor.submission_off')}
                    className={`p-2 transition-colors ${l.submission ? 'text-lime hover:text-lime/70' : 'text-ink-dim hover:text-aqua'}`}
                  >
                    <Paperclip size={15} />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => m.updateLesson.mutate({ id: l.id, patch: { open: !l.open } })}
                    title={l.open ? t('editor.close_lesson') : t('editor.open_lesson')}
                    className={`p-2 transition-colors ${l.open ? 'text-lime hover:text-lime/70' : 'text-ink-dim hover:text-aqua'}`}
                  >
                    {l.open ? <Eye size={15} /> : <EyeOff size={15} />}
                  </button>
                  <Link to={`/app/teach/${c.id}/lessons/${l.id}`} aria-label="Контент урока" className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
                    <FileText size={15} />
                  </Link>
                  <button onClick={async () => { const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${l.title} — удалить урок?`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true }); if (ok) m.deleteLesson.mutate(l.id) }} aria-label={t('editor.del_lesson')} className="p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger">
                    <Trash2 size={15} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
              <button
                onClick={() => m.addLesson.mutate({ courseId: c.id, sectionId: sec.id, order: sec.lessons.length })}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 text-left font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua"
              >
                <Plus size={14} /> {t('editor.add_lesson')}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      </div>

      <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-20">
        <div className="border border-line bg-surface">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line p-4">
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg">{t('teach.students')}</h2>
            <Label>{students.length}</Label>
          </div>
          {students.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('teach.no_students')}</div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-line">
              {students.map((st) => (
                <li key={st.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4">
                  <GenerativeAvatar seed={st.studentId} rank="F" size={38} />
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="block truncate text-sm text-ink">{st.studentCallsign || st.studentName || st.studentId}</span>
                    {st.studentName && st.studentCallsign && <span className="block truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{st.studentName}</span>}
                  </span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-xs text-lime">{st.progressPercent}%</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/CoursePage.tsx =====
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Clock, User, ArrowLeft, Check, Lock, Target, LogOut, PlayCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { ProgressRing } from '@/shared/ui/ProgressRing'
import { Accordion } from '@/shared/ui/Accordion'
import { useCourse } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { courseLessons } from '@/entities/course/lib'
import { isPassed, courseProgress, resumeLessonId } from '@/features/progress/passed'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useMyEnrollment, useEnroll, useUnenroll } from '@/features/enrollment/useEnrollment'
import { useMyCourseSubmissions } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'
import { gradeTone, average } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import type { LessonMarks } from '@/entities/submission/service'
import type { Lesson } from '@/entities/course/types'

function isCheckpoint(l: Lesson) {
  return l.type === 'practice' || l.type === 'assignment'
}

function LessonRow({
  lesson,
  courseId,
  locked,
  passed,
  resume,
  grade,
}: {
  lesson: Lesson
  courseId: string
  locked: boolean
  passed: boolean
  resume: boolean
  grade?: number
}) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()

  const body = (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className={`truncate text-sm ${locked ? 'text-ink-muted' : passed ? 'text-ink-muted line-through' : 'text-ink transition-colors hover:text-aqua'}`}>{lesson.title}</span>
        {isCheckpoint(lesson) && (
          <span className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 border border-line-lime px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-lime">
            <Target size={10} /> {t('course.checkpoint')}
          </span>
        )}
        {resume && (
          <span className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 border border-line-aqua px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-aqua">
            <PlayCircle size={10} /> {t('course.resume')}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">
        {t('course.type_' + lesson.type)} · {lesson.durationHours} {t('common.hours_short')} · +{lesson.xp} XP
      </div>
    </>
  )

  return (
