    <div className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-line px-5 py-3 last:border-b-0 ${resume ? 'bg-aqua/[0.04]' : ''}`}>
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
        <span className={`flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border ${passed ? 'border-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-transparent'}`}>
          <Check size={13} />
        </span>
        {locked ? <div className="min-w-0 opacity-70">{body}</div> : <Link to={`/app/courses/${courseId}/lessons/${lesson.id}`} className="min-w-0">{body}</Link>}
      </div>
      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
        {grade != null && grade > 0 && (
          <span className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center border font-mono text-sm ${gradeTone(grade)}`} title={t('course.grade')}>
            {grade}
          </span>
        )}
        {locked && <Lock size={15} className="text-ink-dim" />}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function CoursePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { id = '' } = useParams()
  const { data: course } = useCourse(id)
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const isStudent = user?.role === 'student'
  const { data: enrollment } = useMyEnrollment(id, user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)
  const enroll = useEnroll()
  const unenroll = useUnenroll()
  const confirm = useConfirm()
  const enrolled = !isStudent || !!enrollment

  async function handleUnenroll() {
    if (!enrollment || !user) return
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('course.unenroll'), message: t('course.unenroll_confirm'), confirmLabel: t('course.unenroll'), danger: true })
    if (ok) unenroll.mutate({ id: enrollment.id, courseId: id, studentId: user.id })
  }

  const { data: mySubs = [] } = useMyCourseSubmissions(id, user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent && enrolled)
  const marks: LessonMarks = {}
  const grades: Record<string, number> = {}
  const gradeValues: number[] = []
  for (const s of mySubs) if (s.status === 'graded') {
    marks[s.lessonId] = { grade: s.grade, retry: s.retry }
    grades[s.lessonId] = s.grade
    gradeValues.push(s.grade)
  }
  const avgGrade = average(gradeValues)

  if (!course) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl">{t('course.not_found')}</h1>
        <Link to="/app/courses" className="mt-4 inline-block font-mono text-sm text-aqua">{t('course.to_catalog')}</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const lessonIds = courseLessons(course).map((l) => l.id)
  const progress = isStudent ? courseProgress(course, marks) : 0
  const resumeId = isStudent && enrolled ? resumeLessonId(course, marks) : null
  const lessonLocked = (l: Lesson) => (isStudent ? !enrolled || !l.open : false)

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <Link to="/app/courses" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
        <ArrowLeft size={14} />{' '}{t('course.back')}
      </Link>

      <div className="clip-hud mt-4 flex flex-col-reverse items-start justify-between gap-6 border border-line-aqua bg-surface p-6 sm:flex-row">
        <div>
          <Label accent>// {course.category}</Label>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{course.title}</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-ink-soft">{course.subtitle}</p>
          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><User size={13} /> {course.instructor}</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Clock size={13} /> {course.totalHours} {t('common.hours_short')}</span>
            <span>{lessonIds.length} {t('course.lessons')}</span>
            {isStudent && avgGrade > 0 && (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                {t('course.avg_grade')}
                <span className={`inline-flex h-6 min-w-[2.75rem] items-center justify-center border px-1.5 text-sm ${gradeTone(Math.round(avgGrade))}`}>
                  {avgGrade.toFixed(2)}
                </span>
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          {isStudent && enrolled && enrollment && (
            <button
              onClick={handleUnenroll}
              disabled={unenroll.isPending}
              className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:border-danger hover:text-danger clip-btn"
            >
              <LogOut size={13} /> {t('course.unenroll')}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {isStudent && <ProgressRing value={progress} size={84} />}
      </div>

      {isStudent && !enrolled && (
        <div className="clip-hud mt-6 border border-line-lime bg-surface p-6">
          <Label accent>{t('course.enroll_kicker')}</Label>
          <h3 className="mt-2 font-display text-xl text-ink">{t('course.enroll_title')}</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 max-w-lg text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('course.enroll_text')}</p>
          <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => user && enroll.mutate({ courseId: id, studentId: user.id })} disabled={enroll.isPending}>
            {enroll.isPending ? t('course.enrolling') : t('course.enroll_btn')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-8 space-y-3">
        {course.sections.map((section) => {
          const done = section.lessons.filter((l) => isPassed(l, marks)).length
          return (
            <Accordion key={section.id} title={section.title} meta={`${done} / ${section.lessons.length}`} defaultOpen>
              {section.lessons.map((lesson) => (
                <LessonRow
                  key={lesson.id}
                  lesson={lesson}
                  courseId={course.id}
                  locked={lessonLocked(lesson)}
                  passed={isPassed(lesson, marks)}
                  resume={lesson.id === resumeId}
                  grade={grades[lesson.id]}
                />
              ))}
            </Accordion>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/CoursesPage.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Search } from 'lucide-react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { CourseCard } from '@/features/courses/CourseCard'
import { usePublishedCourses } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

export function CoursesPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: allCourses = [], isLoading, isError, error } = usePublishedCourses()
  const courses = allCourses.filter((c) => c.status === 'published')
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [cat, setCat] = useState<string>('__all__')

  const categories = useMemo(() => ['__all__', ...Array.from(new Set(courses.map((c) => c.category)))], [courses])
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase()
    return courses.filter(
      (c) =>
        (cat === '__all__' || c.category === cat) &&
        (!q || (c.title + ' ' + c.subtitle).toLowerCase().includes(q)),
    )
  }, [query, cat, courses])

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('courses.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{filtered.length} / {courses.length}</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('courses.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>

      <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 clip-btn sm:w-72">
          <Search size={16} className="text-ink-muted" />
          <input
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={t('courses.search')}
            className="h-11 w-full bg-transparent text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {categories.map((c) => (
            <button
              key={c}
              onClick={() => setCat(c)}
              className={cn(
                'border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs transition-colors clip-btn',
                cat === c ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink',
              )}
            >
              {c === '__all__' ? t('common.all') : c}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-16 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
      ) : isError ? (
        <div className="mt-16 text-center font-mono text-sm text-danger">
          {t('common.error')} — {String((error as Error)?.message ?? error)}
        </div>
      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-16 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.nothing_found')}</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {filtered.map((c) => (
            <CourseCard key={c.id} course={c} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/DashboardPage.tsx =====
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { ProgressRing } from '@/shared/ui/ProgressRing'
import { CourseCard } from '@/features/courses/CourseCard'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useXp } from '@/features/progress/useXp'
import { usePublishedCourses } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useMyEnrollments } from '@/features/enrollment/useEnrollment'
import { useMyLessonMarks } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { courseProgress } from '@/features/progress/passed'
import { staggerContainer, fadeInUp } from '@/shared/lib/motion'
import type { LessonMarks } from '@/entities/submission/service'
import type { Course } from '@/entities/course/types'

function ContinueCard({ courses, marks }: { courses: Course[]; marks: LessonMarks }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { course, progress } = useMemo(() => {
    if (courses.length === 0) return { course: null, progress: 0 }
    const withP = courses.map((c) => ({ c, p: courseProgress(c, marks) }))
    const inProgress = withP.find((x) => x.p > 0 && x.p < 100)
    const pick = inProgress ?? withP[0]
    return { course: pick.c, progress: pick.p }
  }, [courses, marks])

  if (!course) {
    return (
      <div className="clip-hud border border-line bg-surface p-6 text-ink-muted">{t('dashboard.empty')}</div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Link
      to={`/app/courses/${course.id}`}
      className="clip-hud flex flex-col-reverse items-start justify-between gap-5 border border-line-aqua bg-surface p-6 transition-colors hover:border-aqua sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
    >
      <div>
        <Label accent>{t('dashboard.continue')}</Label>
        <h3 className="mt-2 font-display text-2xl text-ink">{course.title}</h3>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-muted">{course.subtitle}</p>
        <span className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-sm text-aqua">
          {t('dashboard.continue_btn')} <ArrowRight size={15} />
        </span>
      </div>
      <ProgressRing value={progress} size={72} />
    </Link>
  )
}

function Stat({ value, label }: { value: string; label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-5">
      <div className="font-mono text-3xl font-bold text-lime">{value}</div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-muted">{label}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function DashboardPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const isStudent = user?.role === 'student'
  const xp = useXp()
  const { data: allCourses = [] } = usePublishedCourses()
  const { data: myEnrollments = [] } = useMyEnrollments(user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)
  const { data: marks = {} } = useMyLessonMarks(user?.id ?? '', !!isStudent)
  const published = allCourses.filter((c) => c.status === 'published')
  // студент видит только курсы, на которые записан; преподаватель/админ — все опубликованные
  const enrolledIds = new Set(myEnrollments.map((e) => e.courseId))
  const courses = isStudent ? published.filter((c) => enrolledIds.has(c.id)) : published

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('dashboard.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{user?.institutionShort}</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">
        {t('dashboard.hello')}, {user?.callsign}<span className="text-aqua">.</span>
      </h1>

      <motion.div variants={staggerContainer} initial="hidden" animate="visible" className="mt-8 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <motion.div variants={fadeInUp} className="lg:col-span-2">
          <ContinueCard courses={courses} marks={marks} />
        </motion.div>
        <motion.div variants={fadeInUp} className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-1">
          <Stat value={String(xp.completedCount)} label={t('dashboard.stat_lessons')} />
          <Stat value={`${xp.xp.toLocaleString('ru-RU')}`} label={t('dashboard.stat_xp')} />
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>

      <div className="mt-14 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-2xl">{t('dashboard.my_courses')}</h2>
        <Link to="/app/courses" className="font-mono text-sm text-aqua hover:text-aqua-bright">
          {t('dashboard.all_catalog')}
        </Link>
      </div>
      {courses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-6 clip-hud border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">
          {t('dashboard.no_enrolled')}{' '}
          <Link to="/app/courses" className="text-aqua hover:underline">{t('dashboard.to_catalog')}</Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {courses.map((c) => (
            <CourseCard key={c.id} course={c} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/JournalPage.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { NotebookPen, Download, GraduationCap, CalendarCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { usePublishedCourses, useCourse } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useCourseEnrollments } from '@/features/enrollment/useEnrollment'
import { useCourseSubmissions } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { useCourseAttendance, useSetAttendance } from '@/features/attendance/useAttendance'
import { courseLessons } from '@/entities/course/lib'
import { gradeTone } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import { buildStyledXls, downloadXls } from '@/shared/lib/xlsExport'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'
import { useToast } from '@/shared/ui/toast'
import type { Lesson } from '@/entities/course/types'
import type { AttendanceStatus } from '@/entities/attendance/service'

const field = 'h-11 w-full max-w-md border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

type Mode = 'grades' | 'attendance'

// цикл отметок посещаемости по клику
const CYCLE: (AttendanceStatus | null)[] = [null, 'present', 'absent', 'late', 'excused']
const ATT_LABEL: Record<AttendanceStatus, string> = { present: 'П', absent: 'Н', late: 'О', excused: 'У' }
const ATT_TONE: Record<AttendanceStatus, string> = {
  present: 'border-line-lime text-lime',
  absent: 'border-danger text-danger',
  late: 'border-amber-500/50 text-amber-300',
  excused: 'border-line-aqua text-aqua',
}

export function JournalPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const isStaffAll = user?.role === 'admin' || user?.role === 'superadmin'

  const { data: allCourses = [] } = usePublishedCourses()
  const courses = useMemo(
    () => (isStaffAll ? allCourses : allCourses.filter((c) => c.ownerId === user?.id)),
    [allCourses, isStaffAll, user?.id],
  )

  const [courseId, setCourseId] = useState('')
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('grades')
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!courseId && courses.length) setCourseId(courses[0].id)
  }, [courses, courseId])

  const { data: course } = useCourse(courseId)
  const { data: enrollments = [] } = useCourseEnrollments(courseId, !!courseId)
  const { data: submissions = [] } = useCourseSubmissions(courseId, !!courseId)
  const { data: attendance = [] } = useCourseAttendance(courseId, !!courseId)
  const setAtt = useSetAttendance(courseId)
  const toast = useToast()
  const [busyCell, setBusyCell] = useState<string | null>(null)   // блокировка только той клетки, по которой кликнули
  const [flashCell, setFlashCell] = useState<string | null>(null)  // вспышка фона = «запись принята»

  const allLessons: Lesson[] = useMemo(() => (course ? courseLessons(course) : []), [course])
  const gradeLessons: Lesson[] = useMemo(
    () => allLessons.filter((l) => l.submission || l.type === 'practice' || l.type === 'assignment' || l.type === 'quiz'),
    [allLessons],
  )
  const lessons = mode === 'grades' ? gradeLessons : allLessons

  const gradeMap = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, Record<string, number>>()
    for (const s of submissions) {
      if (s.grade > 0) {
        const r = m.get(s.studentId) ?? {}
        r[s.lessonId] = s.grade
        m.set(s.studentId, r)
      }
    }
    return m
  }, [submissions])

  const attMap = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, Record<string, AttendanceStatus>>()
    for (const a of attendance) {
      const r = m.get(a.studentId) ?? {}
      r[a.lessonId] = a.status
      m.set(a.studentId, r)
    }
    return m
  }, [attendance])

  const students = useMemo(
    () => enrollments.map((e) => ({ id: e.studentId, name: e.studentName || e.studentCallsign || e.studentId })),
    [enrollments],
  )

  function avgGrade(studentId: string): number {
    const vals = Object.values(gradeMap.get(studentId) ?? {})
    return vals.length ? vals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / vals.length : 0
  }
  function attPercent(studentId: string): number {
    const r = attMap.get(studentId) ?? {}
    const marks = Object.values(r)
    if (!marks.length) return -1
    const attended = marks.filter((s) => s !== 'absent').length
    return Math.round((attended / marks.length) * 100)
  }

  function cycle(lessonId: string, studentId: string) {
    const key = `${lessonId}:${studentId}`
    if (busyCell === key) return                 // двойной клик по той же клетке не шлёт гонку
    const cur = attMap.get(studentId)?.[lessonId] ?? null
    const idx = CYCLE.indexOf(cur)
    const next = CYCLE[(idx + 1) % CYCLE.length]
    setBusyCell(key)
    setAtt.mutate({ lessonId, studentId, status: next }, {
      onSuccess: () => { setFlashCell(key); setTimeout(() => setFlashCell((c) => (c === key ? null : c)), 500) },
      onError: (e) => toast.error((e as Error).message || 'отметка не сохранилась', 'ошибка'),
      onSettled: () => setBusyCell((c) => (c === key ? null : c)),
    })
  }

  function exportXls() {
    const title = course?.title ?? 'Журнал'
    const cols =
      mode === 'grades'
        ? [
            { header: t('journal.student'), value: (r: { id: string; name: string }) => r.name, width: 220 },
            ...gradeLessons.map((l) => ({ header: l.title, value: (r: { id: string }) => (gradeMap.get(r.id)?.[l.id] ? String(gradeMap.get(r.id)![l.id]) : '—'), width: 90 })),
            { header: t('journal.avg'), value: (r: { id: string }) => { const a = avgGrade(r.id); return a > 0 ? a.toFixed(2) : '—' }, width: 90 },
          ]
        : [
            { header: t('journal.student'), value: (r: { id: string; name: string }) => r.name, width: 220 },
            ...allLessons.map((l) => ({ header: l.title, value: (r: { id: string }) => { const s = attMap.get(r.id)?.[l.id]; return s ? ATT_LABEL[s] : '—' }, width: 70 })),
            { header: t('journal.att_pct'), value: (r: { id: string }) => { const p = attPercent(r.id); return p < 0 ? '—' : p + '%' }, width: 90 },
          ]
    const xls = buildStyledXls(students, cols, { title: t('journal.title'), subtitle: `${title} · ${mode === 'grades' ? t('journal.tab_grades') : t('journal.tab_attendance')}` })
    downloadXls(xls, `protocol-journal-${(course?.title ?? 'course').replace(/\s+/g, '_')}-${mode}.xls`)
    toast.success('Ведомость выгружена', 'готово')
  }

  const tab = (m: Mode, icon: React.ReactNode, label: string) => (
    <button
      onClick={() => setMode(m)}
      className={cn('flex items-center gap-2 border px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors clip-btn', mode === m ? 'border-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
    >
      {icon} {label}
    </button>
  )

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('journal.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{t('journal.badge')}</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('journal.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
      <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('journal.lead')}</p>

      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label>{t('journal.course')}</Label>
          <select className={field} value={courseId} onChange={(e) => setCourseId(e.target.value)}>
            {courses.length === 0 && <option value="">—</option>}
            {courses.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.title}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={exportXls} disabled={!students.length || !lessons.length}>
          <Download size={15} /> {t('journal.export')}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {tab('grades', <GraduationCap size={14} />, t('journal.tab_grades'))}
        {tab('attendance', <CalendarCheck size={14} />, t('journal.tab_attendance'))}
      </div>

      {courses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-10 text-center text-ink-muted">
          <NotebookPen size={22} className="text-ink-dim" /> {t('journal.no_courses')}
        </div>
      ) : students.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-6 border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('journal.no_students')}</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-6 overflow-x-auto border border-line bg-surface">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[640px] border-collapse text-left text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-line font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">
                <th className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-surface px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('journal.student')}</th>
                {lessons.map((l, i) => (
                  <th key={l.id} className="px-3 py-3 text-center font-normal" title={l.title}>{i + 1}</th>
                ))}
                <th className="px-3 py-3 text-center font-normal">{mode === 'grades' ? t('journal.avg') : t('journal.att_pct')}</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {students.map((st) => (
                <tr key={st.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-b-0">
                  <td className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-surface px-4 py-2.5 text-ink">{st.name}</td>
                  {lessons.map((l) => {
                    if (mode === 'grades') {
                      const g = gradeMap.get(st.id)?.[l.id]
                      return (
                        <td key={l.id} className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                          {g ? <span className={`inline-flex h-6 min-w-[1.75rem] items-center justify-center border px-1 font-mono text-xs ${gradeTone(g)}`}>{g}</span> : <span className="text-ink-dim">·</span>}
                        </td>
                      )
                    }
                    const s = attMap.get(st.id)?.[l.id]
                    return (
                      <td key={l.id} className="px-1.5 py-2 text-center">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => cycle(l.id, st.id)}
                          disabled={busyCell === `${l.id}:${st.id}`}
                          className={cn('inline-flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center border font-mono text-xs transition-colors', s ? ATT_TONE[s] : 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim hover:border-aqua/50', flashCell === `${l.id}:${st.id}` && 'bg-lime/10')}
                          title={t('journal.att_click')}
                        >
                          {s ? ATT_LABEL[s] : '·'}
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    )
                  })}
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-center">
                    {mode === 'grades' ? (
                      avgGrade(st.id) > 0 ? <span className={`inline-flex h-6 min-w-[2.5rem] items-center justify-center border px-1 font-mono text-xs ${gradeTone(Math.round(avgGrade(st.id)))}`}>{avgGrade(st.id).toFixed(2)}</span> : <span className="text-ink-dim">—</span>
                    ) : (
                      attPercent(st.id) < 0 ? <span className="text-ink-dim">—</span> : <span className="font-mono text-xs text-ink-soft">{attPercent(st.id)}%</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">
        {mode === 'attendance' && (
          <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3">
            <span className="text-lime">П — {t('journal.att_present')}</span>
            <span className="text-danger">Н — {t('journal.att_absent')}</span>
            <span className="text-amber-300">О — {t('journal.att_late')}</span>
            <span className="text-aqua">У — {t('journal.att_excused')}</span>
          </span>
        )}
        {lessons.length > 0 && (
          <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4">
            <span>{t('journal.legend')}:</span>
            {lessons.map((l, i) => <span key={l.id}>{i + 1} — {l.title}</span>)}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/LeaderboardPage.tsx =====
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Trophy, Lock } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useLeaderboard } from '@/features/leaderboard/useLeaderboard'
import { useXp } from '@/features/progress/useXp'
import { useProfileRank } from '@/features/progress/useRank'
import { useMyGrades } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { useMyAttendance } from '@/features/attendance/useAttendance'
import { useMyAttempts } from '@/features/quiz/useQuiz'
import { average } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import { ACHIEVEMENTS, type AchStats } from '@/features/progress/achievements'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

const MEDAL = ['text-amber-300', 'text-slate-300', 'text-orange-400']

function Achievements() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const xp = useXp()
  const rankInfo = useProfileRank()
  const { data: grades = [] } = useMyGrades(user?.id ?? '', true)
  const { data: attendance = [] } = useMyAttendance(user?.id, true)
  const { data: attempts = [] } = useMyAttempts(user?.id, true)

  const stats: AchStats = useMemo(() => {
    const attPct = attendance.length ? Math.round((attendance.filter((a) => a.status !== 'absent').length / attendance.length) * 100) : -1
    const passed = new Set(attempts.filter((a) => a.passed).map((a) => a.quizId)).size
    return {
      xp: xp.xp,
      level: xp.level,
      hours: rankInfo.hours ?? 0,
      coins: user?.coins ?? 0,
      avgGrade: average(grades),
      quizzesPassed: passed,
      attendancePct: attPct,
      rank: rankInfo.rank,
    }
  }, [xp, rankInfo, grades, attendance, attempts, user])

  const doneCount = ACHIEVEMENTS.filter((a) => a.done(stats)).length

  return (
    <div className="mt-14">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('leaderboard.ach_kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{doneCount}/{ACHIEVEMENTS.length}</Label>
      </div>
      <h2 className="mt-3 text-2xl sm:text-3xl">{t('leaderboard.ach_title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h2>
      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {ACHIEVEMENTS.map((a) => {
          const done = a.done(stats)
          const Icon = a.icon
          return (
            <div key={a.id} className={cn('clip-hud flex items-center gap-3 border p-4 transition-colors', done ? 'border-line-lime bg-surface' : 'border-line bg-surface/50 opacity-60')}>
              <span className={cn('flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border', done ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim')}>
                {done ? <Icon size={20} /> : <Lock size={18} />}
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className={cn('text-sm', done ? 'text-ink' : 'text-ink-muted')}>{t('ach.' + a.id + '_title')}</div>
                <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{t('ach.' + a.id + '_desc')}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function LeaderboardPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const scope = user?.role === 'superadmin' ? undefined : user?.institutionId
  const { data: rows = [], isLoading } = useLeaderboard(scope)
  const isStudent = user?.role === 'student'

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('leaderboard.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>TOP</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('leaderboard.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
      <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('leaderboard.lead')}</p>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-8 p-8 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
      ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-10 text-center text-ink-muted">
          <Trophy size={22} className="text-ink-dim" /> {t('leaderboard.empty')}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-8 overflow-x-auto border border-line bg-surface">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[480px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-line font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">#</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('leaderboard.student')}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-normal">{t('leaderboard.group')}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-normal">{t('leaderboard.level')}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-normal">XP</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((r, i) => {
                const me = r.userId === user?.id
                return (
                  <tr key={r.id} className={cn('border-b border-line last:border-b-0', me && 'bg-aqua/5')}>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                      <span className={cn('font-display text-base', i < 3 ? MEDAL[i] : 'text-ink-dim')}>{i + 1}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                      <span className={cn('text-sm', me ? 'text-aqua' : 'text-ink')}>{r.name}</span>
                      <span className="ml-2 font-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim">◆ {r.rank}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{r.groupCode || '—'}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-center font-mono text-xs text-lime">{r.level}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-right font-mono text-xs text-ink-soft">{r.xp.toLocaleString('ru-RU')}</td>
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {isStudent && <Achievements />}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/LessonEditorPage.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ArrowLeft, Plus, Eye, Save } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useCourse, useCourseMutations } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { BlockEditItem } from '@/features/lesson/BlockEditItem'
import { newBlock, parseContent, serializeContent, templateFor, type BlockType, type LessonBlock } from '@/entities/lesson/content'
import type { Lesson } from '@/entities/course/types'

const ADD: { type: BlockType; label: string }[] = [
  { type: 'heading', label: 'Заголовок' },
  { type: 'text', label: 'Текст' },
  { type: 'image', label: 'Изображение' },
  { type: 'callout', label: 'Заметка' },
  { type: 'divider', label: 'Разделитель' },
  { type: 'trainer', label: 'Тренажёр' },
]

function findLesson(course: ReturnType<typeof useCourse>['data'], lessonId: string): Lesson | undefined {
  return course?.sections.flatMap((s) => s.lessons).find((l) => l.id === lessonId)
}

export function LessonEditorPage() {
  const { courseId = '', lessonId = '' } = useParams()
  const { data: course, isLoading } = useCourse(courseId)
  const { updateLesson } = useCourseMutations()
  const lesson = findLesson(course, lessonId)

  const [blocks, setBlocks] = useState<LessonBlock[]>([])
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)
  const [seeded, setSeeded] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (lesson && !seeded) {
      const parsed = parseContent(lesson.content)
      setBlocks(parsed.length ? parsed : templateFor(lesson.type))
      setSeeded(true)
    }
  }, [lesson, seeded])

  if (isLoading) return <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-6 py-20 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">Загрузка…</div>
  if (!lesson) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl">Урок не найден</h1>
        <Link to={`/app/teach/${courseId}`} className="mt-4 inline-block font-mono text-sm text-aqua">← к курсу</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  function change(i: number, patch: Partial<LessonBlock>): void {
    setBlocks((b) => b.map((x, idx) => (idx === i ? ({ ...x, ...patch } as LessonBlock) : x)))
    setDirty(true)
  }
  function move(i: number, dir: -1 | 1): void {
    setBlocks((b) => {
      const j = i + dir
      if (j < 0 || j >= b.length) return b
      const next = [...b]
      ;[next[i], next[j]] = [next[j], next[i]]
      return next
    })
    setDirty(true)
  }
  function remove(i: number): void {
    setBlocks((b) => b.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))
    setDirty(true)
  }
  function addBlock(type: BlockType): void {
    setBlocks((b) => [...b, newBlock(type)])
    setDirty(true)
  }
  function save(): void {
    updateLesson.mutate({ id: lesson!.id, patch: { content: serializeContent(blocks) } }, { onSuccess: () => setDirty(false) })
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Link to={`/app/teach/${courseId}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
          <ArrowLeft size={14} /> к курсу
        </Link>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link to={`/app/courses/${courseId}/lessons/${lessonId}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted hover:text-aqua">
            <Eye size={14} /> предпросмотр
          </Link>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={save} disabled={updateLesson.isPending || !dirty}>
            <Save size={15} /> {dirty ? 'Сохранить' : 'Сохранено'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4">
        <Label accent>// урок · {lesson.title}</Label>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">Контент урока<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {ADD.map((a) => (
          <button key={a.type} onClick={() => addBlock(a.type)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-strong px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:border-aqua hover:text-aqua clip-btn">
            <Plus size={13} /> {a.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3">
        {blocks.length === 0 && (
          <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">Добавьте первый блок.</div>
        )}
        {blocks.map((b, i) => (
          <BlockEditItem key={b.id} block={b} onChange={(p) => change(i, p)} onMove={(d) => move(i, d)} onRemove={() => remove(i)} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/LessonViewPage.tsx =====
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ArrowLeft, Lock, Target, Info } from 'lucide-react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { useCourse } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { SubmitWork } from '@/features/submission/SubmitWork'
import { BlockView } from '@/features/lesson/BlockView'
import { parseContent } from '@/entities/lesson/content'
import type { Lesson } from '@/entities/course/types'

function findLesson(course: ReturnType<typeof useCourse>['data'], lessonId: string): Lesson | undefined {
  return course?.sections.flatMap((s) => s.lessons).find((l) => l.id === lessonId)
}

/** Читаемая колонка для текста, выровнена по левому краю; тренажёр — на всю ширину. */
const READ = 'w-full max-w-3xl'

export function LessonViewPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { courseId = '', lessonId = '' } = useParams()
  const { data: course, isLoading } = useCourse(courseId)
  const lesson = findLesson(course, lessonId)
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const isStudent = user?.role === 'student'
  const isCheckpoint = lesson?.type === 'practice' || lesson?.type === 'assignment'

  if (isLoading) return <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-20 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">Загрузка…</div>
  if (!lesson) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-20 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl">Урок не найден</h1>
        <Link to={`/app/courses/${courseId}`} className="mt-4 inline-block font-mono text-sm text-aqua">← к курсу</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }
  // урок закрыт преподавателем — студенту доступ закрыт
  if (isStudent && !lesson.open) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-20 text-center">
        <span className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center border border-line-strong text-ink-dim">
          <Lock size={22} />
        </span>
        <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl">{t('course.locked')}</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('course.locked_hint')}</p>
        <Link to={`/app/courses/${courseId}`} className="mt-5 inline-block font-mono text-sm text-aqua">← {t('course.back')}</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const blocks = parseContent(lesson.content)

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className={READ}>
        <Link to={`/app/courses/${courseId}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
          <ArrowLeft size={14} /> к курсу
        </Link>
        <div className="mt-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Label accent>// {isCheckpoint ? t('course.checkpoint') : 'урок'}</Label>
            {isCheckpoint && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 border border-line-lime px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-lime">
                <Target size={11} /> {t('course.checkpoint')}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{lesson.title}</h1>
          <div className="mt-2 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted">+{lesson.xp} XP · {lesson.durationHours} ч</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <article className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-4">
        {blocks.length === 0 ? (
          <div className={`${READ} border border-dashed border-line-strong p-8 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-dim`}>
            Материалы урока скоро появятся
          </div>
        ) : (
          blocks.map((b) =>
            b.type === 'trainer' ? (
              <BlockView key={b.id} block={b} />
            ) : (
              <div key={b.id} className={READ}>
                <BlockView block={b} />
              </div>
            ),
          )
        )}
      </article>

      {isStudent && user && (
        <div className={`${READ} mt-8`}>
          <SubmitWork courseId={courseId} lessonId={lessonId} studentId={user.id} />
        </div>
      )}

      {isStudent && (
        <div className={`${READ} mt-8 flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-line pt-6 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted`}>
          <Info size={14} className="text-ink-dim" />
          {t('lesson.status_by_grade')}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/PlaygroundPage.tsx =====
import { useState, type ComponentType } from 'react'
import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'

const TRAINER_COMPONENTS: Record<string, ComponentType> = {
  uxui: UxUiTrainer,
  console: ConsoleTrainer,
  network: NetworkTrainer,
  app: CodeTrainer,
}

export function PlaygroundPage() {

  const open = TRAINERS.find((t) => t.id === openId)
  const Trainer = open ? TRAINER_COMPONENTS[open.id] : undefined

  if (open && Trainer) {

      </p>

          return (

              key={tr.id}
              onClick={() => live && setOpenId(tr.id)}

                live ? 'border-line-aqua hover:border-aqua' : 'cursor-default border-line opacity-70',

===== FILE: src/pages/app/QuizTakePage.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ArrowLeft, Check, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Send } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useQuizForTake, useQuizMutations } from '@/features/quiz/useQuiz'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

const field = 'h-11 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

export function QuizTakePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const { data, isLoading } = useQuizForTake(id)
  const m = useQuizMutations()

  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, unknown>>({})
  const [result, setResult] = useState<{ score: number; passed: boolean } | null>(null)

  if (isLoading || !data) return <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
  const { quiz, questions } = data

  function setSingle(qid: string, idx: string) {
    setAnswers((a) => ({ ...a, [qid]: idx }))
  }
  function toggleMulti(qid: string, idx: string) {
    setAnswers((a) => {
      const cur = Array.isArray(a[qid]) ? (a[qid] as string[]) : []
      return { ...a, [qid]: cur.includes(idx) ? cur.filter((x) => x !== idx) : [...cur, idx] }
    })
  }
  function setText(qid: string, val: string) {
    setAnswers((a) => ({ ...a, [qid]: val }))
  }

  function submit() {
    if (!user || !id) return
    m.submit.mutate(
      { quizId: id, studentId: user.id, answers },
      { onSuccess: (res) => setResult(res) },
    )
  }

  if (result) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg px-4 py-16 text-center">
        {result.passed ? <CheckCircle2 size={56} className="mx-auto text-lime" /> : <XCircle size={56} className="mx-auto text-danger" />}
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl">{result.passed ? t('quiz.res_passed') : t('quiz.res_failed')}</h1>
        <div className="mt-2 font-display text-5xl text-aqua">{result.score}%</div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('quiz.pass_score')} {quiz.passScore}%</p>
        <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center gap-3">
          <Link to="/app/quizzes"><Button variant="secondary"><ArrowLeft size={15} /> {t('quiz.to_list')}</Button></Link>
          {!result.passed && <Button onClick={() => { setResult(null); setAnswers({}) }}>{t('quiz.retake')}</Button>}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <Link to="/app/quizzes" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
        <ArrowLeft size={14} /> {t('quiz.to_list')}
      </Link>
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{quiz.title}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
      {quiz.description && <p className="mt-2 text-ink-soft">{quiz.description}</p>}
      <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">{t('quiz.pass_score')} {quiz.passScore}% · {questions.length} {t('quiz.q_count')}</p>

      <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-5">
        {questions.map((q, i) => (
          <div key={q.id} className="border border-line bg-surface p-5">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
              <span className="font-display text-lg text-aqua">{i + 1}</span>
              <h3 className="text-lg text-ink">{q.text}</h3>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
              {q.type === 'text' ? (
                <input className={field} value={(answers[q.id] as string) ?? ''} onChange={(e) => setText(q.id, e.target.value)} placeholder={t('quiz.your_answer')} />
              ) : (
                q.options.map((opt, idx) => {
                  const sIdx = String(idx)
                  const selected = q.type === 'single' ? answers[q.id] === sIdx : Array.isArray(answers[q.id]) && (answers[q.id] as string[]).includes(sIdx)
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={idx}
                      onClick={() => (q.type === 'single' ? setSingle(q.id, sIdx) : toggleMulti(q.id, sIdx))}
                      className={cn('flex items-center gap-3 border px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm transition-colors', selected ? 'border-line-aqua text-aqua' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-soft hover:border-aqua/40')}
                    >
                      <span className={cn('flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center border', q.type === 'single' ? 'rounded-full' : '', selected ? 'border-aqua bg-aqua/20' : 'border-line-strong')}>
                        {selected && <Check size={12} className="text-aqua" />}
                      </span>
                      {opt}
                    </button>
                  )
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Button className="mt-8" onClick={submit} disabled={m.submit.isPending}>
        <Send size={15} /> {m.submit.isPending ? '…' : t('quiz.finish')}
      </Button>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/QuizzesPage.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Plus, Trash2, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Check, Play, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { Coin } from '@/features/currency/Coin'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { usePublishedCourses } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useConfirm } from '@/shared/ui/Confirm'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'
import {
  useAuthorQuizzes,
  usePublishedQuizzes,
  useQuizQuestions,
  useMyAttempts,
  useQuizMutations,
} from '@/features/quiz/useQuiz'
import type { Quiz, QuestionType } from '@/entities/quiz/service'

const field = 'h-11 w-full border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none'

// ---- редактор вопросов одного теста ----------------------------------------
function QuestionEditor({ quiz }: { quiz: Quiz }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { data: questions = [] } = useQuizQuestions(quiz.id)
  const m = useQuizMutations()
  const confirm = useConfirm()

  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [type, setType] = useState<QuestionType>('single')
  const [optionsText, setOptionsText] = useState('')
  const [correct, setCorrect] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [textAnswers, setTextAnswers] = useState('')
  const [points, setPoints] = useState(1)

  const options = useMemo(() => optionsText.split('\n').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean), [optionsText])

  function toggleCorrect(idx: string) {
    if (type === 'single') setCorrect([idx])
    else setCorrect((c) => (c.includes(idx) ? c.filter((x) => x !== idx) : [...c, idx]))
  }

  function add() {
    if (!text.trim()) return
    const correctVals = type === 'text' ? textAnswers.split('\n').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : correct
    if (type !== 'text' && (options.length < 2 || correctVals.length === 0)) return
    m.addQuestion.mutate(
      { quizId: quiz.id, text: text.trim(), type, options: type === 'text' ? [] : options, correct: correctVals, points, order: questions.length },
      { onSuccess: () => { setText(''); setOptionsText(''); setCorrect([]); setTextAnswers(''); setPoints(1) } },
    )
  }
  async function removeQ(id: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: t('quiz.q_delete_confirm'), confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true })
    if (ok) m.removeQuestion.mutate(id)
  }

  return (
    <div className="border-t border-line bg-surface-2/40 p-4">
      <Label accent>{t('quiz.questions')} · {questions.length}</Label>
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
        {questions.map((q, i) => (
          <div key={q.id} className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-2">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-sm text-ink">{i + 1}. {q.text}</div>
              <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{t('quiz.type_' + q.type)} · {q.points} {t('quiz.points')}</div>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => removeQ(q.id)} className="shrink-0 p-1.5 text-ink-muted hover:text-danger" aria-label={t('common.delete')}><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* добавить вопрос */}
      <div className="mt-4 border border-line-lime bg-surface p-4">
        <Label>{t('quiz.add_question')}</Label>
        <textarea className={cn(field, 'mt-2 h-auto py-2')} rows={2} value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} placeholder={t('quiz.q_text_ph')} />
        <div className="mt-2 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <select className={field} value={type} onChange={(e) => { setType(e.target.value as QuestionType); setCorrect([]) }}>
            <option value="single">{t('quiz.type_single')}</option>
            <option value="multi">{t('quiz.type_multi')}</option>
            <option value="text">{t('quiz.type_text')}</option>
          </select>
          <input type="number" min={1} className={field} value={points} onChange={(e) => setPoints(Number(e.target.value) || 1)} placeholder={t('quiz.points')} />
        </div>

        {type === 'text' ? (
          <div className="mt-2">
            <Label>{t('quiz.correct_answers')}</Label>
            <textarea className={cn(field, 'mt-1 h-auto py-2')} rows={2} value={textAnswers} onChange={(e) => setTextAnswers(e.target.value)} placeholder={t('quiz.answers_ph')} />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-2">
            <Label>{t('quiz.options_hint')}</Label>
            <textarea className={cn(field, 'mt-1 h-auto py-2')} rows={3} value={optionsText} onChange={(e) => { setOptionsText(e.target.value); setCorrect([]) }} placeholder={t('quiz.options_ph')} />
            {options.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{t('quiz.mark_correct')}:</span>
                {options.map((opt, idx) => (
                  <button
                    key={idx}
                    onClick={() => toggleCorrect(String(idx))}
                    className={cn('flex items-center gap-2 border px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-sm transition-colors', correct.includes(String(idx)) ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-soft hover:border-aqua/50')}
                  >
                    <span className={cn('flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center border', correct.includes(String(idx)) ? 'border-lime bg-lime/20' : 'border-line-strong')}>
                      {correct.includes(String(idx)) && <Check size={11} className="text-lime" />}
                    </span>
                    {opt}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        <Button className="mt-3" size="sm" onClick={add} disabled={m.addQuestion.isPending || !text.trim()}>
          <Plus size={14} /> {t('quiz.add_question')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ---- преподаватель ---------------------------------------------------------
function TeacherQuizzes() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const { data: quizzes = [] } = useAuthorQuizzes(user?.id)
  const { data: courses = [] } = usePublishedCourses()
  const myCourses = useMemo(() => courses.filter((c) => c.ownerId === user?.id || user?.role !== 'teacher'), [courses, user])
  const m = useQuizMutations()
  const confirm = useConfirm()

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('')
  const [courseId, setCourseId] = useState('')
  const [passScore, setPassScore] = useState(60)
  const [coins, setCoins] = useState(15)
  const [xp, setXp] = useState(30)
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string | null>(null)

  function create() {
    if (!title.trim() || !user) return
    m.create.mutate(
      { title: title.trim(), authorId: user.id, courseId: courseId || undefined, institutionId: user.institutionId || undefined, passScore, rewardCoins: coins, rewardXp: xp, published: false },
      { onSuccess: (id) => { setTitle(''); setOpen(id) } },
    )
  }
  async function removeQuiz(id: string, name: string) {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: t('common.delete'), message: `${name} — ${t('quiz.delete_confirm')}`, confirmLabel: t('common.delete'), danger: true })
    if (ok) m.remove.mutate(id)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <div className="border border-line-lime bg-surface p-5">
        <Label>{t('quiz.new')}</Label>
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <input className={cn(field, 'sm:col-span-2')} value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder={t('quiz.title_ph')} />
          <select className={field} value={courseId} onChange={(e) => setCourseId(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">{t('quiz.no_course')}</option>
            {myCourses.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.title}</option>)}
          </select>
          <input type="number" min={1} max={100} className={field} value={passScore} onChange={(e) => setPassScore(Number(e.target.value) || 60)} placeholder={t('quiz.pass_score')} />
          <input type="number" min={0} className={field} value={coins} onChange={(e) => setCoins(Number(e.target.value) || 0)} placeholder={t('quiz.reward_coins')} />
          <input type="number" min={0} className={field} value={xp} onChange={(e) => setXp(Number(e.target.value) || 0)} placeholder={t('quiz.reward_xp')} />
        </div>
        <Button className="mt-3" onClick={create} disabled={m.create.isPending || !title.trim()}><Plus size={15} /> {t('quiz.create')}</Button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {quizzes.length === 0 && <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('quiz.empty_teacher')}</div>}
        {quizzes.map((q) => (
          <div key={q.id} className="border border-line bg-surface">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3">
              <button onClick={() => setOpen(open === q.id ? null : q.id)} className="text-ink-muted hover:text-aqua">
                {open === q.id ? <ChevronDown size={18} /> : <ChevronRight size={18} />}
              </button>
              <div className="min-w-40 flex-1">
                <div className="text-sm text-ink">{q.title}</div>
                <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{t('quiz.pass_score')}: {q.passScore}% · <Coin size={11} className="inline" /> {q.rewardCoins} · {q.rewardXp} XP</div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => m.update.mutate({ id: q.id, patch: { published: !q.published } })}
                className={cn('border px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors clip-btn', q.published ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted hover:text-ink')}
              >
                {q.published ? t('quiz.published') : t('quiz.draft')}
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => removeQuiz(q.id, q.title)} className="p-2 text-ink-muted hover:text-danger" aria-label={t('common.delete')}><Trash2 size={16} /></button>
            </div>
            {open === q.id && <QuestionEditor quiz={q} />}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ---- студент ---------------------------------------------------------------
function StudentQuizzes() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const { data: quizzes = [], isLoading } = usePublishedQuizzes()
  const { data: attempts = [] } = useMyAttempts(user?.id, true)

  const bestByQuiz = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, number>()
    for (const a of attempts) m.set(a.quizId, Math.max(m.get(a.quizId) ?? 0, a.score))
    return m
  }, [attempts])

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
      <div>
        <Label accent>{t('quiz.available')}</Label>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-3">
          {quizzes.length === 0 && <div className="border border-line bg-surface p-8 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('quiz.empty_student')}</div>}
          {quizzes.map((q) => {
            const best = bestByQuiz.get(q.id)
            return (
              <div key={q.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border border-line bg-surface p-5">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <h3 className="text-lg text-ink">{q.title}</h3>
                  {q.description && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-soft">{q.description}</p>}
                  <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3 font-mono text-xs">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-300"><Coin size={14} /> +{q.rewardCoins}</span>
                    <span className="text-lime">+{q.rewardXp} XP</span>
                    <span className="text-ink-dim">{t('quiz.pass_score')} {q.passScore}%</span>
                    {best != null && <span className={best >= q.passScore ? 'text-lime' : 'text-ink-muted'}>{t('quiz.your_best')}: {best}%</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Link to={`/app/quiz/${q.id}`}>
                  <Button size="sm"><Play size={14} /> {best != null ? t('quiz.retake') : t('quiz.take')}</Button>
                </Link>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {attempts.length > 0 && (
        <div>
          <Label accent>{t('quiz.history')}</Label>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
            {attempts.map((a) => (
              <div key={a.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-2.5">
                <span className="text-sm text-ink">{a.quizTitle}</span>
                <span className={cn('flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-xs', a.passed ? 'text-lime' : 'text-danger')}>
                  {a.passed ? <CheckCircle2 size={14} /> : <XCircle size={14} />} {a.score}%
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function QuizzesPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const role = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.role ?? 'student')
  const isTeacher = role === 'teacher' || role === 'admin' || role === 'superadmin'

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('quiz.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{t('quiz.badge')}</Label>
      </div>
      <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('quiz.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
      <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink-muted">{isTeacher ? t('quiz.lead_teacher') : t('quiz.lead_student')}</p>
      <div className="mt-8">{isTeacher ? <TeacherQuizzes /> : <StudentQuizzes />}</div>
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/SubmissionsPage.tsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { ArrowLeft, CheckCircle2, Clock } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { GenerativeAvatar } from '@/features/profile/GenerativeAvatar'
import { FilePreview } from '@/features/submission/FilePreview'
import { useCourse } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { useCourseSubmissions, useGradeSubmission } from '@/features/submission/useSubmissions'
import { gradeTone } from '@/shared/lib/grade'
import type { Submission } from '@/entities/submission/service'

const GRADES = [2, 3, 4, 5]

function GradeCard({ s, courseId }: { s: Submission; courseId: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const grade = useGradeSubmission(courseId)
  const [value, setValue] = useState<number>(s.grade || 0)
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState(s.feedback)

  return (
    <div className="border border-line bg-surface">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <GenerativeAvatar seed={s.studentId} rank="F" size={38} />
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="truncate text-sm text-ink">{s.studentCallsign || s.studentName || s.studentId}</div>
            <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{s.lessonTitle}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span
          className={
            'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 border px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase ' +
            (s.status === 'graded' ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-aqua text-aqua')
          }
        >
          {s.status === 'graded' ? <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> : <Clock size={12} />}
          {s.status === 'graded' ? t('review.graded') : t('review.awaiting')}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
        <div>
          <div className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{t('review.answer')}</div>
          <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-sm text-ink-soft">{s.text || t('review.no_answer')}</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-dim">{t('review.files')}</div>
          {s.fileUrls.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('review.no_files')}</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-2">
              {s.fileUrls.map((url, i) => (
                <FilePreview key={url} url={url} name={s.files[i]} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 border-t border-line pt-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label>{t('review.grade_ph')}</Label>
            <div className="flex gap-1.5">
              {GRADES.map((g) => (
                <button
                  key={g}
                  onClick={() => setValue(g)}
                  className={`flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center border font-mono text-base transition-colors ${
                    value === g ? gradeTone(g) + ' bg-surface-2' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-dim hover:text-ink'
                  }`}
                >
                  {g}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <textarea
            value={feedback}
            onChange={(e) => setFeedback(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={t('review.feedback_ph')}
            rows={2}
            className="min-w-48 flex-1 resize-y border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 p-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-ink-dim focus:border-aqua focus:outline-none"
          />
          <Button
            onClick={() => grade.mutate({ id: s.id, grade: value, feedback: feedback.trim() })}
            disabled={grade.isPending || value === 0}
          >
            {grade.isPending ? '…' : t('review.save')}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function SubmissionsPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const { courseId = '' } = useParams()
  const { data: course } = useCourse(courseId)
  const { data: submissions = [], isLoading } = useCourseSubmissions(courseId, true)

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <Link to={`/app/teach/${courseId}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua">
        <ArrowLeft size={14} /> {t('review.back')}
      </Link>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <Label accent>// {course?.title ?? ''}</Label>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('review.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
        </div>
        <Label>{t('review.count', { count: submissions.length })}</Label>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-16 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
      ) : submissions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-10 border border-line bg-surface p-10 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('review.empty')}</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-4">
          {submissions.map((s) => (
            <GradeCard key={s.id} s={s} courseId={courseId} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/app/TeachPage.tsx =====
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { usePublishedCourses, useCourseMutations } from '@/features/courses/useCourses'
import { courseLessons } from '@/entities/course/lib'

export function TeachPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const { data: allCourses = [], isLoading } = usePublishedCourses()
  const courses = allCourses.filter((c) => c.ownerId === user?.id)
  const { createCourse, updateCourse, deleteCourse } = useCourseMutations()
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  function onCreate(): void {
    if (!user) return
    createCourse.mutate(
      { ownerId: user.id, instructor: user.fullName, institutionId: user.institutionId },
      { onSuccess: (id) => navigate(`/app/teach/${id}`) },
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 sm:py-10">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <Label accent>{t('teach.kicker')}</Label>
        <Label>{t('teach.courses_count', { count: courses.length })}</Label>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl">{t('teach.title')}<span className="text-aqua">.</span></h1>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={onCreate} disabled={createCourse.isPending}>
          <Plus size={16} /> {t('teach.create')}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="mt-10 text-center font-mono text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</div>
      ) : courses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-10 border border-line bg-surface p-10 text-center text-ink-muted">{t('teach.empty')}</div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-3">
          {courses.map((c) => {
            const lessons = courseLessons(c).length
            const published = c.status === 'published'
            return (
              <li key={c.id} className="clip-hud flex flex-col gap-4 border border-line bg-surface p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <h3 className="truncate font-display text-lg text-ink">{c.title}</h3>
                    <span className={'shrink-0 border px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase ' + (published ? 'border-line-lime text-lime' : 'border-line-strong text-ink-muted')}>
                      {published ? t('teach.published') : t('teach.draft')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs text-ink-dim">
                    {c.category} · {t('teach.meta', { sections: c.sections.length, lessons })}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                  <Link to={`/app/teach/${c.id}`}>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary"><Pencil size={15} /> {t('teach.edit')}</Button>
                  </Link>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => updateCourse.mutate({ id: c.id, patch: { status: published ? 'draft' : 'published' } })}
                    aria-label={published ? t('teach.unpublish') : t('teach.publish')}
                    className="border border-line-strong p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-aqua clip-btn"
                  >
                    {published ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => deleteCourse.mutate(c.id)}
                    aria-label={t('teach.delete')}
                    className="border border-line-strong p-2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger clip-btn"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={16} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </li>
            )
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/pages/FeaturesPage/FeaturesPage.tsx =====
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { ArrowLeft, GraduationCap, UserCog, Shield, Cpu, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { Header } from '@/widgets/Header'
import { Container } from '@/shared/ui/Container'
import { Button } from '@/shared/ui/Button'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'
import { staggerContainer, fadeInUp } from '@/shared/lib/motion'

interface Role {
  icon: typeof GraduationCap
  glyph: string
  title: string
  tag: string
  accent: 'aqua' | 'lime' | 'ink'
  items: string[]
}

const ROLES: Role[] = [
  {
    icon: GraduationCap,
    glyph: '◈',
    title: 'Студент',
    tag: 'student · доступ к учёбе',
    accent: 'aqua',

      'Сдача работ на любом уроке: текст + файлы (до 3, по 10 МБ) — уходит в проверку',
      'Прохождение подтверждает преподаватель оценкой: 3–5 — пройдено, 2 — нет',
      'Опыт зависит от оценки (5 — полный, 4/3 — меньше); пересдача двойки — потолок как у 4',
      'Ранг растёт по часам обучения: F → SSS (≈4000 ч — «выпускник»)',
      'Средний балл по курсу и по всем предметам',
      'Тренажёры с живой авто-проверкой',
      'Своя цифровая ID-карта профиля',
    ],
  },
  {
    icon: UserCog,
    glyph: '◇',
    title: 'Преподаватель',
    tag: 'teacher · всё как у студента + кабинет',
