===== FILE: src/shared/ui/FileDrop.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ChangeEvent, type DragEvent } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { Check, CloudUpload, FileText, Loader2, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

const EASE = [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] as const
interface Props {
  files: File[]
  onPick: (list: FileList | null) => void
  onRemove: (index: number) => void
  uploading?: boolean
  error?: unknown
  accept?: string
  multiple?: boolean
  max?: number
  label?: string
  hint?: string
}

/** HUD‑зона вложения файла: drag&drop + список + спиннер загрузки + вспышка подтверждения. */
export function FileDrop({ files, onPick, onRemove, uploading, error, accept, multiple, max, label = 'прикрепить файл', hint }: Props) {
  const [over, setOver] = useState(false)
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState<'ok' | 'err' | null>(null)
  const prev = useRef(false)
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (prev.current && !uploading) {
      setFlash(error ? 'err' : 'ok')
      const t = setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), 1000)
      prev.current = false
      return () => clearTimeout(t)
    }
    prev.current = !!uploading
  }, [uploading, error])

  const onDrop = (e: DragEvent) => { e.preventDefault(); setOver(false); if (!uploading) onPick(e.dataTransfer.files) }
  const full = max != null && files.length >= max

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div
        onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!full) setOver(true) }}
        onDragLeave={() => setOver(false)}
        onDrop={onDrop}
        onClick={() => !full && !uploading && input.current?.click()}
        className={cn(
          'clip-hud flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 border bg-surface-2 px-4 py-6 text-center transition-colors',
          over ? 'border-aqua bg-aqua/5' : 'border-line-strong hover:border-line-aqua',
          (full || uploading) && 'cursor-default opacity-60',
        )}
      >
        <input ref={input} type="file" accept={accept} multiple={multiple} className="hidden"
          onChange={(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => { onPick(e.target.files); e.target.value = '' }} />
        {uploading ? <Loader2 size={20} className="animate-spin text-aqua" />
          : flash === 'ok' ? <motion.span initial={{ scale: 0.4 }} animate={{ scale: 1 }} transition={{ ease: EASE }}><Check size={20} className="text-lime" /></motion.span>
          : flash === 'err' ? <X size={20} className="text-danger" />
          : <CloudUpload size={20} className={cn(over ? 'text-aqua' : 'text-ink-dim')} />}
        <div className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted">
          {uploading ? 'загрузка…' : flash === 'ok' ? <span className="glitch text-lime">загружено</span> : flash === 'err' ? <span className="text-danger">ошибка</span> : label}
        </div>
        {hint && !uploading && !flash && <div className="text-[11px] text-ink-dim">{hint}</div>}
      </div>

      {files.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {files.map((f, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="clip-btn flex items-center gap-2 border border-line bg-surface px-2.5 py-1.5">
              {f.type.startsWith('image/')
                ? <img src={URL.createObjectURL(f)} alt="" className="h-8 w-8 border border-line-strong object-cover" />
                : <FileText size={16} className="text-ink-dim" />}
              <span className="max-w-[140px] truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-soft">{f.name}</span>
              <button onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onRemove(i) }} aria-label="Убрать" className="text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-danger"><X size={12} /></button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/shared/ui/Input/index.ts =====
export { Input } from './Input'


===== FILE: src/shared/ui/Input/Input.tsx =====
import { forwardRef, useId, useState, type InputHTMLAttributes } from 'react'
import { Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'
import { Label } from '@/shared/ui/Label'

interface InputProps extends InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  label?: string
  error?: string
  /** Показать переключатель видимости для пароля. */
  reveal?: boolean
}

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ label, error, reveal = false, type = 'text', className, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const autoId = useId()
    const inputId = id ?? autoId
    const { t } = useTranslation()
    const [show, setShow] = useState(false)
    const effectiveType = reveal ? (show ? 'text' : 'password') : type

    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId}>
            <Label>{label}</Label>
          </label>
        )}
        <div className="relative">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            id={inputId}
            type={effectiveType}
            aria-invalid={!!error}
            className={cn(
              'h-12 w-full rounded-[10px] border bg-surface-2 px-4 text-ink',
              'placeholder:text-ink-dim transition-colors duration-200',
              'focus:border-lime focus:outline-none',
              error ? 'border-danger' : 'border-line-strong',
              reveal && 'pr-11',
              className,
            )}
            {...props}
          />
          {reveal && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShow((s) => !s)}
              aria-label={show ? t('auth.hide_password') : t('auth.show_password')}
              className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-lime"
            >
              {show ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {error && <span className="text-sm text-danger">{error}</span>}
      </div>
    )
  },
)
Input.displayName = 'Input'

===== FILE: src/shared/ui/ProgressRing/ProgressRing.tsx =====
interface Props {
  value: number // 0..100
  size?: number
  stroke?: number
  color?: string
  label?: string
}

export function ProgressRing({ value, size = 56, stroke = 5, color = '#9fe8ff', label }: Props) {
  const r = (size - stroke) / 2
  const c = 2 * Math.PI * r
  const off = c - (Math.min(100, Math.max(0, value)) / 100) * c
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`} role="img" aria-label={`Прогресс ${value}%`}>
      <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={r} fill="none" stroke="#232838" strokeWidth={stroke} />
      <circle
        cx={size / 2}
        cy={size / 2}
        r={r}
        fill="none"
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={stroke}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        strokeDasharray={c}
        strokeDashoffset={off}
        transform={`rotate(-90 ${size / 2} ${size / 2})`}
        style={{ transition: 'stroke-dashoffset 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)' }}
      />
      <text x="50%" y="50%" dominantBaseline="central" textAnchor="middle" fill="#eaf2f0" fontSize={size * 0.26} fontFamily="'JetBrains Mono', monospace">
        {label ?? `${value}%`}
      </text>
    </svg>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/shared/ui/toast.tsx =====
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { Check, X, AlertOctagon, Info, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

type Kind = 'success' | 'error' | 'info' | 'loading'
interface Toast { id: number; kind: Kind; title?: string; message: string; duration?: number }
interface Ctx {
  push: (t: Omit<Toast, 'id'>) => number
  update: (id: number, patch: Partial<Omit<Toast, 'id'>>) => void
  dismiss: (id: number) => void
  success: (message: string, title?: string) => void
  error: (message: string, title?: string) => void
  info: (message: string, title?: string) => void
  /** Один вызов = «идёт…» → «готово/ошибка». Удобно для мутаций и uploadFile. */
  promise: <T>(p: Promise<T>, m: { loading: string; success: string | ((v: T) => string); error: string }) => Promise<T>
}
const ToastCtx = createContext<Ctx | null>(null)
export const useToast = (): Ctx => {
  const c = useContext(ToastCtx)
  if (!c) throw new Error('useToast вне <ToastProvider>')
  return c
}

const EASE = [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] as const
const TONE: Record<Kind, { border: string; text: string; icon: ReactNode; dur: number }> = {
  success: { border: 'border-line-lime', text: 'text-lime', icon: <Check size={16} />, dur: 2600 },
  error:   { border: 'border-danger',    text: 'text-danger', icon: <AlertOctagon size={16} />, dur: 4200 },
  info:    { border: 'border-line-aqua', text: 'text-aqua', icon: <Info size={16} />, dur: 3000 },
  loading: { border: 'border-line-strong', text: 'text-ink-soft', icon: <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />, dur: 0 },
}

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Toast[]>([])
  const seq = useRef(0)
  const timers = useRef<Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>(new Map())

  const clear = useCallback((id: number) => {
    const t = timers.current.get(id); if (t) clearTimeout(t); timers.current.delete(id)
  }, [])
  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => { clear(id); setItems((a) => a.filter((x) => x.id !== id)) }, [clear])
  const arm = useCallback((id: number, dur: number) => {
    if (!dur) return
    clear(id)
    timers.current.set(id, setTimeout(() => setItems((a) => a.filter((x) => x.id !== id)), dur))
  }, [clear])

  const push = useCallback((t: Omit<Toast, 'id'>) => {
    const id = ++seq.current
    setItems((a) => [...a, { ...t, id }])
    arm(id, t.duration ?? TONE[t.kind].dur)
    return id
  }, [arm])
  const update = useCallback((id: number, patch: Partial<Omit<Toast, 'id'>>) => {
    setItems((a) => a.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)))
    const k = patch.kind ?? items.find((x) => x.id === id)?.kind ?? 'info'
    arm(id, patch.duration ?? TONE[k].dur)
  }, [arm, items])

  const success = useCallback((message: string, title?: string) => { push({ kind: 'success', message, title }) }, [push])
  const error = useCallback((message: string, title?: string) => { push({ kind: 'error', message, title }) }, [push])
  const info = useCallback((message: string, title?: string) => { push({ kind: 'info', message, title }) }, [push])
  const promise = useCallback(<T,>(p: Promise<T>, m: { loading: string; success: string | ((v: T) => string); error: string }) => {
    const id = push({ kind: 'loading', message: m.loading })
    return p.then((v) => { update(id, { kind: 'success', message: typeof m.success === 'function' ? m.success(v) : m.success }); return v })
            .catch((e) => { update(id, { kind: 'error', message: (e as Error)?.message || m.error }); throw e })
  }, [push, update])

  useEffect(() => () => { timers.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t)) }, [])

  return (
    <ToastCtx.Provider value={{ push, update, dismiss, success, error, info, promise }}>
      {children}
      {/* Адаптив: на мобиле — над нижним меню (AppLayout pb-24), на десктопе — угол. z выше шапки/модалок. */}
      <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-3 bottom-24 z-[80] flex flex-col items-stretch gap-2 sm:inset-x-auto sm:right-4 sm:bottom-4 sm:w-[360px] lg:bottom-6">
        <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
          {items.map((t) => {
            const tone = TONE[t.kind]
            return (
              <motion.div
                key={t.id}
                layout
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 24, scale: 0.96 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, scale: 1 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 24, scale: 0.96 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.32, ease: EASE }}
                className={cn('pointer-events-auto clip-hud flex items-start gap-3 border bg-surface px-4 py-3 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(0,0,0,0.45)]', tone.border)}
              >
                <span className={cn('mt-0.5 shrink-0', tone.text)}>{tone.icon}</span>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  {t.title && (
                    <div className={cn('font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest', tone.text, t.kind === 'success' && 'glitch')}>{t.title}</div>
                  )}
                  <div className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-ink">{t.message}</div>
                </div>
                {t.kind !== 'loading' && (
                  <button onClick={() => dismiss(t.id)} aria-label="Закрыть" className="shrink-0 text-ink-dim transition-colors hover:text-ink">
                    <X size={14} />
                  </button>
                )}
              </motion.div>
            )
          })}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </ToastCtx.Provider>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/shared/ui/ToggleSwitch/index.ts =====

===== FILE: src/shared/ui/ToggleSwitch/ToggleSwitch.tsx =====
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

interface Props {
  checked: boolean
  onChange: () => void
  label?: string
}

export function ToggleSwitch({ checked, onChange, label }: Props) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      onClick={onChange}
      className={cn(
        'relative h-6 w-11 rounded-full border transition-colors duration-200',
        checked ? 'border-lime bg-lime/20' : 'border-line-strong bg-surface-2',
      )}
    >
      <span
        className={cn(
          'absolute top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full transition-all duration-200',
          checked ? 'left-[calc(100%-1.25rem)] bg-lime' : 'left-1 bg-ink-muted',
        )}
      />
    </button>
  )
}

@import 'tailwindcss';

/* ============================================================
   PROTOCOL — единая тема NEON GRID (киберпанк)
   Индиго-чёрный фон, неон аква + кислотный лайм, HUD-разметка,
   катакана-акценты, глитч и голографика. Одна тема, финально.
   ============================================================ */
@theme {
  /* Фон и поверхности — индиго-чёрный */
  --color-bg: #0b0d12;
  --color-surface: #12151e;
  --color-surface-2: #171b26;
  --color-elevated: #1e2331;

  /* Текст */
  --color-ink: #eaf2f0;
  --color-ink-soft: #b3bccb;
  --color-ink-muted: #7a8699;
  --color-ink-dim: #4c5568;

  /* Неон-аква — основной акцент (линии, метки, ссылки) */
  --color-aqua: #9fe8ff;
  --color-aqua-bright: #c4f2ff;
  --color-aqua-dim: #4d7f92;

  /* Кислотный лайм — акцент действий (кнопки, XP) */
  --color-lime: #beef8d;
  --color-lime-bright: #d2f7a8;
  --color-lime-dim: #7fa85a;

  /* Глубокий тил — приглушённый акцент */
  --color-teal: #3a745d;

  /* Границы */
  --color-line: #232838;
  --color-line-strong: #333a4d;
  --color-line-lime: #294a34;
  --color-line-aqua: #1e3a44;

  /* Семантические */
  --color-success: #beef8d;
  --color-warning: #e8c14a;
  --color-danger: #ff5d6c;

  /* Типографика */
  --font-sans: 'Inter Variable', 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
  --font-display: 'Unbounded Variable', 'Unbounded', system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', ui-monospace, 'SFMono-Regular', monospace;
  --font-jp: 'Yu Gothic', 'Hiragino Kaku Gothic Pro', 'Noto Sans JP', 'MS Gothic', sans-serif;

  /* Радиусы (умеренные, углы часто срезаем clip-path в компонентах) */
  --radius-sm: 6px;
  --radius-md: 10px;
  --radius-lg: 14px;

  /* Тени — неон-свечение */
  --shadow-soft: 0 2px 14px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  --shadow-aqua: 0 0 26px rgba(159, 232, 255, 0.22);
  --shadow-lime: 0 0 26px rgba(190, 239, 141, 0.22);
}

@layer base {
  html {
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
  }
  html, body {
    overflow-x: hidden;
  }
  body {
    margin: 0;
    min-height: 100vh;
    min-height: 100dvh;
    background-color: var(--color-bg);
    color: var(--color-ink);
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 16px;
    line-height: 1.55;
  }
  /* SVG никогда не шире контейнера — защита от горизонтального переполнения */
  svg { max-width: 100%; }
  h1, h2, h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    color: var(--color-ink);
    line-height: 1.0;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  }

  /* Моно-метка «код»: // section, ID_NET · 0x2F */
  .label-mono {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: 12px;
    letter-spacing: 0.18em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--color-ink-muted);
  }
  /* Катакана-акцент (декоративный) */
  .jp {
    font-family: var(--font-jp);
    letter-spacing: 0.2em;
  }
  /* Лайм-маркер за словом */
  .mark-lime {
    background-color: var(--color-lime);
    color: var(--color-bg);
    padding: 0 0.12em;
    box-decoration-break: clone;
    -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;
  }
  /* Срез угла HUD-панели (снизу-справа) */
  .clip-hud {
    clip-path: polygon(0 0, 100% 0, 100% calc(100% - 14px), calc(100% - 14px) 100%, 0 100%);
  }
  .clip-btn {
    clip-path: polygon(8px 0, 100% 0, 100% calc(100% - 8px), calc(100% - 8px) 100%, 0 100%, 0 8px);
  }

  /* Глитч заголовка (аккуратный, редкий) */
  @keyframes protocol-glitch {
    0%, 88%, 100% { transform: translate(0); text-shadow: none; }
    89% { transform: translate(-2px, 1px); text-shadow: 2px 0 var(--color-aqua), -2px 0 var(--color-lime); }

    93% { transform: translate(-1px, 0); text-shadow: 2px 0 var(--color-lime), -2px 0 var(--color-aqua); }
    95% { transform: translate(1px, 1px); text-shadow: -1px 0 var(--color-aqua), 1px 0 var(--color-lime); }
    97% { transform: translate(0); text-shadow: none; }
  }

===== FILE: src/widgets/app/AppTopbar.tsx =====
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { LogOut, LayoutGrid, Home } from 'lucide-react'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { LanguageSwitcher } from '@/features/i18n/LanguageSwitcher'
import { XpHud } from './XpHud'
import { NotificationBell } from '@/features/notification/NotificationBell'
import { A11yMenu } from '@/features/a11y/A11yMenu'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { logout as authLogout } from '@/features/auth/authService'
import { FEATURES } from '@/shared/config/features'

export function AppTopbar() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-line bg-bg/85 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="flex h-16 items-center justify-between gap-4 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link to="/app" className="flex items-center gap-2.5" aria-label="PROTOCOL">
            <span className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center bg-lime font-display font-bold text-bg clip-btn">P</span>
            <span className="hidden font-display font-semibold tracking-[0.14em] sm:inline">PROTOCOL</span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link
            to="/about"
            className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-strong px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:border-aqua/50 hover:text-aqua md:flex clip-btn"
            title={t('nav.landing')}
          >
            <Home size={13} /> {t('nav.landing')}
          </Link>
          {FEATURES.portal && (
            <Link
              to="/portal"
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-line-aqua px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-aqua transition-colors hover:bg-aqua/10 clip-btn"
              title={t('nav.portal')}
            >
              <LayoutGrid size={13} /> {t('nav.portal')}
            </Link>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event('protocol:cmdk'))}
            aria-label={t('nav.open_palette')}
            className="hidden items-center gap-2 border border-line-strong px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:border-aqua/50 hover:text-ink sm:flex clip-btn"
          >
            ⌘K
          </button>
          {FEATURES.accessibilityMenu && <A11yMenu />}
          <NotificationBell />
          <LanguageSwitcher />
          <XpHud />
          {user && (
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                authLogout()
                navigate('/login')
              }}
              aria-label={t('nav.logout')}
              className="text-ink-muted transition-colors hover:text-danger"
            >
              <LogOut size={18} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  )
}


===== FILE: src/widgets/app/MobileNav.tsx =====
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { LayoutDashboard, BookOpen, Blocks, User, GraduationCap, Shield, ClipboardList, NotebookPen, FileQuestion, Trophy } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { FEATURES } from '@/shared/config/features'
import { cn } from '@/shared/lib/cn'

interface Item {
  to: string
  key: string
  icon: typeof BookOpen
  roles?: Array<'student' | 'teacher' | 'admin' | 'superadmin'>
  end?: boolean
}

const ITEMS: Item[] = [
  { to: '/app', key: 'nav.dashboard', icon: LayoutDashboard, end: true },
  { to: '/app/courses', key: 'nav.courses', icon: BookOpen },
  { to: '/app/playground', key: 'nav.playground', icon: Blocks },
  { to: '/app/board', key: 'nav.board', icon: ClipboardList },
  { to: '/app/quizzes', key: 'nav.quizzes', icon: FileQuestion },
  { to: '/app/leaderboard', key: 'nav.leaderboard', icon: Trophy },
  { to: '/app/profile', key: 'nav.profile', icon: User },
  { to: '/app/teach', key: 'nav.teach_short', icon: GraduationCap, roles: ['teacher', 'admin', 'superadmin'] },
  { to: '/app/journal', key: 'nav.journal', icon: NotebookPen, roles: ['teacher', 'admin', 'superadmin'] },
  { to: '/app/admin', key: 'nav.admin', icon: Shield, roles: ['admin', 'superadmin'] },
]

export function MobileNav() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const role = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.role ?? 'student')
  const items = ITEMS.filter(
    (i) =>
      (!i.roles || i.roles.includes(role)) &&
      (i.key !== 'nav.playground' || FEATURES.trainers) &&
      (i.key !== 'nav.board' || FEATURES.gamification),
  )

  return (
    <nav
      className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 flex border-t border-line bg-bg/95 backdrop-blur-md lg:hidden"
      style={{ paddingBottom: 'env(safe-area-inset-bottom)' }}
      aria-label={t('nav.label')}
    >
      {items.map(({ to, key, icon: Icon, end }) => (
        <NavLink
          key={to}
          to={to}
          end={end}
          className={({ isActive }) =>
            cn(
              'flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1 py-2.5 text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wide transition-colors',
              isActive ? 'text-aqua' : 'text-ink-muted',
            )
          }
        >
          <Icon size={20} strokeWidth={1.75} aria-hidden />
          {t(key)}
        </NavLink>
      ))}
    </nav>
  )
}

import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'

  key: string

  end?: boolean

  { to: '/app', key: 'nav.dashboard', icon: LayoutDashboard, end: true },
  { to: '/app/courses', key: 'nav.courses', icon: BookOpen },

===== FILE: src/widgets/app/XpHud.tsx =====
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
import { RoleAvatar } from '@/features/profile/RoleAvatar'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/features/auth/authStore'
import { useXp } from '@/features/progress/useXp'
import { useProfileRank } from '@/features/progress/useRank'

export function XpHud() {
  const { t } = useTranslation()
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user)
  const xp = useXp()
  const rankInfo = useProfileRank()
  if (!user) return null
  const isStudent = user.role === 'student'
  const isTeacher = user.role === 'teacher'
  const pct = xp.forNext > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((xp.inLevel / xp.forNext) * 100))) : 0

  return (
    <Link
      to="/app/profile"
      className="flex items-center gap-3 border border-line-strong bg-surface-2 px-2.5 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:border-aqua/50 clip-btn"
      aria-label="Профиль и опыт"
    >
      <RoleAvatar seed={user.id} rank={rankInfo.rank} role={user.role} size={30} />
      <div className="hidden sm:block">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px]">
          {isStudent && <span className="text-lime">LVL {xp.level}</span>}
          {isStudent && <span className="text-ink-dim">·</span>}
          <span className="text-aqua">RANK {rankInfo.rank}</span>
        </div>
        {isStudent ? (
          <div className="mt-1 h-1 w-24 overflow-hidden bg-surface">
            <div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-aqua to-lime transition-all duration-500" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-ink-dim">
            {isTeacher ? t('profile.students_advanced', { count: rankInfo.teacherStudents }) : t('auth.role_' + user.role)}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Link>
  )
}

===== FILE: vite.config.ts =====
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      '@': fileURLToPath(new URL('./src', import.meta.url)),
    },
  },
  server: {
    // Жёсткий IPv4-бинд: Vite слушает 127.0.0.1 напрямую, без резолва имени
    // localhost. Убирает лотерею IPv4/IPv6 в Windows + Node 24, из-за которой
    // браузер ловил ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED то на localhost, то на 127.0.0.1.
    // Открывать всегда: http://127.0.0.1:5173/  (не localhost).
    host: '127.0.0.1',
    port: 5173,
  },
})

===== FILE: ЗАПУСК.md =====
# PROTOCOL LMS — как запустить

Проект лежит в `E:\protocol-lms`. Ниже — пошаговый запуск на Windows.

## 1. Установить Node.js

Нужен **Node.js 20+** (LTS). Проверьте в терминале:

```bash
node -v
npm -v
```

Если нет — скачайте LTS с https://nodejs.org и установите.

## 2. Открыть папку проекта

В терминале (PowerShell или встроенный терминал VS Code):

```powershell
cd E:\protocol-lms
```

## 3. Установить зависимости

```powershell
npm install
```

Скачает React, Vite, Framer Motion, шрифты и всё остальное в папку `node_modules`.
Делается один раз (и после изменений в `package.json`).

## 4. Настроить адрес PocketBase

Скопируйте пример окружения и укажите URL вашего PocketBase:

```powershell
copy .env.example .env
```

Откройте `.env` и впишите адрес (по умолчанию локальный PocketBase):

```
VITE_POCKETBASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8090
```

## 5. Запустить в режиме разработки

```powershell
npm run dev
```

Vite поднимет сайт на **http://localhost:5173** — откройте в браузере.
Изменения в коде применяются мгновенно (hot reload).

## 6. Полезные команды

```powershell
npm run build     # сборка в папку dist (проверка ошибок TypeScript)
npm run preview   # посмотреть собранную версию
npm run lint      # проверка ESLint
npm run format    # автоформат Prettier
```

## Если что-то не работает

- **`node`/`npm` не найдены** — переустановите Node.js и перезапустите терминал.
- **Порт 5173 занят** — Vite сам предложит другой порт, либо закройте прежний процесс.
- **Ошибка про VITE_POCKETBASE_URL** — не создан `.env` (см. шаг 4).
- **Белый экран** — откройте консоль браузера (F12) и пришлите мне текст ошибки.

## Структура проекта

```
src/
├── app/        приложение, роутер, провайдеры
├── pages/      страницы (сейчас: главная, вход)
├── widgets/    Header, Footer
├── features/   переключатель языка (и далее — auth, courses…)
├── entities/   типы данных (user)
├── shared/     api PocketBase, ui-кит, анимации, i18n, утилиты
└── styles/     ло-фай тема (index.css)
```
